<script>
(function(){
  var root=document.documentElement,btn=null,drawer=null,body=null,shade=null,host=null;
  var ICON='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M4 6h10M18 6h2M4 12h4M12 12h8M4 18h12M20 18h0"/><circle cx="16" cy="6" r="2"/><circle cx="10" cy="12" r="2"/><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="2"/></svg>';
  function build(){
    if(btn)return;
    btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='fsopt-btn';btn.innerHTML=ICON+'Options';
    shade=document.createElement('div');shade.className='fsopt-shade';
    drawer=document.createElement('div');drawer.className='fsopt-drawer';drawer.setAttribute('role','dialog');drawer.setAttribute('aria-label','Options');
    drawer.innerHTML='<div class="fsopt-head"><span>Options</span><button type="button">Close</button></div><div class="fsopt-body"></div>';
    body=drawer.querySelector('.fsopt-body');

    shade.addEventListener('click',close);drawer.querySelector('.fsopt-head button').addEventListener('click',close);
    addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape')close();});
    document.body.appendChild(shade);document.body.appendChild(drawer);
  }
  function close(){root.classList.remove('fsopt-open');}
  function park(el){if(!el.__ph){var ph=document.createElement('span');ph.style.display='none';el.parentNode.insertBefore(ph,el);el.__ph=ph;}}
  function unpark(el){if(el.__ph&&el.__ph.parentNode){el.__ph.parentNode.insertBefore(el,el.__ph);el.__ph.parentNode.removeChild(el.__ph);}el.__ph=null;}
  function narrow(){return innerWidth<760||(window.__fsvh||innerHeight)<520;}
  function move(on){
    build();
    var opts=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-opt],[data-opt-narrow]'));
    var huds=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-hud]'));
    if(on){
      opts.forEach(function(el){if(el.hasAttribute('data-opt-narrow')&&!el.hasAttribute('data-opt')&&!narrow())return;park(el);body.appendChild(el);});
      huds.forEach(function(el){var t=document.querySelector(el.getAttribute('data-hud'));if(!t)return;park(el);t.appendChild(el);el.classList.add('hud');});
      host=document.querySelector('[data-optbtn]');
      if(host){btn.classList.remove('floating');host.appendChild(btn);}else{btn.classList.add('floating');document.body.appendChild(btn);}
    } else {
      close();
      huds.forEach(function(el){unpark(el);el.classList.remove('hud');});
      opts.slice().reverse().forEach(unpark);
    }
  }
  var cur=false,lastN=null;
  function set(on,vh){
    if(vh){window.__fsvh=vh;root.style.setProperty('--fsvh',vh+'px');}
    root.classList.toggle('fsshort',!!on&&(window.__fsvh||innerHeight)<520);root.classList.toggle('fsnarrow',!!on&&innerWidth<760);
    var nn=narrow();
    if(!!on!==cur){cur=!!on;root.classList.toggle('fs',cur);move(cur);lastN=nn;}
    else if(cur&&nn!==lastN){move(false);root.classList.add('fs');move(true);lastN=nn;}
    try{window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(d&&d.arcade==='fs')set(d.on,d.vh);});
  /* the shell can hand us full screen up front, so a game never flashes its small layout first */
  if(window.__SUFS){var go=function(){set(true,window.__SUFS.vh);};if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',go);else go();}
})();

   '.suq-q.done{border-color:rgba(52,211,153,.55);background:rgba(16,185,129,.1)}.suq-q.cl{opacity:.55}'+
   '.suq-none{font-size:13px;color:#cbd5e1;padding:8px 2px 10px;line-height:1.45}'+
   '.suq-go{display:block;width:100%;margin-top:8px;padding:11px;border:0;border-radius:12px;font:900 14px Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;color:#1a1206;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#fde68a,#c6a75e)}.suq-go.alt{background:rgba(242,210,126,.12);color:#f2d27e;border:1px solid rgba(242,210,126,.5)}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  var D=null,wrap=null,pop=null;
  var IQ='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M6 3h10l3 3v15H6z"/><path d="M9 9h7M9 13h7M9 17h4"/></svg>',
      IT='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 8a2 2 0 0 0 0 4v5h18v-5a2 2 0 0 0 0-4V3H3z"/><path d="M12 5v2M12 10v2M12 15v1"/></svg>';
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
  function fmt(q){var m=function(v){return '$'+Math.round(v).toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,',');};return q.amt?m(q.n)+' / '+m(q.goal):q.n+' / '+q.goal;}
  function go(tab){close();try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'nav',to:'quests',tab:tab},'*');}catch(e){}}
  function place(){if(!wrap)return;var o=document.querySelector('.fsopt-btn.floating'),r=12;if(o&&o.offsetParent!==null){var b=o.getBoundingClientRect();if(b.width&&b.top<60)r=Math.max(12,innerWidth-b.left+8);}wrap.style.right=r+'px';}
  function render(){
    if(!D||D.self){if(wrap)wrap.remove();wrap=null;return;}
    if(!wrap){wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='suq-pills';document.body.appendChild(wrap);}
    var L=(D.list||[]).filter(function(x){return !x.claimed;}),h='';
    if(L.length){var done=L.filter(function(x){return x.done;}).length;var best=L.filter(function(x){return !x.done;}).sort(function(a,b){return b.n/b.goal-a.n/a.goal;})[0],pct=best?Math.round(100*best.n/best.goal):100;
      h+='<button type="button" class="suq-pill'+(D.tickets>0?' hastk':'')+'" data-a="q" title="Quest tracker">'+IQ+(done&&done===L.length?'<span>All done!</span>':'<span><b>'+L.length+'</b> quest'+(L.length>1?'s':'')+'</span><span class="bar"><i style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></span>')+'</button>';}
    if(D.tickets>0)h+='<button type="button" class="suq-pill tk" data-a="t" title="Your scratch-off tickets">'+IT+'<span><b>'+D.tickets+'</b> to scratch</span></button>';
    wrap.innerHTML=h;[].forEach.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('button'),function(b){b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(b.getAttribute('data-a')==='t')go('t');else open();};});
    place();if(pop)fill();}
  function open(){if(pop)return close();pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='suq-pop';pop.innerHTML='<div class="suq-box"></div>';
    pop.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===pop)close();});document.body.appendChild(pop);fill();}
  function close(){if(pop){pop.remove();pop=null;}}
  function fill(){var bx=pop&&pop.querySelector('.suq-box');if(!bx)return;var L=D.list||[],h='<div class="suq-h"><b>'+(D.room?'Today’s quests':'Quests for this game')+'</b><button type="button" class="suq-x" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div>';
    if(!L.length)h+='<div class="suq-none">None of today’s quests count here. Open the quest list to see where they are.</div>';
    L.forEach(function(q){var pct=Math.min(100,100*q.n/q.goal);h+='<div class="suq-q'+(q.done?' done':'')+(q.claimed?' cl':'')+'">'+esc(q.txt)+'<small>'+(q.t==='e'?'Bronze':q.t==='m'?'Silver':'Gold')+' ticket · '+(q.claimed?'done \u2713 ticket earned':q.done?'done!':fmt(q))+'</small><div class="bar"><i style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></div></div>';});
    if(D.tickets>0)h+='<button type="button" class="suq-go" data-a="t">Scratch my tickets ('+D.tickets+')</button>';
    h+='<button type="button" class="suq-go'+(D.tickets>0?' alt':'')+'" data-a="q">All daily quests</button>';
    bx.innerHTML=h;bx.querySelector('.suq-x').onclick=close;[].forEach.call(bx.querySelectorAll('.suq-go'),function(b){b.onclick=function(){go(b.getAttribute('data-a'));};});}
  var toastT=0;function toast(d){var t=document.querySelector('.suq-toast');if(!t){t=document.createElement('div');t.className='suq-toast';document.body.appendChild(t);t.onclick=function(){t.classList.remove('on');go('t');};}
    var nm=d.tier==='v'?'High-Roller Vault':d.tier==='g'?'Gold':d.tier==='s'?'Silver':'Bronze';t.innerHTML='<i>'+(d.tier==='v'?'\ud83d\udd10':'\ud83c\udf9f\ufe0f')+'</i><span><b>'+(d.tier==='v'?'Vault unlocked!':'Quest complete!')+'</b>'+esc(d.txt)+' \u00b7 '+nm+' ticket earned. Tap to scratch.</span>';
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){t.classList.add('on');});clearTimeout(toastT);toastT=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('on');},4200);}
  addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(d&&d.arcade==='qdone'){if(document.body)toast(d);return;}if(d&&d.arcade==='qcount'){D=d;if(document.body)render();}else if(d&&d.arcade==='fs'){setTimeout(place,80);setTimeout(place,400);}});
  addEventListener('resize',function(){setTimeout(place,60);});
  addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape')close();});
})();

/* art skin control (Western plus every extra skin) in every game's Options: the shell rebuilds the games with the chosen art */
(function(){
  var LIST=/*SKINLIST*/[["anime", "Classic"], ["xmas", "Christmas"], ["beach", "Beach"]]/*SKINLIST*/;
  if(LIST.length<2)return;
  var cur=document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-season')||document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-skin')||'western';
  function btns(){return LIST.map(function(k){return '<button type="button" data-skin="'+k[0]+'"'+(cur===k[0]?' class="on" aria-pressed="true"':' aria-pressed="false"')+'>'+k[1]+'</button>';}).join('');}
  function wire(root){[].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('button[data-skin]'),function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    var v=b.getAttribute('data-skin');[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.su-skinseg button'),function(x){var on=x.getAttribute('data-skin')===v;x.classList.toggle('on',on);x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');});
    if(v!==cur){try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'setSkin',skin:v},'*');}catch(err){}}});});}
  function add(){
    if(document.querySelector('.su-skinseg'))return;
    var mo=document.getElementById('optMotion'),row=mo&&mo.closest('.row');
    if(row){var r=document.createElement('div');r.className='row';r.innerHTML='<span>Art</span><div class="seg su-skinseg" role="group" aria-label="Art style">'+btns()+'</div>';row.parentNode.insertBefore(r,row.nextSibling);wire(r);return;}
    var el=document.createElement('div');el.className='su-skin';
    el.innerHTML='<span>Art style</span><div class="su-skinseg" role="group" aria-label="Art style">'+btns()+'</div>';
    var all=[].filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-opt]'),function(x){return !(x.parentNode&&x.parentNode.closest&&x.parentNode.closest('[data-opt]'));}),last=all[all.length-1];
    if(last&&last.parentNode)last.parentNode.insertBefore(el,last.nextSibling);else (document.querySelector('.wrap')||document.body).appendChild(el);
    wire(el);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',add);else add();
  /* full screen moves the options into the drawer: keep the art switch at the top of it */
  var ph=null;
  addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(!d||d.arcade!=='fs')return;setTimeout(function(){var el=document.querySelector('.su-skin'),bd=document.querySelector('.fsopt-body');if(!el)return;
    if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('fs')&&bd){if(!ph){ph=document.createElement('span');ph.style.display='none';el.parentNode.insertBefore(ph,el);}if(bd.firstChild!==el)bd.insertBefore(el,bd.firstChild);}
    else if(ph&&ph.parentNode){ph.parentNode.insertBefore(el,ph);ph.parentNode.removeChild(ph);ph=null;}},0);});
})();

/* remember each game's option buttons (difficulty, speed, draw mode...) on this device */
(function(){
  var KEY='su_arcade_'+(document.title||'game').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_');
  function read(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'{}')||{};}catch(e){return {};}}
  function write(o){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(o));}catch(e){}}
  function btns(s){return [].slice.call(s.children).filter(function(x){return x.tagName==='BUTTON';});}
  function snap(){if(window.__ownSettings)return;var o=read();o.segs=o.segs||{};
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.seg[id]'),function(s){var i=btns(s).findIndex(function(x){return x.classList.contains('on');});if(i>=0)o.segs[s.id]=i;});write(o);}
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.isTrusted&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.seg[id] button'))setTimeout(snap,0);},true);
  addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(function(){if(window.__ownSettings)return;var o=read();if(!o.segs)return;
    for(var id in o.segs){var s=document.getElementById(id);if(!s)continue;var bt=btns(s)[o.segs[id]];if(bt&&!bt.classList.contains('on'))bt.click();}},0);});
})();
<\u002fscript>
<script>
/* desktop: the mouse wheel never scrolls the page behind the arcade. A wheel turn is only passed on when the pointer
   is over something inside the game that can still scroll that way (a list, the chat, a drawer). */
(function(){
  if(window.__wheelLock)return;window.__wheelLock=1;
  try{if(matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches)return;}catch(e){}
  function canScroll(el,dy,dx){
    for(;el&&el.nodeType===1;el=el.parentNode){
      var cs=getComputedStyle(el),oy=cs.overflowY,ox=cs.overflowX,root=el===document.scrollingElement;
      if(Math.abs(dy)>=Math.abs(dx)){
        if((root||oy==='auto'||oy==='scroll')&&el.scrollHeight>el.clientHeight+1&&(root?getComputedStyle(document.body).overflowY!=='hidden'&&cs.overflowY!=='hidden':true)){
          if(dy<0&&el.scrollTop>0)return true;if(dy>0&&el.scrollTop+el.clientHeight<el.scrollHeight-1)return true;}
      }else if((ox==='auto'||ox==='scroll')&&el.scrollWidth>el.clientWidth+1){
        if(dx<0&&el.scrollLeft>0)return true;if(dx>0&&el.scrollLeft+el.clientWidth<el.scrollWidth-1)return true;}
    }
    return false;}
  addEventListener('wheel',function(e){if(e.ctrlKey)return;if(!canScroll(e.target,e.deltaY,e.deltaX))e.preventDefault();},{passive:false});
})();
<\u002fscript></head>

function isRed(c){return c.s==='h'||c.s==='d';}
var SUIT_ART={"s": "https://assets.cdn.filesafe.space/iB2q5QJOsnXjnbSKdJ0s/media/6ae40040736386d0dc63e41f.webp", "h": "https://assets.cdn.filesafe.space/iB2q5QJOsnXjnbSKdJ0s/media/6ae4004036aa4a07e862e181.webp", "d": "https://assets.cdn.filesafe.space/iB2q5QJOsnXjnbSKdJ0s/media/6ae40040c9d5b7168d177bd6.webp", "c": "https://assets.cdn.filesafe.space/iB2q5QJOsnXjnbSKdJ0s/media/6ae40040c9d5b7168d177bc7.webp"};
function suitSvg(s,cls){return '<svg class="'+(cls||'')+'" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">'+(SUIT_ART[s]?'<image href="'+SUIT_ART[s]+'" x="0" y="0" width="24" height="24"/>':'<path d="'+SUIT_PATH[s]+'" fill="currentColor"/>')+'</svg>';}
function safeUrl(u){return (typeof u==='string'&&/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(u))?u.replace(/["'()\\<>\s]/g,encodeURIComponent):'';}
function artFor(c){
  var k=RANK_TXT[c.r];var e=FACE_ART&&FACE_ART[k];
  if(!e)return '';if(typeof e==='string')return safeUrl(e);return safeUrl(e[c.s]||e.all||'');
}
function cardFrontHTML(c){
  var rk=RANK_TXT[c.r],corner='<span class="rk">'+rk+'</span>'+suitSvg(c.s,'cs');
  var h='<div class="tl">'+corner+'</div><div class="br">'+corner+'</div>';
  var aceArt=c.r===1?artFor(c):'';
  if(aceArt){
    h+='<div class="art"><img src="'+aceArt+'" alt="" draggable="false" loading="lazy" onerror="var c=this.closest(\'.card\');if(c){c.classList.add(\'artfail\');c.classList.remove(\'hasart\');}"></div><div class="big">'+rk+suitSvg(c.s)+'</div>';
  } else if(c.r>10){
    var url=artFor(c);
    if(url)h+='<div class="art"><img src="'+url+'" alt="" draggable="false" loading="lazy" onerror="var c=this.closest(\'.card\');if(c){c.classList.add(\'artfail\');c.classList.remove(\'hasart\');}"></div>';
    else h+='<div class="art fb"><svg class="fi" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">'+FACE_ICON[c.r]+'</svg><b>'+rk+'</b><i>'+FACE_ROLE[c.r]+'</i><em>'+SUIT_TRADE[c.s]+'</em></div>';
    h+='<div class="big">'+rk+suitSvg(c.s)+'</div>';
  } else {
    h+='<div class="pips">'+PIPS[c.r].map(function(p){
      return '<span style="left:'+p[0]+'%;top:'+p[1]+'%"'+(p[2]?' class="f"':'')+'>'+suitSvg(c.s)+'</span>';}).join('')+'</div>';
    h+='<div class="big">'+suitSvg(c.s)+'</div>';
    if(c.r===1)h=h.replace('<div class="pips">','<div class="pips ace">');
  }
  return h;
}
function makeCard(c,down){
  var el=document.createElement('div');
  el.className='card '+(isRed(c)?'red':'blk')+(down?' down':'')+(c.r===1?' isace':'')+(c.r>10?' isface':'')+((c.r>10||c.r===1)&&artFor(c)?' hasart':'');
  el.setAttribute('data-id',c.id);
  var back=safeUrl(FACE_ART&&FACE_ART.back);
  el.innerHTML='<div class="inner"><div class="front">'+cardFrontHTML(c)+'</div><div class="back'+(back?' artback':'')+'"'+(back?' style="background-image:url(\''+back+'\'),linear-gradient(145deg,#10b981,#065f46);background-size:cover;background-position:center"':'')+'>'+(back?'':'<span class="mk"></span>')+'</div></div>';
  el.setAttribute('aria-label',down?'Face-down card':(RANK_TXT[c.r]+' of '+SUIT_NAME[c.s]));
  return el;
}

/* ===== card zoom: right-click or long-press any face-up card ===== */
var Zoom=(function(){
  var ov=null,last=0;
  function close(){if(!ov)return;var o=ov;ov=null;o.classList.remove('on');setTimeout(function(){if(o.parentNode)o.parentNode.removeChild(o);},200);}
  function open(c,y){
    close();last=Date.now();
    ov=document.createElement('div');ov.className='zoomov';ov.setAttribute('role','dialog');ov.setAttribute('aria-label','Card close-up');
    var vis=Math.min(innerHeight,window.__fsvh||(screen&&screen.height)||900),zw=Math.max(220,Math.min(innerWidth*0.86,(vis-90)/1.4,520)),zh=zw*1.4;
    var top=Math.max(12,Math.min((y||innerHeight/2)-zh/2,innerHeight-zh-50));ov.style.setProperty('--zw',zw+'px');ov.style.paddingTop=top+'px';
    var el=makeCard(c,false);el.classList.remove('down');ov.appendChild(el);
    var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='zhint';h.textContent='Tap anywhere to close';ov.appendChild(h);
    ov.addEventListener('click',close);document.body.appendChild(ov);
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){if(ov)ov.classList.add('on');});
  }
  addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape')close();});
  function attach(root,lookup){
    var t=null,sx=0,sy=0;
    function cancel(){if(t){clearTimeout(t);t=null;}}
    root.addEventListener('contextmenu',function(e){
      var el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.card');if(!el||el.classList.contains('down'))return;
      var c=lookup(el);if(!c)return;e.preventDefault();open(c,e.clientY);
    });
    root.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
      if(e.pointerType==='mouse')return;
      var el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.card');if(!el||el.classList.contains('down'))return;
      var c=lookup(el);if(!c)return;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;cancel();
      var py=e.clientY;t=setTimeout(function(){t=null;open(c,py);},520);
    },true);
    root.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){if(t&&Math.hypot(e.clientX-sx,e.clientY-sy)>9)cancel();},true);
    root.addEventListener('pointerup',cancel,true);root.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);
  }
  return {attach:attach,open:open,close:close,recent:function(){return !!ov||Date.now()-last<700;}};
})();

    try{var l=document.createElement('link');l.rel='stylesheet';l.href='https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Rye&display=swap';document.head.appendChild(l);}catch(e){}}

/* keeps painted table art at its true shape: on wide screens the table narrows to the art's aspect instead of stretching */
window.artLock=function(sel,ar){var busy=0;function run(){if(busy)return;busy=1;requestAnimationFrame(function(){busy=0;
  document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(function(el){var cs=getComputedStyle(el);if(!/url\(/.test(cs.backgroundImage)){el.style.maxWidth='';return;}
    var h=el.offsetHeight;if(!h)return;var mw=Math.round(h*ar),cur=parseFloat(el.style.maxWidth)||0;
    if(Math.abs(cur-mw)>3){el.style.maxWidth=mw+'px';el.style.marginLeft='auto';el.style.marginRight='auto';}});});}
  addEventListener('resize',run);addEventListener('message',function(){setTimeout(run,60);});
  if(window.ResizeObserver){try{var ro=new ResizeObserver(run);var t=setInterval(function(){var e=document.querySelectorAll(sel);if(e.length){e.forEach(function(x){ro.observe(x);});clearInterval(t);}},300);}catch(e){}}
  setTimeout(run,100);setInterval(run,1500);};

/* ===== Live tables: real people on the site at the same time share one table =====
   Transport: Supabase Realtime (broadcast + presence). Config is injected at build time
   by replacing the LIVE_CFG marker with {url:'https://xxx.supabase.co',key:'sb_publishable_...'}.
   Tests can force a same-browser transport with window.__LIVE_TRANSPORT='bc' (BroadcastChannel).

   API
     var L=Live.create({game:'holdem',maxSeats:5});
     L.on('roster',fn(list))      list=[{id,name,joined,isHost,isMe}] oldest first (only this table)
     L.on('host',fn(isHost))      fires when this browser becomes / stops being the host
     L.on('msg:<type>',fn(payload,fromId))
     L.on('status',fn(status))    'off' | 'connecting' | 'live' | 'error'
     L.start(); L.stop();
     L.send(type,payload)         broadcast to everyone at the table (not echoed to self)
     L.me -> {id,name}; L.isHost(); L.roster(); L.table (1,2,..); L.status
     L.setName(name)
   Host = the person who has been at the table longest. If they leave, the next oldest takes over.
   When a table already has maxSeats people, newcomers move on to the next table automatically. */
var LIVE_CFG=/*LIVE*/null/*LIVE*/;
var Live=(function(){
  var SUPA_URL='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2.45.4/dist/umd/supabase.js';
  function uid(){try{var a=new Uint32Array(2);crypto.getRandomValues(a);return a[0].toString(36)+a[1].toString(36);}catch(e){return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)+Date.now().toString(36);}}
  function store(k,v){try{if(v===undefined)return localStorage.getItem(k);localStorage.setItem(k,v);}catch(e){return null;}}
  var ME=null,SID=uid();
  function me(){
    if(ME)return ME;
    var id=null;try{id=sessionStorage.getItem('su_live_id');}catch(e){}
    if(!id){id=uid();try{sessionStorage.setItem('su_live_id',id);}catch(e){}}
    try{var ac=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('saddleup-arcade-tok')||'null');if(ac&&ac.t&&ac.u)id='u_'+String(ac.u).toLowerCase();}catch(e){}
    var nm=store('su_live_name');if(!nm){nm='Guest '+(1000+Math.floor(Math.random()*9000));store('su_live_name',nm);}
    ME={id:id,name:String(nm).slice(0,18)};return ME;
  }
  var supaP=null;
  function loadSupa(){
    if(window.supabase&&window.supabase.createClient)return Promise.resolve(window.supabase);
    if(supaP)return supaP;
    supaP=new Promise(function(res,rej){var s=document.createElement('script');s.src=SUPA_URL;s.async=true;
      s.onload=function(){window.supabase&&window.supabase.createClient?res(window.supabase):rej(new Error('supabase missing'));};
      s.onerror=function(){supaP=null;rej(new Error('could not load supabase'));};document.head.appendChild(s);});
    return supaP;
  }
  var client=null;
  var clientP=null;
  function getClient(){
    if(client)return Promise.resolve(client);
    if(clientP)return clientP;
    clientP=loadSupa().then(function(sb){client=sb.createClient(LIVE_CFG.url,LIVE_CFG.key,{auth:{persistSession:false,autoRefreshToken:false},realtime:{params:{eventsPerSecond:30}}});return client;});
    clientP.catch(function(){clientP=null;});return clientP;
  }

  /* ---- same-browser transport for tests: BroadcastChannel with heartbeat presence ---- */
  function bcChannel(name,key){
    var bc=new BroadcastChannel('live:'+name),peers={},mine=null,hb=0,cbs={presence:[],broadcast:{}},alive=true;
    function emitSync(){cbs.presence.forEach(function(f){f();});}
    function prune(){var now=Date.now(),ch=false;for(var k in peers)if(now-peers[k].t>6000){delete peers[k];ch=true;}if(ch)emitSync();}
    bc.onmessage=function(e){var d=e.data;if(!alive||!d)return;
      if(d.k==='hb'){var nw=!peers[d.key];peers[d.key]={t:Date.now(),meta:d.meta};if(nw||d.meta.u!==((peers[d.key]||{}).u))emitSync();if(nw&&mine&&alive)try{bc.postMessage({k:'hb',key:key,meta:mine});}catch(_){}}
      else if(d.k==='bye'){delete peers[d.key];emitSync();}
      else if(d.k==='b'){(cbs.broadcast[d.event]||[]).forEach(function(f){f({payload:d.payload});});}};
    return {
      on:function(kind,filter,fn){if(kind==='presence')cbs.presence.push(fn);else{(cbs.broadcast[filter.event]=cbs.broadcast[filter.event]||[]).push(fn);}return this;},
      subscribe:function(cb){var pt=setInterval(function(){if(!alive){clearInterval(pt);return;}prune();},2500);setTimeout(function(){cb('SUBSCRIBED');},10);return this;},
      track:function(meta){mine=meta;peers[key]={t:Date.now(),meta:meta};bc.postMessage({k:'hb',key:key,meta:meta});
        clearInterval(hb);hb=setInterval(function(){if(!alive)return;peers[key].t=Date.now();bc.postMessage({k:'hb',key:key,meta:mine});prune();},2000);emitSync();return Promise.resolve();},
      presenceState:function(){var o={};for(var k in peers)o[k]=[peers[k].meta];return o;},
      send:function(m){bc.postMessage({k:'b',event:m.event,payload:m.payload});return Promise.resolve('ok');},
      unsubscribe:function(){alive=false;clearInterval(hb);try{bc.postMessage({k:'bye',key:key});bc.close();}catch(e){}return Promise.resolve();}
    };
  }

  function create(opts){
    var game=opts.game,maxSeats=opts.maxSeats||5,handlers={},ch=null,table=1,status='off',hostFlag=false,list=[],joined=0,stopped=true,moving=false,retryT=0,tries=0,hiddenPaused=false;
    var self={me:me(),table:1,status:'off'};
    function emit(k){var a=[].slice.call(arguments,1);(handlers[k]||[]).forEach(function(f){try{f.apply(null,a);}catch(e){console.error(e);}});}
    function setStatus(s){status=s;self.status=s;emit('status',s);
      if(s==='live')tries=0;
      if(s==='error'&&!stopped&&!moving){clearTimeout(retryT);var wait=[8000,15000,30000][Math.min(tries,2)];tries++;
        retryT=setTimeout(function(){if(stopped||status==='live')return;leave().then(function(){if(!stopped)join(table);});},wait);}}
    var heard={},hbT=0,syncT=0,STALE=12000;
    function readRoster(){
      var st=ch?ch.presenceState():{},out=[],seen={},now=Date.now();
      for(var k in st){(st[k]||[]).forEach(function(m){if(!m||!m.id||seen[m.id])return;seen[m.id]=1;
        if(m.id!==self.me.id&&now-(heard[m.id]||joined)>STALE)return;
        out.push({id:m.id,name:String(m.name||'Guest').slice(0,18),joined:+m.joined||0});});}
      out.sort(function(a,b){return a.joined-b.joined||(a.id<b.id?-1:1);});
      return out;
    }
    function dupeOf(){var st=ch?ch.presenceState():{},hit=null;
      for(var k in st){(st[k]||[]).forEach(function(m){if(!m||m.id!==self.me.id||!m.sid||m.sid===SID)return;
        var j=+m.joined||0;if(j<joined||(j===joined&&m.sid<SID))hit=m;});}return hit;}
    function sync(){
      if(!ch)return;
      if(dupeOf()){stopped=true;hiddenPaused=false;clearTimeout(retryT);var wasH=hostFlag;hostFlag=false;leave();setStatus('off');if(wasH)emit('host',false);emit('roster',[]);emit('dupe');dupeNote();return;}
      var r=readRoster();
      /* table full: the newest people beyond the seat limit move on to the next table */
      var myIdx=-1;r.forEach(function(p,i){if(p.id===self.me.id)myIdx=i;});
      if(myIdx>=maxSeats&&!moving){moving=true;var t=table+1;leave().then(function(){moving=false;join(t);});return;}
      r=r.slice(0,maxSeats);
      var host=r.length?r[0].id:null,was=hostFlag;hostFlag=host===self.me.id&&myIdx>=0;
      list=r.map(function(p){return {id:p.id,name:p.name,joined:p.joined,isHost:p.id===host,isMe:p.id===self.me.id};});
      emit('roster',list.slice());
      if(was!==hostFlag)emit('host',hostFlag);
    }
    function dupeNote(){try{var n=document.getElementById('su-dupe');if(!n){n=document.createElement('div');n.id='su-dupe';
        n.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:50%;top:14px;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:99999;max-width:min(92vw,420px);background:rgba(10,15,28,.97);border:1px solid rgba(242,210,126,.6);border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;color:#e2e8f0;font:600 13px/1.4 Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.55);text-align:center';
        document.body.appendChild(n);}
      n.innerHTML='<b style="color:#f2d27e">You&rsquo;re already at this table</b><br>Your account is sitting here on another device, so this one is playing alone.';
      n.style.display='block';clearTimeout(n.__t);n.__t=setTimeout(function(){n.style.display='none';},7000);}catch(e){}}
    function leave(){clearInterval(hbT);clearInterval(syncT);heard={};var c=ch;ch=null;list=[];if(c){try{return Promise.resolve(c.unsubscribe());}catch(e){}}return Promise.resolve();}
    function join(t){
      table=t;self.table=t;setStatus('connecting');joined=Date.now();
      var name='arcade-'+game+'-t'+t,useBC=window.__LIVE_TRANSPORT==='bc';
      var make=useBC?Promise.resolve(bcChannel(name,self.me.id+':'+SID)):getClient().then(function(c){return c.channel(name,{config:{presence:{key:self.me.id+':'+SID,enabled:true},broadcast:{self:false,ack:false}}});});
      return make.then(function(c){
        if(stopped){try{c.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}return;}
        ch=c;
        c.on('presence',{event:'sync'},sync);
        c.on('broadcast',{event:'m'},function(e){var d=e&&e.payload;if(!d||d.from===self.me.id)return;var was=heard[d.from];heard[d.from]=Date.now();
          if(!was||Date.now()-was>STALE)setTimeout(sync,0);if(d.type==='hb')return;if(d.to&&d.to!==self.me.id)return;emit('msg:'+d.type,d.payload,d.from);});
        c.on('presence',{event:'join'},function(e){(e&&e.newPresences||[]).forEach(function(m){if(m&&m.id)heard[m.id]=Date.now();});});
        c.subscribe(function(st){
          if(st==='SUBSCRIBED'){c.track({id:self.me.id,sid:SID,name:self.me.name,joined:joined,u:Date.now()}).then(function(){setStatus('live');sync();});
            clearInterval(hbT);clearInterval(syncT);self.send('hb',0);hbT=setInterval(function(){if(ch)self.send('hb',0);},4000);syncT=setInterval(function(){if(ch)sync();},3000);}
          else if(st==='CHANNEL_ERROR'||st==='TIMED_OUT'){setStatus('error');}
          else if(st==='CLOSED'&&!stopped&&!moving){setStatus('off');}
        });
      }).catch(function(e){console.warn('live:',e&&e.message);setStatus('error');});
    }
    /* switching to another game in the arcade leaves this table; coming back takes a seat again */
    addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(!d||d.arcade!=='visible')return;
      if(!d.on){if(!stopped){hiddenPaused=true;stopped=true;clearTimeout(retryT);var was=hostFlag;hostFlag=false;leave();setStatus('off');if(was)emit('host',false);emit('roster',[]);}}
      else if(hiddenPaused&&!document.hidden){hiddenPaused=false;stopped=false;join(1);}});
    var docAway=false;
    function docVis(){var h=document.hidden;
      if(h&&!stopped){docAway=true;hiddenPaused=true;stopped=true;clearTimeout(retryT);var was=hostFlag;hostFlag=false;leave();setStatus('off');if(was)emit('host',false);emit('roster',[]);}
      else if(!h&&docAway){docAway=false;if(hiddenPaused&&!arcAway){hiddenPaused=false;stopped=false;join(1);}}}
    var arcAway=false;addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(d&&d.arcade==='visible')arcAway=!d.on;});
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',docVis);addEventListener('pagehide',function(){if(!stopped){try{leave();}catch(e){}}});
    self.on=function(k,f){(handlers[k]=handlers[k]||[]).push(f);return self;};
    self.start=function(){if(!stopped)return self;if(!LIVE_CFG&&window.__LIVE_TRANSPORT!=='bc'){setStatus('off');return self;}stopped=false;join(1);return self;};
    self.stop=function(){stopped=true;hiddenPaused=false;clearTimeout(retryT);var was=hostFlag;hostFlag=false;leave();setStatus('off');if(was)emit('host',false);emit('roster',[]);return self;};
    self.send=function(type,payload,to){if(!ch)return false;try{ch.send({type:'broadcast',event:'m',payload:{type:type,from:self.me.id,to:to||null,payload:payload}});}catch(e){return false;}return true;};
    self.isHost=function(){return hostFlag;};
    self.roster=function(){return list.slice();};
    self.available=function(){return !!LIVE_CFG||window.__LIVE_TRANSPORT==='bc';};
    self.setName=function(n){n=String(n||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().slice(0,18);if(!n)return;self.me.name=n;store('su_live_name',n);
      if(ch)ch.track({id:self.me.id,sid:SID,name:n,joined:joined,u:Date.now()});};
    return self;
  }

    BAD.forEach(function(w){t=t.replace(new RegExp('\\b'+w+'\\w*','gi'),function(m){return m.charAt(0)+new Array(m.length).join('*');});});return t;}

  function chat(L,mount,opt){
    opt=opt||{};
    if(!mount||!L)return null;
    if(!document.getElementById('lchat-css')){var st=document.createElement('style');st.id='lchat-css';st.textContent=CSS;document.head.appendChild(st);}
    var tab=document.createElement('button');tab.type='button';tab.className='lchat-tab';tab.innerHTML='Chat<b></b>';tab.setAttribute('aria-label','Open table chat');
    var pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='lchat-pop';pop.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    var box=document.createElement('div');box.className='lchat';box.setAttribute('role','dialog');box.setAttribute('aria-label','Table chat');
    box.innerHTML='<div class="lh"><div><b>Table chat</b> <span></span></div><div style="display:flex;gap:6px"><button type="button" class="lv">Leave table</button><button type="button" class="cl">Close</button></div></div><div class="ll" aria-live="polite"></div>'+
      '<form><input type="text" maxlength="200" placeholder="Say something to the table" aria-label="Chat message" autocomplete="off"><button type="submit">Send</button></form>';
    mount.appendChild(tab);mount.appendChild(pop);mount.appendChild(box);
    var lv=box.querySelector('.lh .lv'),list=box.querySelector('.ll'),input=box.querySelector('input'),sub=box.querySelector('.lh span'),msgs=[],seen={},unread=0,open=false,lastSend=0,popT=0,prevRoster={},shown=false;
    function isLive(){return L.status==='live'&&L.roster().length>=2;}
    function show(){var live=isLive(),vis=live||!!(opt.local&&opt.local());lv.style.display=live?'':'none';if(!live)sub.textContent=opt.local&&opt.local()?(opt.localLabel||'Computer players'):'';else sub.textContent='Table '+L.table;if(vis!==shown){shown=vis;tab.style.display=vis?'':'none';if(!vis){setOpen(false);pop.classList.remove('on');}}}
    function badge(){tab.querySelector('b').textContent=unread?String(Math.min(unread,99)):'';}
    function setOpen(v){open=v;box.classList.toggle('on',v);if(v){unread=0;badge();pop.classList.remove('on');list.scrollTop=list.scrollHeight;setTimeout(function(){try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(e){}},60);}}
    function line(m){
      var d=document.createElement('div');
      if(m.sys){d.className='m sys';d.textContent=m.text;}
      else{d.className='m'+(m.from===L.me.id?' me':'');var n=document.createElement('i');n.textContent=m.name+':';n.style.color=(m.bot&&/^#[0-9a-f]{3,6}$/i.test(m.col||''))?m.col:'hsl('+hue(m.from)+',75%,72%)';
        var t=document.createElement('span');t.textContent=m.text;var tm=document.createElement('small');var dt=new Date(m.ts||Date.now());
        tm.textContent=dt.getHours()%12===0?'12':dt.getHours()%12;tm.textContent+=':'+('0'+dt.getMinutes()).slice(-2);d.appendChild(n);d.appendChild(t);d.appendChild(tm);}
      var atEnd=list.scrollHeight-list.scrollTop-list.clientHeight<40;list.appendChild(d);
      while(list.children.length>80)list.removeChild(list.firstChild);
      if(atEnd||m.from===L.me.id)list.scrollTop=list.scrollHeight;
    }
    function add(m,quiet){
      if(!m||seen[m.id])return;seen[m.id]=1;m.text=m.sys?m.text:clean(m.text);if(!m.text)return;
      m.name=String(m.name||'Guest').slice(0,18);msgs.push(m);if(msgs.length>60)msgs.shift();line(m);
      if(!open&&!quiet&&!m.sys&&m.from!==L.me.id){unread++;badge();
        pop.innerHTML='';var n=document.createElement('i');n.textContent=m.name+':';pop.appendChild(n);pop.appendChild(document.createTextNode(m.text.length>90?m.text.slice(0,88)+'\u2026':m.text));
        pop.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(popT);popT=setTimeout(function(){pop.classList.remove('on');},4500);}
    }
    function sys(t){add({id:'s'+Date.now()+Math.random(),sys:1,text:t,ts:Date.now()},true);}
    tab.addEventListener('click',function(){setOpen(!open);});
    box.querySelector('.lh .cl').addEventListener('click',function(){setOpen(false);});
    box.querySelector('.lh .lv').addEventListener('click',function(){setOpen(false);var off=document.querySelector('#optLive button[data-v="0"]');if(off)off.click();else L.stop();});
    /* keep typing out of the game's keyboard shortcuts and taps out of the table */
    ['keydown','keyup','keypress'].forEach(function(ev){box.addEventListener(ev,function(e){e.stopPropagation();if(e.key==='Escape'&&ev==='keydown')setOpen(false);});});
    ['pointerdown','pointerup','click','contextmenu'].forEach(function(ev){box.addEventListener(ev,function(e){e.stopPropagation();});tab.addEventListener(ev,function(e){e.stopPropagation();});});
    box.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit',function(e){e.preventDefault();
      var t=clean(input.value);if(!t)return;var now=Date.now();if(now-lastSend<600)return;lastSend=now;
      var m={id:L.me.id+'-'+now,from:L.me.id,name:(opt.myName&&!isLive()?opt.myName():L.me.name),text:t,ts:now};add(m);if(isLive())L.send('chat',m);input.value='';if(opt.onMessage)try{opt.onMessage(m,true);}catch(_){}});
    L.on('msg:chat',function(m,from){if(m&&(m.from===from||m.bot)){var had=seen[m.id];add(m);if(!had&&!m.bot&&opt.onMessage)try{opt.onMessage(m,false);}catch(_){}}});
    L.on('msg:chatlog',function(arr){if(Array.isArray(arr))arr.forEach(function(m){if(m&&!m.sys)add(m,true);});});
    L.on('status',function(){show();});
    L.on('roster',function(r){
      var now={};r.forEach(function(p){now[p.id]=p.name;});
      if(shown&&Object.keys(prevRoster).length>1){for(var id in now)if(!prevRoster[id]&&id!==L.me.id)sys(String(now[id]).slice(0,18)+' sat down');
        for(var id2 in prevRoster)if(!now[id2])sys(String(prevRoster[id2]).slice(0,18)+' left the table');}
      /* the host catches newcomers up on the last few messages */
      if(L.isHost()){var recent=msgs.filter(function(m){return !m.sys;}).slice(-20);if(recent.length)for(var id3 in now)if(!prevRoster[id3]&&id3!==L.me.id)L.send('chatlog',recent,id3);}
      prevRoster=now;show();});
    tab.style.display='none';show();
    /* a computer player says something: show it here and pass it on to anyone else at a live table */
    function post(from,name,text,col){var now=Date.now(),m={id:from+'-'+now+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*1e4),from:from,name:name,text:String(text||'').slice(0,200),ts:now,bot:1,col:col||''};add(m);if(isLive()&&L.isHost())L.send('chat',m);return m;}
    return {post:post,sys:sys,refresh:show,open:function(){setOpen(true);},close:function(){setOpen(false);},messages:function(){return msgs.slice();},send:function(t){input.value=t;box.querySelector('form').dispatchEvent(new Event('submit',{cancelable:true}));}};
  }
  
  /* ===== shared casino wallet: one bankroll that follows you between the casino games =====
     Each game attaches with get/set/idle/active callbacks. Changes in any game are passed on as deltas,
     so a hand still settling in a game you just left still pays into the same wallet. */
  var WKEY='su_casino_wallet';
  function wread(){try{var v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(WKEY)||'null');return v&&typeof v.amt==='number'&&isFinite(v.amt)?v.amt:null;}catch(e){return null;}}
  function wwrite(a){try{localStorage.setItem(WKEY,JSON.stringify({amt:Math.round(a*100)/100,t:Date.now()}));}catch(e){}}
  function wallet(o){
    var last=null,inactive=false,timer=0;
    function tick(){
      try{
        if(o.active&&!o.active()){inactive=true;return;}
        var b=o.get();if(typeof b!=='number'||!isFinite(b))return;
        var w=wread();
        if(last===null||inactive){
          if(w===null){wwrite(b);last=b;inactive=false;return;}
          if(o.idle()){if(Math.abs(w-b)>0.004)o.set(w);last=w;inactive=false;}
          return;
        }
        var d=b-last;
        if(Math.abs(d)>0.004){w=(w===null?b-d:w)+d;wwrite(w);last=b;
          /* daily quests: money going onto the table is a bet, money coming back is a payout */
          /* going from broke to a fresh stack is a rebuy, not a payout */
          var rebuy=d>=500&&last<5;
          try{if(parent!==window&&!rebuy)parent.postMessage({arcade:'quest',ev:d<0?'bet':'pay',n:Math.abs(d)},'*');}catch(x){}}
        if(w!==null&&Math.abs(w-b)>0.004&&o.idle()){o.set(w);last=w;}
      }catch(e){}
    }
    timer=setInterval(tick,600);setTimeout(tick,50);
    addEventListener('storage',function(e){if(e.key===WKEY)tick();});
    addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible')setTimeout(tick,30);});
    return {tick:tick,
      /* the game replaced the bankroll on purpose (e.g. a new tournament): take the wallet amount instead, without counting it as a win or loss */
      adopt:function(){var w=wread();if(w===null){last=o.get();wwrite(last);return last;}o.set(w);last=w;return w;},
      peek:wread};
  }
  /* ===== computer players answering the chat =====
     bots: [{id,name,key,col}]  ctx: {game, advice(), mood(key) -> 'up'|'down'|'even', say(bot,text)} */
  function banter(text,bots,ctx){

        bye:['Leaving already? Take a chip for the road.'],rude:['Whoa, easy, high roller here.'],lol:['HA!'],
        idle:['Ha! Love it.','Big talk, I like it.','Waiter! Another round.','Bet bigger, it is more fun.']}
    };
    var ALIAS={dusty:'book',rosa:'hunch',hank:'never',lou:'counter'};
    function topic(){
      if(has(/ (how are|how you|how's it|hows it|how ya|how is everyone|how r u|how are u|wyd) /))return 'how';
      if(has(/ (hi|hello|hey|howdy|sup|yo|hiya|evening|morning|afternoon) /))return 'hi';
      if(has(/ (thanks|thank you|thx|ty|appreciate) /))return 'thanks';
      if(has(/ (gg|good game|nice hand|nh|wp|well played|nice one|good one|nice) /))return 'gg';
      if(has(/ (who are|your name|who is this|who's|whos|what are you) /))return 'who';
      if(has(/ (rigged|cheat|cheating|scam|fixed) /))return 'rigged';
      if(has(/ (luck|lucky|feeling it|hot streak|hot) /))return 'luck';
      if(has(/ (bye|gotta go|leaving|later|gn|good night|cya|see ya) /))return 'bye';
      if(has(/ (stupid|idiot|dumb|suck|sucks|shut up|trash|loser) /))return 'rude';
      if(has(/ (lol|lmao|haha|hah|rofl|lmfao) /))return 'lol';
      if(has(/ (should i|what do i|what should|hit or stand|help me|advice|what would you) /))return 'advice';
      if(has(/ (count|counting) /))return 'count';
      if(has(/ (bluff|bluffing) /))return 'bluff';
      if(has(/ (are you a bot|are you real|you a bot|are you ai|robot|computer) /))return 'bot';
      return 'idle';

      var ro=bots.filter(function(x){return (ALIAS[x.key]||x.key)==='hunch';})[0];if(ro&&lou&&Math.random()<.5)ctx.say(ro,lou.name+' is ALWAYS counting something.',2300);return;}
    if(tp==='bluff'){var bb=named[0]||pick(bots);ctx.say(bb,pick(['Who, me? Never.','Guess you will have to call to find out.','I only bluff on Tuesdays.']),1000);return;}
    if(tp==='bot'){var b2=named[0]||pick(bots);ctx.say(b2,pick(['Just a regular at this table.','Last I checked I was a cowboy.','Ha. I get that a lot.']),1000);return;}
    if(named.length)who=named.slice(0,2);
    else if(everyone||tp==='hi'||tp==='bye')who=bots.filter(function(){return Math.random()<.6;}).slice(0,3);
    else if(tp==='idle'){if(Math.random()<.55)who=[pick(bots)];}
    else who=[pick(bots)];
    if(!who.length&&(tp==='hi'||named.length))who=[pick(bots)];
    who.forEach(function(b,i){var v=V[ALIAS[b.key]||b.key]||V.book,line;
      if(tp==='how'){var md=ctx&&ctx.mood?ctx.mood(b):'even';line=pick(v.how[md]||v.how.even);}
      else line=pick(v[tp]||v.idle);
      ctx.say(b,String(line).replace(/\{n\}/g,b.name),900+i*1300+Math.random()*700);});
  }

  /* ---------- floor watcher: read who is seated at live tables without taking a seat ---------- */
  function watch(names,cb){
    var chans={},stopped=true,useBC=window.__LIVE_TRANSPORT==='bc',wid='w'+uid(),pr=0;
    function read(n){var c=chans[n],st=c?c.presenceState():{},out=[],seen={};
      for(var k in st){(st[k]||[]).forEach(function(m){if(!m||!m.id||seen[m.id])return;seen[m.id]=1;out.push({id:m.id,name:String(m.name||'Guest').slice(0,18),joined:+m.joined||0});});}
      out.sort(function(a,b){return a.joined-b.joined||(a.id<b.id?-1:1);});return out;}
    function emit(){var o={};names.forEach(function(n){o[n]=read(n);});try{cb(o);}catch(e){console.error(e);}}
    function start(){if(!stopped)return;stopped=false;if(!LIVE_CFG&&!useBC)return;
      names.forEach(function(n){
        var mk=useBC?Promise.resolve(bcChannel(n,wid)):getClient().then(function(c){return c.channel(n,{config:{presence:{key:wid,enabled:true},broadcast:{self:false,ack:false}}});});
        mk.then(function(c){if(stopped){try{c.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}return;}chans[n]=c;c.on('presence',{event:'sync'},emit);c.subscribe(function(st){if(st==='SUBSCRIBED')emit();});}).catch(function(e){console.warn('floor:',e&&e.message);});
      });
      clearInterval(pr);pr=setInterval(emit,4000);}
    function stop(){stopped=true;clearInterval(pr);for(var n in chans){try{chans[n].unsubscribe();}catch(e){}}chans={};emit();}
    return {start:start,stop:stop,available:function(){return !!LIVE_CFG||useBC;}};
  }
  return {create:create,me:me,chat:chat,banter:banter,wallet:wallet,walletAmount:wread,watch:watch};
})();

/*ENGINE_START*/
/* ===== Saddle Up Stampede math engine (shared by the game and the Monte Carlo simulator) =====
   5 reels x 4 rows, 1,024 ways, left to right from reel 1. Grid g[c][r]: c = reel 0..4, r = row 0..3 (top to bottom). */
var ENG=(function(){
  var SYMS=['W','S','A','K','Q','J','E','H','R','P','1','2','3','D','X','F','m','n','j','g','T','L'],W=0,S=1;
  var NR=5,NCELL=20; /* reels, and cells on the screen (5 x 4) */
  /* nugget family: 1/2/3 = gold nuggets (count 1, 2, 3), D = dynamite (counts 1, blows open into a big cash nugget), X = strongbox (counts 1, holds cash, wakes bandits),
     F = gold pan (Gold Frenzy trigger), m/n/j/g = Mini/Minor/Major/Grand jackpot nuggets, T = bandit (bonus only), L = locomotive (Gold Frenzy only) */
  var N1=10,N2=11,N3=12,DY=13,BX=14,FP=15,JM=16,BD=20,LO=21;
  /* reel strips: each reel is a fixed strip; a spin shows 4 consecutive positions, cascades keep feeding from above */
  var STRIPS=/*STRIPS*/[
    /* base game: 5 reels (S = Respin Charge on reels 1-4; 1/2/3 nuggets, D dynamite, X strongbox, F Frenzy Horseshoe, m/n/j/g jackpot nuggets) */ "QQFRRRHHHSAA1JJEEEPPPJJQQHHHKKH11RRRJ1QQ1PPPAAERRRSJJFHHHEEEPPP1RRREEEKFPPPKKQQRRRHHHSAA1JJmDnEEEPPPJJQQHHHKK2HRRR1JQQPPPAAEXRRRSJJHHHEEEPPP1RRREEEKPPPKK",
    "WHHHQQRRR1jHHHJJPPP1AAJJPPPSAA2JEKKHHHPPP1SFmH1WKKEEEQQRRRDKRRRQQWEEE1PPPEEERRRJJWHHHQQRRRHHHJJ1PPPAAFJJPPP1SAAJEKKHHHPPPSH1WKKEEEQQRRRKRRRQQWEEEPPPFEEE1RRRJJ",
    "S2PPPWKKEEESEEEJJRRR2PPP1RRRQQJJQQWRRRPPPJJAAEEE1QQHHHPPPEmHRRRKJHHHWAAFKK1HHH1SPPPWKKEEES1EEEJJRRRPPP1RRRQQJJQQWRRRFPPPJJ1FAAEEE1QQHHHPPPEHRRRKn1JHHHWAAKK1HHH",
    "2PPP1JJEHHHJHHHFKKAARRRFmJJ11FPPP1QQEEEPPPEEEKQQRRRWPPP11HHH1RRRWRRRJJEEEKKSAAQQ1HWPPPJJEHHHJHHHKKAARRRJJ1PPPQQEEEPPPEEEKQQRRRWgPPP1HHHRRRWRRRJJEEEKKSAAQQHW",
    "AQQHWPPPJJEHHHJHHHKKAARRRJJPPPQQEEEPPPEEEKQQ1RRRWPPPHHHRRRWRRRJJEEEKKAAQQHWPPPJJEHHHJHHHKK1AARRRJJ1PPPQQEEEPPPEEEKQQRRRWPPPHHHRRRWRRRJJEEE1KKA",
    /* free spins / Stampede (more wilds, no scatters, no nugget family) */ "HKKEEERRRPPPJQQJJAAHHHRRRJJEEEAAKPPPEEEQQKKHHHQQPPPRRRJJERRRHHHPPP",
    "WEEEAAPPPJJWEEEHJJWJQQPPPKKWQQHHHWRRRHHHKAARRRKKEWRRRPPPHHHQQEEERRRJJPPP",
    "WQQKJJWEEEHHHPPPQQKKAAPPPHJJRRRJWRRRAAWQQHHHWPPPEEERRREEEHHHRRRKKPPPJJE",
    "RRRHHHQQEEEJEEEWAARRRPPPQQRRRWJJPPPJJEAAPPPKWQQJJHHHWPPPRRREEEKKHWKKHHH",
    "JEAAPPPKWQQJJHHHWPPPRRREEEKKHWKKHHHRRRHHHQQEEEJEEEWAARRRPPPQQRRRWJJPPPJ"]/*STRIPS*/;
  var CODE='WSAKQJEHRP123DXFmnjgTL';
  /* pays per way, as a multiple of the total bet: [3 of a kind, 4 of a kind, 5 of a kind] */
  var PAY={2: [0.0736, 0.2813, 0.8444], 3: [0.0615, 0.2079, 0.6238], 4: [0.0439, 0.147, 0.4408], 5: [0.037, 0.1104, 0.3311], 6: [0.0194, 0.0542, 0.1621], 7: [0.0194, 0.0542, 0.1621], 8: [0.0146, 0.0393, 0.118], 9: [0.0146, 0.0393, 0.118]};
  var SCAT={},FSAWARD={},FSMAX=20; /* the S scatter (Respin Charge) no longer pays or awards free spins: 3+ start the Nugget Respins */
  var SCATN=3;
  var LADDER=[1,2,3,5,10];
  var JP={names:['mini','minor','major','grand'],label:['Mini','Minor','Major','Grand'],seed:[20,50,250,1000],inc:[.002,.002,.003,.003],w:[700,220,70,10]};
  var MAXWIN=5000;
  /* features */
  var GOLDP=40;            /* Gold Rush: per 1000 paid spins a whole reel (2-5) turns Wild before the first evaluation */
  var LASSOP=60;           /* Lasso: % chance it fires when a high symbol on reels 1 and 4 is one rope away from a win */
  var BOUNTY={need:6,order:[5,4,3,2],x:[10,12,15,18,20,25,30]};  /* bullet holes to collect, wanted order (J,Q,K,A), reward x bet by level */
  var METER={len:135,idx:4,spins:20}; /* Saddle Up meter: every winning cascade moves the rider one step; at the finish line the Stampede starts: 20 free spins, every win x10 */
  var COLL={need:8,p:36,ev:4.5,names:['Sheriff\u2019s Badge','Lantern','Lucky Boot'],games:['Quick Draw','Mine Cart Run','Bronco Ride']}; /* collect tokens: per item p/1000 per paid spin; 8 fill it; minigame worth ev x the average bet */
  /* ===== Nugget family: Nugget Respins (hold & spin) and the Gold Frenzy (four mine claims) =====
     cnt: how many nuggets each symbol counts as (keys are symbol indexes: 10-12 nuggets, 13 dynamite, 14 strongbox, 16-19 Mini..Grand, 20 bandit). pot: (retired Prize Pot, unused).
     val: cash value tables (x bet) [value, weight]. rs / fz: what lands in each empty cell on a respin, per 10,000 (the rest stays empty). */
  var NUG={need:6,cnt:{10:1,11:2,12:3,13:1,14:1,16:1,17:1,18:1,19:1},
    pot:[0,0,0,0,0,0,2,3,4,6,10,15],
    val:{10:[[0.38,30],[0.75,35],[1.5,20],[2.25,10],[3.75,5]],11:[[0.75,30],[1.5,35],[2.25,20],[3.75,10],[7.5,5]],12:[[1.5,30],[2.25,30],[3.75,25],[7.5,10],[15,5]],
      13:[[1.5,40],[2.25,30],[3.75,20],[7.5,10]],14:[[1.5,35],[2.25,30],[3.75,20],[7.5,10],[18.75,5]]},
    rs:[[10,173],[11,86],[12,32],[16,14],[17,6],[18,2],[19,1],[14,22],[20,32]],
    fz:[[10,136],[11,65],[12,24],[16,10],[17,4],[18,1],[19,1],[14,20],[20,29]],loco:[1450,600,280,150],
    seedW:[[10,55],[11,30],[12,15]],
    spins:3,lode:500,claimFull:100,seed:3,drop:3,multMax:3,cap:2500};
  var rng=function(n){return Math.floor(Math.random()*n);};
  var REEL=[],BASE=[],FREE=[],POS=[0,0,0,0,0],FEEDQ=[[],[],[],[],[]];
  function load(st){STRIPS=st;var a=st.map(function(x){return x.split('').map(function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);});});BASE=a.slice(0,NR);FREE=a.length>NR?a.slice(NR,2*NR):BASE;REEL=BASE;}
  load(STRIPS);
  function setRng(f){rng=f;}
  function hasS(col,v){v=v===undefined?S:v;for(var r=0;r<col.length;r++)if(col[r]===v)return true;return false;}
  function one(v){return v===S||v===FP;}
  /* next symbol above the current top of reel c (skips a second scatter on the same reel) */
  function feed(c,col){if(FEEDQ[c].length)return FEEDQ[c].shift();var R=REEL[c],L=R.length;for(var i=0;i<L;i++){POS[c]=(POS[c]-1+L)%L;var v=R[POS[c]];if(one(v)&&hasS(col,v))continue;return v;}return 2;}
  function fresh(){var g=[];for(var c=0;c<NR;c++){var R=REEL[c],L=R.length,p=rng(L),col=[];POS[c]=p;for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var v=R[(p+r)%L];if(one(v)&&hasS(col,v)){v=R[(p+r+4)%L];if(one(v))v=2;}col.push(v);}g.push(col);}return g;}
  function clone(g){return g.map(function(c){return c.slice();});}
  function evalWays(g){
    var wins=[],total=0;
    for(var s=2;s<10;s++){
      var cnt=[],n=0;
      for(var c=0;c<NR;c++){var k=0;for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var v=g[c][r];if(v===s||(v===W&&c>0))k++;}if(!k)break;cnt.push(k);n++;}
      if(n<3)continue;
      var ways=1;for(var i=0;i<n;i++)ways*=cnt[i];
      var pay=PAY[s][n-3]*ways,cells=[];
      for(var c2=0;c2<n;c2++)for(var r2=0;r2<4;r2++){var v2=g[c2][r2];if(v2===s||(v2===W&&c2>0))cells.push([c2,r2]);}
      wins.push({sym:s,n:n,ways:ways,per:PAY[s][n-3],pay:pay,cells:cells});total+=pay;
    }
    return {wins:wins,total:total};
  }
  function wildRow(g){for(var r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[1][r]===W&&g[2][r]===W&&g[3][r]===W)return r;return -1;}
  function scatters(g){var out=[];for(var c=0;c<NR;c++)for(var r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[c][r]===S)out.push([c,r]);return out;}
  function pickTier(){var t=0,i;for(i=0;i<4;i++)t+=JP.w[i];var x=rng(t);for(i=0;i<4;i++){x-=JP.w[i];if(x<0)return i;}return 0;}
  /* cascade: remove cells, let the rest fall, refill from the top. falls[c][r] = row the symbol came from (negative = new) */
  function cascade(g,cells){
    var dead={},falls=[];cells.forEach(function(p){dead[p[0]+','+p[1]]=1;});
    for(var c=0;c<NR;c++){
      var keep=[],from=[];for(var r=3;r>=0;r--)if(!dead[c+','+r]){keep.push(g[c][r]);from.push(r);}
      var col=[],fr=[],k=0;for(r=3;r>=0;r--){if(k<keep.length){col[r]=keep[k];fr[r]=from[k];k++;}else{col[r]=-1;}}
      var nNew=4-keep.length;
      for(r=nNew-1;r>=0;r--){col[r]=feed(c,col.filter(function(x){return x>=0;}));fr[r]=-(nNew-r);}
      g[c]=col;falls.push(fr);
    }
    return falls;
  }
  /* Lasso: a high symbol on reel 1 and an exact match on reel 4, reel 3 already matching (or Wild) and reel 2 the only gap:
     the rope drags the reel-4 symbol into reel 2. Returns the move or null. */
  function lasso(g,force){
    var cands=[];
    for(var s=2;s<=5;s++){
      if(!hasS(g[0],s))continue;var gap=true;for(var r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[1][r]===s||g[1][r]===W)gap=false;if(!gap)continue;
      var ok2=false;for(r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[2][r]===s||g[2][r]===W)ok2=true;if(!ok2)continue;
      var src=[];for(r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[3][r]===s)src.push(r);if(!src.length)continue;
      var dst=[];for(r=0;r<4;r++)if(!one(g[1][r]))dst.push(r);if(!dst.length)continue;
      cands.push({sym:s,src:src,dst:dst});
    }
    if(!cands.length)return null;if(!force&&rng(100)>=LASSOP)return null;
    var c=cands[rng(cands.length)],r3=c.src[rng(c.src.length)],r1=c.dst[rng(c.dst.length)],t=g[1][r1];g[1][r1]=g[3][r3];g[3][r3]=t;
    return {sym:c.sym,from:[3,r3],to:[1,r1],swap:t};
  }
  /* ---------- nugget family ---------- */
  function wd(tab,tot){var t=0,i;for(i=0;i<tab.length;i++)t+=tab[i][1];var x=rng(tot||t);for(i=0;i<tab.length;i++){x-=tab[i][1];if(x<0)return tab[i][0];}return 0;}
  function isJL(k){return k>=JM&&k<JM+4;}
  function cash(k){return {k:k,v:wd(NUG.val[k])};}
  /* what the nuggets on a base-game screen do */
  function nugEval(g){
    var n=0,cells=[],keys=0,pans=[],lab=[0,0,0,0],sc=[];
    for(var c=0;c<NR;c++)for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var v=g[c][r];if(NUG.cnt[v]){n+=NUG.cnt[v];cells.push([c,r]);if(v===DY||v===BX)keys++;if(isJL(v))lab[v-JM]++;}else if(v===FP)pans.push([c,r]);else if(v===S)sc.push([c,r]);}
    var byScat=sc.length>=SCATN,respin=n>=NUG.need||byScat,pot=false,jps=[];
    if(!respin)for(var t=0;t<4;t++)if(lab[t]>=3)jps.push(t);
    return {n:n,cells:cells,keys:keys,pans:pans,scat:sc,byScat:byScat,lab:lab,frenzy:pans.length>=3,respin:respin,pot:pot,potX:pot?NUG.pot[Math.min(n,NUG.pot.length-1)]:0,jps:jps,near:(!respin&&n>=4)?NUG.need-n:0};
  }
  /* Nugget Respins (kind 'respin', one 5x4 claim seeded from the trigger screen) and the Gold Frenzy (kind 'frenzy', four claims,
     claim 1 open with 3 nuggets, claims 2-4 locked until a locomotive lands). Plays the whole bonus and returns a script for the UI.
     o.script: optional list of forced landings per spin, each a list of {cl,c,r,k,v} (k = symbol index, 0 = nothing). */
  function bonusRun(kind,g,o){
    o=o||{};var fz=kind==='frenzy',NC=fz?4:1,cl=[],act=[],full=[],jpm=[0,0,0,0],jpf=[],meter=0,mult=1,steps=[],q=o.script?o.script.slice():null,i,idx;
    for(i=0;i<NC;i++){var row=[];for(var z=0;z<NCELL;z++)row.push(null);cl.push(row);act.push(i===0);full.push(false);}
    function label(t){jpm[t]++;if(jpm[t]>=3&&jpf.indexOf(t)<0){jpf.push(t);return true;}return false;}
    function empties(i){var e=[];for(var x=0;x<NCELL;x++)if(!cl[i][x])e.push(x);return e;}
    var pre=[];
    if(!fz){for(var c=0;c<NR;c++)for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var v=g[c][r],cell=null;
      if(v>=N1&&v<=N3||v===BX)cell=cash(v);else if(v===DY||v===S)cell={k:N3,v:wd(NUG.val[DY]),blast:true,from:v};else if(isJL(v))cell={k:v,t:v-JM};
      if(cell){cl[0][c*4+r]=cell;if(cell.v)meter+=cell.v;if(cell.t!==undefined)label(cell.t);}}}
    else{var e0=empties(0);for(i=0;i<NUG.seed;i++){var p=e0.splice(rng(e0.length),1)[0],sc=cash(wd(NUG.seedW));cl[0][p]=sc;meter+=sc.v;}}
    var start={claims:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cl)),meter:meter,jpm:jpm.slice(),jpf:jpf.slice(),act:act.slice()};
    var left=NUG.spins;
    for(var guard=0;guard<400&&left>0;guard++){
      var sp={lands:[],locos:[],unlocks:[],collects:[],jp:[],full:[],multUp:0},forced=q&&q.length?q.shift():null;
      for(i=0;i<NC;i++){if(!act[i]||full[i])continue;
        for(idx=0;idx<NCELL;idx++){if(cl[i][idx])continue;var k,f=null;
          if(forced){for(var fi=0;fi<forced.length;fi++){var x=forced[fi];if((x.cl||0)===i&&x.c*4+x.r===idx){f=x;break;}}k=f?f.k:0;}
          else k=wd(fz?NUG.fz:NUG.rs,10000);
          if(!k||k===LO)continue;
          var nc=isJL(k)?{k:k,t:k-JM}:k===BD?{k:BD,a:false}:cash(k);if(f&&f.v&&nc.v!==undefined)nc.v=f.v;
          cl[i][idx]=nc;sp.lands.push({cl:i,idx:idx,cell:nc});}}
      /* the locomotive: one roll per Frenzy spin (forced scripts may place one with k = 22) */
      if(fz){var lf=forced?forced.filter(function(x){return x.k===LO;}):null;
        var nOpen=act.filter(Boolean).length;if(lf?lf.length:rng(10000)<NUG.loco[Math.min(nOpen,NUG.loco.length)-1]){(lf&&lf.length?lf:[null]).forEach(function(x){var ok=[];for(var a=0;a<NC;a++)if(act[a]&&!full[a])for(var b2=0;b2<NCELL;b2++)if(!cl[a][b2])ok.push([a,b2]);
          var at=x?[x.cl||0,x.c*4+x.r]:ok.length?ok[rng(ok.length)]:null;if(at)sp.locos.push({cl:at[0],idx:at[1]});});}}
      /* 1 land, 2 lock + meter */
      sp.lands.forEach(function(l){if(l.cell.v)meter+=l.cell.v;if(l.cell.t!==undefined&&label(l.cell.t))sp.jp.push(l.cell.t);});
      /* 3 a strongbox turns every bandit in its claim active, 4 each bandit collects its orthogonal neighbours' cash */
      for(i=0;i<NC;i++){var boxes=sp.lands.filter(function(l){return l.cl===i&&l.cell.k===BX;}).length;
        for(var bx=0;bx<boxes;bx++)for(idx=0;idx<NCELL;idx++){var bd=cl[i][idx];if(!bd||bd.k!==BD)continue;bd.a=true;
          var cc=idx>>2,rr=idx&3,sum=0,from=[];[[cc-1,rr],[cc+1,rr],[cc,rr-1],[cc,rr+1]].forEach(function(p){if(p[0]<0||p[0]>NR-1||p[1]<0||p[1]>3)return;var nb=cl[i][p[0]*4+p[1]];if(nb&&nb.v){sum+=nb.v;from.push(p[0]*4+p[1]);}});
          sum=Math.round(sum*100)/100;bd.got=Math.round(((bd.got||0)+sum)*100)/100;meter+=sum;sp.collects.push({cl:i,idx:idx,amt:sum,from:from});}}
      /* locomotives open the next claim (it arrives with nuggets), or raise the meter multiplier once all four are open */
      sp.locos.forEach(function(){var nx=act.indexOf(false);
        if(nx>=0){act[nx]=true;var drops=[],e=empties(nx);for(var d=0;d<NUG.drop&&e.length;d++){var pp=e.splice(rng(e.length),1)[0],dc=cash(wd(NUG.seedW));cl[nx][pp]=dc;meter+=dc.v;drops.push({idx:pp,cell:dc});}sp.unlocks.push({cl:nx,drops:drops});}
        else if(mult<NUG.multMax){mult++;sp.multUp++;}});
      for(i=0;i<NC;i++)if(act[i]&&!full[i]&&cl[i].every(Boolean)){full[i]=true;sp.full.push(i);}
      left=(sp.lands.length||sp.locos.length)?NUG.spins:left-1;
      meter=Math.round(meter*100)/100;sp.left=left;sp.meter=meter;sp.mult=mult;sp.jpm=jpm.slice();
      steps.push(sp);
      var open=false;for(i=0;i<NC;i++)if(act[i]&&!full[i])open=true;if(!open)break;
    }
    var nFull=full.filter(Boolean).length,fillX=fz?nFull*NUG.claimFull:(nFull?NUG.lode:0);
    return {kind:kind,start:start,steps:steps,meter:meter,mult:mult,full:nFull,fillX:fillX,jp:jpf,cashX:Math.round((meter*mult+fillX)*100)/100,claims:cl,open:act.filter(Boolean).length};
  }
  /* one paid spin (or one free spin). Returns everything the UI needs to animate it. */
  function spin(o){
    o=o||{};REEL=o.fs?FREE:BASE;var fs=!!o.fs,idx=(o.saddle||o.x10)?LADDER.length-1:(fs?(o.idx||0):0),g=o.grid?(fresh(),clone(o.grid)):fresh(),pre=clone(g),gold=-1,lassoed=false,pend=null,steps=[],total=0,jp=null;
    /* Saddle Up spins: a Gold Rush wild reel on every spin */
    if(o.saddle||(!fs&&(o.gold!==undefined?o.gold>0:rng(1000)<GOLDP))){gold=o.gold>0?o.gold:1+rng(NR-1);for(var r0=0;r0<4;r0++)g[gold][r0]=W;}
    var start=clone(g);
    for(var guard=0;guard<60;guard++){
      if(!jp){var wr=wildRow(g);if(wr>=0)jp={row:wr,tier:o.tier!==undefined?o.tier:pickTier(),at:steps.length};}
      var ev=evalWays(g);if(!ev.wins.length){if(!lassoed&&o.lasso!==false){lassoed=true;var la=lasso(g,o.lasso===true);if(la){pend=la;continue;}}break;}
      var m=LADDER[Math.min(idx,LADDER.length-1)],win=ev.total*m,all={},cells=[];
      ev.wins.forEach(function(w){w.cells.forEach(function(p){var k=p[0]+','+p[1];if(!all[k]){all[k]=1;cells.push(p);}});});
      var before=clone(g),falls=cascade(g,cells);
      total+=win;steps.push({wins:ev.wins,base:ev.total,mult:m,idx:idx,win:win,cells:cells,before:before,falls:falls,grid:clone(g),lasso:pend});pend=null;
      idx++;
    }
    var sc=scatters(g),n=sc.length,sp=0,fsw=0;
    var tok=[];if(!fs)for(var ti=0;ti<3;ti++)if(o.tokens?o.tokens.indexOf(ti)>=0:rng(1000)<COLL.p)tok.push(ti);
    return {pre:pre,gold:gold,start:start,steps:steps,lineWin:total,scat:sc,scatPay:sp,fsAward:fsw,jp:jp,idx:idx,final:clone(g),tokens:tok,nug:fs?null:nugEval(g)};
  }
  return {SYMS:SYMS,STRIPS:function(){return STRIPS;},load:load,PAY:PAY,SCAT:SCAT,FSAWARD:FSAWARD,FSMAX:FSMAX,SCATN:SCATN,LADDER:LADDER,JP:JP,MAXWIN:MAXWIN,
    setRng:setRng,queue:function(cols){FEEDQ=cols.map(function(x){return String(x||'').split('').map(function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);}).filter(function(v){return v>=0;});});},CODE:CODE,NUG:NUG,NR:NR,NCELL:NCELL,nugEval:nugEval,bonusRun:bonusRun,N1:N1,DY:DY,BX:BX,FP:FP,JM:JM,BD:BD,LO:LO,GOLDP:GOLDP,LASSOP:LASSOP,BOUNTY:BOUNTY,METER:METER,COLL:COLL,lasso:lasso,fresh:fresh,clone:clone,evalWays:evalWays,wildRow:wildRow,scatters:scatters,cascade:cascade,spin:spin,pickTier:pickTier,W:W,S:S};
})();

var root=document.documentElement,felt=$('felt'),cab=$('cab'),reelsEl=$('reels'),pathsEl=$('paths');
function fmt(n,dec){var s=Math.abs(n),i=Math.floor(s+1e-9),c=Math.round((s-i)*100);if(c===100){i++;c=0;}var t=String(i).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,',');return dec||c?t+'.'+('0'+c).slice(-2):t;}
function money(n,dec){return (n<0?'\u2212$':'$')+fmt(n,dec);}
function smoney(n){return (n>0?'+$':n<0?'\u2212$':'$')+fmt(n);}
function r2(x){return Math.round(x*100)/100;}
function clamp(v,a,b){return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));}
function pick(a){return a[Math.floor(Math.random()*a.length)];}
function esc(t){return String(t).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
/* secure RNG for the reels: crypto.getRandomValues with rejection sampling (no modulo bias) */
var RBUF=new Uint32Array(64),RPOS=64;
function crnd(n){try{var lim=Math.floor(4294967296/n)*n,x;do{if(RPOS>=RBUF.length){crypto.getRandomValues(RBUF);RPOS=0;}x=RBUF[RPOS++];}while(x>=lim);return x%n;}catch(e){return Math.floor(Math.random()*n);}}
ENG.setRng(crnd);

var JTC=['#0e7490','#047857','#6d28d9','#9f1239'],JLK='mnjg';
function symInner(k){
  if(ARTKEY[k]&&A(ARTKEY[k]))return '<div class="tart">'+imgTag(ARTKEY[k])+'</div>';
  if(k==='1'||k==='2'||k==='3')return '<div class="tn">'+(fzImg('nug'+k)||nugSvg(+k))+(k!=='1'?'<i class="ncnt">&times;'+k+'</i>':'')+'</div>';
  if(JLK.indexOf(k)>=0){var jt=JLK.indexOf(k),jl=ENG.JP.label[jt].toUpperCase();return FZART.jpbadge?'<div class="tn tjl tjb" style="--jc:'+JTC[jt]+'">'+fzImg('jpbadge')+'<b class="rib">'+jl+'</b></div>':'<div class="tn tjl" style="--jc:'+JTC[jt]+'">'+nugSvg(1)+'<b>'+jl+'</b></div>';}
  if(k==='D')return '<div class="tdx">'+(fzImg('tnt')||TNTSVG)+'<b>TNT</b></div>';
  if(k==='X')return '<div class="tbx">'+(fzImg('box')||BOXSVG)+'</div>';
  if(k==='F')return '<div class="tpan">'+(fzImg('pan','glow')||PANSVG)+'<b>FRENZY</b></div>';
  if(k==='T')return '<div class="tbd">'+(fzImg('bandit')||BANDITSVG)+'<b>BANDIT</b></div>';
  if(k==='L')return '<div class="tlo">'+(fzImg('loco')||LOCOSVG)+'</div>';
  if(k==='W')return '<div class="tw"><i></i><b>WILD</b></div>';
  if(k==='S')return '<div class="ts rch">'+(fzImg('tnt','lit')||TNTSVG)+'<b>RESPINS</b></div>';
  if(LOWSUIT[k])return '<div class="tj">'+suitSvg(LOWSUIT[k])+'</div>';
  var u=artUrl(k);
  return '<div class="th">'+(u?'<i class="art" style="background-image:url(\''+u+'\')"></i>':'<i class="art fb"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">'+FB_ICON[k]+'</svg></i>')+'<b class="rb">'+k+'</b></div>';
}
function makeSym(s,c,r){var k=SK[s],el=document.createElement('div');el.className='sy s-'+k;el.style.setProperty('--c',c);el.style.setProperty('--r',r);el.innerHTML=symInner(k);el.__s=s;el.setAttribute('aria-label',SYMNAME[k]);return el;}
