['A','K','Q','J'].forEach(function(k){try{var im=new Image();im.src=artUrl(k);}catch(e){}});

/* =====================================================================
   Sound (WebAudio, no files)
   ===================================================================== */
var ac=null,master=null,unlocked=false,AWAY=false;
function unlock(){unlocked=true;try{var A=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(A&&!ac){ac=new A();master=ac.createGain();master.gain.value=0.85;master.connect(ac.destination);}if(ac&&ac.state==='suspended'&&!AWAY)ac.resume();}catch(e){ac=null;}}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',unlock,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',unlock,true);
function canSnd(){return opt.sound&&unlocked&&!!ac&&!AWAY;}
function noise(d,vol,delay,f,type,q){if(!canSnd())return;try{var t=ac.currentTime+(delay||0),len=Math.max(1,Math.floor(ac.sampleRate*d)),buf=ac.createBuffer(1,len,ac.sampleRate),ch=buf.getChannelData(0);
  for(var i=0;i<len;i++)ch[i]=(Math.random()*2-1)*Math.pow(1-i/len,2);var s=ac.createBufferSource(),g=ac.createGain(),fl=ac.createBiquadFilter();fl.type=type||'highpass';fl.frequency.value=f||1800;if(q)fl.Q.value=q;
  s.buffer=buf;g.gain.value=vol||0.15;s.connect(fl);fl.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start(t);}catch(e){}}
function tone(f,d,type,vol,delay,f2){if(!canSnd())return;try{var t=ac.currentTime+(delay||0),o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();
  o.type=type||'triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);if(f2)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f2,t+d);g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol||0.04,t+0.008);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+d);o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+0.03);}catch(e){}}
var whir=null,antic=null;
var SFX={
  click:function(){tone(1700,0.035,'square',0.012);noise(0.02,0.08,0,4000);},
  whirOn:function(){if(!canSnd()||whir)return;try{var len=ac.sampleRate,buf=ac.createBuffer(1,len,ac.sampleRate),ch=buf.getChannelData(0),b=0;for(var i=0;i<len;i++){b=b*0.9+(Math.random()*2-1)*0.3;ch[i]=b*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(i/len*Math.PI*2*28));}
    var s=ac.createBufferSource();s.buffer=buf;s.loop=true;var f=ac.createBiquadFilter();f.type='bandpass';f.frequency.value=520;f.Q.value=0.8;var g=ac.createGain();g.gain.value=0;
    s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start();g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.09,ac.currentTime,0.05);whir={s:s,g:g};}catch(e){whir=null;}},
  whirOff:function(){if(!whir)return;var w=whir;whir=null;try{w.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,ac.currentTime,0.06);setTimeout(function(){try{w.s.stop();}catch(e){}},400);}catch(e){}},
  thunk:function(c){tone(118-c*7,0.17,'sine',0.22,0,46);noise(0.07,0.2,0,420,'lowpass');noise(0.03,0.05,0,3000,'highpass');},
  ding:function(n){var f=[880,1046.5,1318.5,1568][clamp(n-1,0,3)];tone(f,0.7,'sine',0.06);tone(f*1.5,0.55,'sine',0.03,0.05);tone(f*2,0.4,'triangle',0.014,0.1);},
  chime:function(k){var b=523.25*Math.pow(2,clamp(k,0,9)*2/12);[1,1.26,1.5,2].forEach(function(m,i){tone(b*m,0.3,'triangle',0.05,i*0.055);});tone(b*4,0.25,'sine',0.012,0.22);},
  burst:function(){noise(0.28,0.22,0,2200,'highpass');noise(0.14,0.2,0,700,'bandpass',1);tone(1600,0.16,'square',0.008,0,500);},
  whoosh:function(k){noise(0.32,0.1,0,500+k*260,'bandpass',0.9);tone(300+k*90,0.25,'sine',0.03,0,900+k*200);},
  anticOn:function(){if(!canSnd()||antic)return;try{var o=ac.createOscillator(),o2=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain(),t=ac.currentTime;o.type='sawtooth';o2.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(140,t);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(620,t+1.3);
    o2.frequency.setValueAtTime(280,t);o2.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1240,t+1.3);var f=ac.createBiquadFilter();f.type='lowpass';f.frequency.value=1400;g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.035,t+0.3);
    o.connect(f);o2.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start();o2.start();antic={o:o,o2:o2,g:g};}catch(e){antic=null;}},
  anticOff:function(){if(!antic)return;var a=antic;antic=null;try{a.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.0001,ac.currentTime,0.05);setTimeout(function(){try{a.o.stop();a.o2.stop();}catch(e){}},300);}catch(e){}},
  fanfare:function(t){var seq=[[523,659,784,1047],[523,659,784,1047,1319],[392,523,659,784,1047,1319,1568]][clamp(t,0,2)];
    for(var rep=0;rep<(t>=1?2:1);rep++)seq.forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,0.22,'triangle',0.05,rep*0.62+i*0.08);tone(f*2,0.18,'sine',0.015,rep*0.62+i*0.08);});
    tone(seq[seq.length-1],1.2,'sine',0.05,(t>=1?1.24:0.62));},
  coin:function(){tone(2300+Math.random()*900,0.07,'square',0.01);tone(3500+Math.random()*500,0.09,'sine',0.018,0.02);},
  gallop:function(sec){if(!canSnd())return;for(var t=0;t<sec;t+=0.24){noise(0.06,0.32,t,200,'lowpass');noise(0.05,0.22,t+0.07,240,'lowpass');noise(0.05,0.26,t+0.12,180,'lowpass');}},
  bell:function(){for(var i=0;i<4;i++){tone(1318.5,1.1,'sine',0.06,i*0.32);tone(1975.5,0.9,'sine',0.035,i*0.32+0.02);tone(2637,0.6,'sine',0.02,i*0.32+0.04);}},
  scatterWin:function(){[784,988,1175,1568,1976].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,0.35,'triangle',0.045,i*0.07);});},
  nowin:function(){tone(330,0.12,'triangle',0.02);}
};

/* =====================================================================
   Clock: spins, cascades, neighbours and effects run on a virtual clock
   that stops while the game is hidden in the arcade.
   ===================================================================== */
var vt=0,timers=[],tweens=[],lastRT=0,rafOn=false,DBGF=1;
function after(ms,fn){var t={at:vt+ms,fn:fn};timers.push(t);kick();return t;}
function cancelT(t){var i=timers.indexOf(t);if(i>=0)timers.splice(i,1);}
function wait(ms){return new Promise(function(res){after(ms,res);});}
function tween(ms,fn){return new Promise(function(res){tweens.push({t0:vt,ms:Math.max(1,ms),fn:fn,res:res});kick();});}
function runTimers(){for(var g=0;g<200;g++){var due=null;for(var i=0;i<timers.length;i++)if(timers[i].at<=vt&&(!due||timers[i].at<due.at))due=timers[i];if(!due)break;timers.splice(timers.indexOf(due),1);try{due.fn();}catch(e){console.error(e);}}}
function runTweens(){var keep=[];tweens.forEach(function(t){var p=Math.min(1,(vt-t.t0)/t.ms);try{t.fn(p);}catch(e){console.error(e);}if(p>=1)t.res();else keep.push(t);});tweens=keep;}
function loop(rt){rafOn=false;if(AWAY){lastRT=0;return;}var dt=lastRT?Math.min(rt-lastRT,100):16;lastRT=rt;vt+=dt;runTimers();runTweens();fxStep(dt);if(timers.length||tweens.length||parts.length||fxDirty)kick();}
function kick(){if(!rafOn&&!AWAY){rafOn=true;requestAnimationFrame(loop);}}
function anims(fn){try{(document.getAnimations?document.getAnimations():[]).forEach(fn);}catch(e){}}
/* durations: turbo halves them, pressing Spin mid-spin hurries the rest */
function D(ms){return ms*(opt.turbo?0.5:1)*(st.hurry?0.35:1)*DBGF;}
function anim(el,kf,o){try{var a=el.animate(kf,o);if(AWAY)a.pause();return a;}catch(e){return null;}}
function done(a){return a&&a.finished?a.finished.catch(function(){}):Promise.resolve();}

/* =====================================================================
   State
   ===================================================================== */
var BETS=[1,2,5,10,25,50,100,250,500],START=1000,LAD=ENG.LADDER,JP=ENG.JP;
var opt={sound:true,turbo:false,fx:!REDUCED,bots:3,talk:true,live:true,autoN:25,stopFS:false,stopBig:false,loss:25};
var st={bank:START,betI:3,busy:false,spins:0,big:0,sessBase:START,fs:null,auto:0,autoHeld:0,autoBank:0,hurry:false,idx:0,force:null,celeb:null,
  stats:{hits:0,wager:0,won:0,feats:0,fsSpins:0,jps:0,best:0,bestChain:0,lastFS:null}};
function bet(){return BETS[st.betI];}
var JKEY='su_slots_jackpot',pools=JP.seed.slice();
(function(){try{var v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(JKEY)||'null');if(v&&Array.isArray(v.m)&&v.m.length===4&&v.m.every(function(x,i){return typeof x==='number'&&isFinite(x)&&x>=JP.seed[i]&&x<JP.seed[i]*50;}))pools=v.m.slice();}catch(e){}})();
function saveJP(){try{localStorage.setItem(JKEY,JSON.stringify({m:pools.map(function(x){return Math.round(x*10000)/10000;}),t:Date.now()}));}catch(e){}}
(function(){try{var p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('su_slots_prefs')||'null');if(p&&typeof p.betI==='number'&&BETS[p.betI])st.betI=p.betI;}catch(e){}})();
function savePrefs(){try{localStorage.setItem('su_slots_prefs',JSON.stringify({betI:st.betI}));}catch(e){}}

/* =====================================================================
   Build the machine
   ===================================================================== */
var NRE=ENG.NR,tiles=[[],[],[],[],[]],blurs=[],glows=[],ladEls=[];
for(var c0=0;c0<NRE;c0++){var bl=document.createElement('div');bl.className='blur';bl.style.setProperty('--c',c0);bl.innerHTML='<div class="bs"></div>';reelsEl.appendChild(bl);blurs.push(bl);
  var gl=document.createElement('div');gl.className='colglow';gl.style.setProperty('--c',c0);reelsEl.appendChild(gl);glows.push(gl);}
LAD.forEach(function(m,i){var e=document.createElement('i');e.textContent=m+'\u00d7';$('lad').appendChild(e);ladEls.push(e);});
var wlab=document.createElement('div');wlab.className='wlab';wlab.style.opacity='0';reelsEl.appendChild(wlab);
function setLadder(i,pulse){i=Math.min(i,LAD.length-1);ladEls.forEach(function(e,k){e.classList.toggle('on',k===i);e.classList.toggle('done',k<i);e.classList.remove('pulse');});
  if(pulse&&opt.fx){void ladEls[i].offsetWidth;ladEls[i].classList.add('pulse');}}
function placeGrid(g){for(var c=0;c<NRE;c++){tiles[c].forEach(function(t){if(t&&t.parentNode)t.parentNode.removeChild(t);});tiles[c]=[];for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var t=makeSym(g[c][r],c,r);reelsEl.appendChild(t);tiles[c].push(t);}}}
function gridLetters(){return tiles.map(function(col){return col.map(function(t){return t?SK[t.__s]:'?';}).join('');});}
function fillBlur(c,fs){var bs=blurs[c].firstChild,h='';for(var i=0;i<8;i++){var s=2+crnd(8);if(i%5===2&&c>0)s=0;h+='<div class="sy s-'+SK[s]+'">'+symInner(SK[s])+'</div>';}bs.innerHTML=h;}

/* =====================================================================
   Layout: wide cabinet, tall phone, and the short phone-landscape full screen
   ===================================================================== */
var LAY='wide',CS=96,CABFULL=false;
function setCS(cs){cs=Math.max(34,Math.floor(cs));CS=cs;felt.style.setProperty('--cs',cs+'px');felt.style.setProperty('--g',Math.max(3,Math.round(cs*0.07))+'px');try{sizeCab();}catch(e){}try{sizeBoard();}catch(e){}}
function over(){var c=cab.getBoundingClientRect(),bad=false;[].forEach.call(cab.querySelectorAll('.marq,.lad,.rw,.side,.winbar,.ctl,.feed,.side .nb,.mtr,.bty,.coll,.pbar'),function(e){var r=e.getBoundingClientRect();if(r.width&&(r.bottom>c.bottom+1||r.top<c.top-1||r.right>c.right+1||r.left<c.left-1))bad=true;});return bad;}
function layout(){
  var fs=root.classList.contains('fs'),cs0=getComputedStyle(felt);
  var W=felt.clientWidth-parseFloat(cs0.paddingLeft)-parseFloat(cs0.paddingRight),H=felt.clientHeight-parseFloat(cs0.paddingTop)-parseFloat(cs0.paddingBottom);
  var mode=fs?(W>H*1.25&&(H<520||W>H*1.45)?'short':(W>=760&&W>H*1.02?'wide':'narrow')):(W>=720?'wide':'narrow');
  var big=mode==='short'&&H>=520;
  if(mode!==LAY){felt.classList.remove('lay-'+LAY);LAY=mode;}felt.classList.add('lay-'+mode);felt.classList.toggle('big',big);
  var cs,cabOn=!!A('cabinet'),rwf=cabOn?6.65:5.52,rhf=cabOn?5.4:4.45;CABFULL=false;
  if(mode==='wide'){cs=(W-180-70-24-10)/rwf;if(cabOn&&!fs){var cf=(W-214-34)/12.1;if(cf>=60){cs=cf;CABFULL=true;}}}
  else if(mode==='narrow')cs=(W-10)/rwf;
  else{var lc=Math.round(big?clamp(W*0.24,230,340):clamp(W*0.27,150,230)),rc=Math.round(big?clamp(W*0.22,230,320):clamp(W*0.25,160,240));felt.style.setProperty('--lcol',lc+'px');felt.style.setProperty('--rcol',rc+'px');cs=Math.min((W-lc-rc-24-10)/rwf,(H-10)/rhf);}
  cs=clamp(cs,34,fs?170:(mode==='wide'?118:110));setCS(cs);
  felt.classList.remove('noside');
  if(fs){cab.style.alignContent='start';for(var i=0;i<80&&over()&&CS>34;i++)setCS(CS-(CS>80?3:1));
    if(mode==='narrow'&&CS<cs*0.94){felt.classList.add('noside');setCS(cs);for(i=0;i<80&&over()&&CS>34;i++)setCS(CS-(CS>80?3:1));}
    cab.style.alignContent='';}
  sizeFx();placeAutoPop();try{renderJP();}catch(e){}
}
var relT=0,lastSig='';
function relayout(force){cancelAnimationFrame(relT);relT=requestAnimationFrame(function(){var fs=root.classList.contains('fs'),sig=felt.clientWidth+'x'+(fs?felt.clientHeight:'n')+(fs?'f':'')+(root.classList.contains('fsshort')?'s':'');if(sig===lastSig&&!force)return;lastSig=sig;layout();});}
addEventListener('resize',function(){relayout();});
addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(d&&d.arcade==='fs')setTimeout(function(){relayout(true);},30);});

/* =====================================================================
   Effects canvas: sparks, coins, dust
   ===================================================================== */
var fxc=$('fxc'),fx=fxc.getContext('2d'),parts=[],FW=0,FH=0,DPR=1,fxDirty=false;
function sizeFx(){DPR=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);FW=felt.clientWidth;FH=felt.clientHeight;fxc.width=Math.round(FW*DPR);fxc.height=Math.round(FH*DPR);fxc.style.width=FW+'px';fxc.style.height=FH+'px';}
function feltXY(el){var a=el.getBoundingClientRect(),b=felt.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:a.left-b.left+a.width/2,y:a.top-b.top+a.height/2,w:a.width,h:a.height};}
function spark(x,y,col,n,sp){if(!opt.fx)return;for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,v=(0.08+Math.random()*0.32)*(sp||1);parts.push({k:'s',x:x,y:y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v-0.05,g:0.0004,life:0,max:420+Math.random()*420,sz:1.5+Math.random()*2.8,col:col});}kick();}
function ring(x,y,col,r){if(!opt.fx)return;parts.push({k:'r',x:x,y:y,life:0,max:380,sz:r,col:col});kick();}
function coins(n,from){for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var bottom=from==='bottom';parts.push({k:'c',x:bottom?FW*(0.2+Math.random()*0.6):Math.random()*FW,y:bottom?FH+20:-20-Math.random()*60,
  vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*(bottom?0.5:0.12),vy:bottom?-(0.75+Math.random()*0.55):(0.05+Math.random()*0.2),g:bottom?0.0016:0.0007,life:0,max:3400,sz:7+Math.random()*9,rot:Math.random()*6,vr:0.006+Math.random()*0.012});}kick();}
function dust(x,y,n){if(!opt.fx)return;for(var i=0;i<n;i++)parts.push({k:'d',x:x+(Math.random()-0.5)*30,y:y+(Math.random()-0.5)*10,vx:-(0.02+Math.random()*0.08),vy:-(0.01+Math.random()*0.04),g:0,life:0,max:700+Math.random()*600,sz:8+Math.random()*16});kick();}
function fxStep(dt){
  if(!parts.length){if(fxDirty){fx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);fx.clearRect(0,0,fxc.width,fxc.height);fxDirty=false;}return;}
  fx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);fx.clearRect(0,0,FW,FH);fxDirty=true;var keep=[];
  for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var p=parts[i];p.life+=dt;if(p.life>=p.max)continue;var t=p.life/p.max;
    if(p.k!=='r'){p.vy+=p.g*dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;}
    if(p.k==='c'&&p.y>FH+40)continue;keep.push(p);
    if(p.k==='s'){fx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';fx.globalAlpha=1-t;var r=p.sz*(1-t*0.5);var gr=fx.createRadialGradient(p.x,p.y,0,p.x,p.y,r*3);gr.addColorStop(0,'#fff');gr.addColorStop(0.25,p.col);gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');fx.fillStyle=gr;fx.beginPath();fx.arc(p.x,p.y,r*3,0,6.283);fx.fill();}
    else if(p.k==='r'){fx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';fx.globalAlpha=(1-t)*0.9;fx.strokeStyle=p.col;fx.lineWidth=3*(1-t)+1;fx.beginPath();fx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.sz*(0.3+t*1.1),0,6.283);fx.stroke();}
    else if(p.k==='k'){fx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';fx.globalAlpha=1-t*t;p.rot+=0.01*dt;fx.save();fx.translate(p.x,p.y);fx.rotate(p.rot);fx.fillStyle='#78350f';fx.strokeStyle='#3b1d0b';fx.lineWidth=1;fx.beginPath();fx.moveTo(-p.sz,-p.sz*0.4);fx.lineTo(p.sz*0.2,-p.sz);fx.lineTo(p.sz,0);fx.lineTo(0,p.sz*0.8);fx.closePath();fx.fill();fx.stroke();fx.restore();}
    else if(p.k==='d'){fx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';fx.globalAlpha=(1-t)*0.35;fx.fillStyle='#c8a46e';fx.beginPath();fx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.sz*(0.6+t),0,6.283);fx.fill();}
    else{fx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';fx.globalAlpha=t>0.85?(1-t)/0.15:1;p.rot+=p.vr*dt;var sx=Math.abs(Math.cos(p.rot));fx.save();fx.translate(p.x,p.y);fx.scale(Math.max(0.12,sx),1);
      var cg=fx.createRadialGradient(-p.sz*0.3,-p.sz*0.35,0,0,0,p.sz);cg.addColorStop(0,'#fffbe6');cg.addColorStop(0.45,'#f2d27e');cg.addColorStop(1,'#8a6a2a');fx.fillStyle=cg;fx.beginPath();fx.arc(0,0,p.sz,0,6.283);fx.fill();
      fx.strokeStyle='#6b4a12';fx.lineWidth=1.4;fx.stroke();fx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,248,220,.75)';fx.lineWidth=1;fx.beginPath();fx.arc(0,0,p.sz*0.62,0,6.283);fx.stroke();fx.restore();}
  }
  fx.globalAlpha=1;fx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';parts=keep;
}
function shake(k){if(!opt.fx)return;var a=Math.min(2+k*1.6,11),kf=[];for(var i=0;i<8;i++)kf.push({transform:'translate('+((Math.random()-0.5)*a*2).toFixed(1)+'px,'+((Math.random()-0.5)*a*1.4).toFixed(1)+'px)'});kf.push({transform:'none'});anim(cab,kf,{duration:420});}

/* =====================================================================
   HUD
   ===================================================================== */
function hud(){
  $('sBank').textContent=money(st.bank);var net=r2(st.bank-st.sessBase);var n=$('sNet');n.textContent=smoney(net);n.className='v'+(net>0?' g':net<0?' r':'');
  $('sSpins').textContent=st.spins;$('sBig').textContent=st.big?money(st.big):'\u2013';
  $('pbC').textContent=money(st.bank);$('pbW').textContent=st.lastWin?money(st.lastWin,true):'$0';
}
function renderBet(){$('betV').textContent=money(bet());$('pbB').textContent=money(bet());renderJP();setButtons();try{renderBounty();renderMeter();}catch(e){}}
function jpShort(v){return v>=1e6?'$'+(Math.round(v/1e5)/10)+'M':v>=1000?'$'+(Math.round(v/100)/10)+'K':'$'+Math.round(v);}
function renderJP(){var tiny=(LAY==='short'&&!felt.classList.contains('big'))||(LAY==='narrow'&&root.classList.contains('fs')&&felt.classList.contains('a-jp_grand'));for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var v=r2(pools[i]*bet());var el=$('jp'+i);el.textContent=tiny?jpShort(v):(LAY==='narrow'||LAY==='short')?money(Math.round(v)):money(v,true);
  /* if the amount does not fit its pill, drop the cents, then shorten to $10K */
  if(el.scrollWidth>el.clientWidth+1){el.textContent=money(Math.round(v));if(el.scrollWidth>el.clientWidth+1)el.textContent=jpShort(v);}}}
function winMsg(t){var b=$('winV');b.className='msg';b.textContent=t;$('winK').textContent='';$('winK').style.display='none';}
function winAmt(v,label){var b=$('winV');b.className='';b.textContent=money(v,true);var k=$('winK');k.style.display='';k.textContent=label||'Win';}
function setButtons(){
  var busy=st.busy,auto=st.auto>0,sb=$('spinBtn');
  $('betDn').disabled=busy||auto||st.betI===0;$('betUp').disabled=busy||auto||st.betI===BETS.length-1;$('maxBtn').disabled=busy||auto;
  sb.classList.toggle('busy',busy&&!auto);sb.classList.toggle('stop',auto);$('spinL').textContent=auto?'STOP':'SPIN';$('autoCount').textContent=auto?String(st.auto):'';
  sb.setAttribute('aria-label',auto?'Stop autoplay':busy?'Hurry the spin':'Spin');
  var held=st.autoHeld>0&&!opt.stopFS;$('autoBtn').classList.toggle('run',auto||held);$('autoBtn').textContent=auto?'Stop':held?'Stop auto ('+st.autoHeld+')':'Auto';$('turboBtn').classList.toggle('on',opt.turbo);
}

/* =====================================================================
   Reels: drop out, spin blur, land column by column
   ===================================================================== */
function dropOut(){
  var ps=[],P=CS+Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07));
  for(var c=0;c<NRE;c++)tiles[c].forEach(function(t,r){if(!t)return;var a=anim(t,opt.fx?[{transform:'none',opacity:1},{transform:'translateY('+(P*(4.6-r)).toFixed(0)+'px) scaleY(1.1)',opacity:0.6}]:[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],
    {duration:D(opt.fx?250:160),delay:D(c*45+(3-r)*14),easing:'cubic-bezier(.55,0,1,.6)',fill:'forwards'});ps.push(done(a).then(function(){if(t.parentNode)t.parentNode.removeChild(t);}));});
  tiles=[[],[],[],[],[]];return Promise.all(ps);
}
function dropIn(c,col,fromRows,isLand){
  var P=CS+Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07)),ps=[];
  for(var r=3;r>=0;r--){
    var t=tiles[c][r],from=fromRows?fromRows[r]:r-4.6;
    if(!fromRows||from<0){t=makeSym(col[r],c,r);reelsEl.appendChild(t);tiles[c][r]=t;}
    else{t=tiles[c][r];}
    if(fromRows&&from===r)continue;
    var dy=(from-r)*P,dl=D((3-r)*(isLand?26:34));
    var kf=opt.fx?[{transform:'translateY('+dy.toFixed(1)+'px) scaleY(1.1)',filter:isLand?'blur(2.5px)':'none',easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.9,.5)'},
      {offset:0.7,transform:'translateY(0) scaleY(.93)',filter:'none',easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.4,1)'},{offset:0.85,transform:'translateY('+(-CS*0.06).toFixed(1)+'px) scaleY(1.02)'},{transform:'none'}]
      :[{transform:'translateY('+dy.toFixed(1)+'px)',opacity:0.3},{transform:'none',opacity:1}];
    ps.push(done(anim(t,kf,{duration:D(isLand?360:420),delay:dl,fill:'backwards'})));
  }
  return Promise.all(ps);
}
async function spinReels(g){
  SFX.whirOn();
  var outP=dropOut();
  for(var c=0;c<NRE;c++){fillBlur(c);blurs[c].classList.add('on');blurs[c].classList.remove('slow');}
  await wait(D(opt.fx?320:180));
  var scat=0,pans=0,lands=[];
  for(c=0;c<NRE;c++){
    var anti=scat>=2||pans>=2;
    if(anti&&!st.hurry){glows[c].classList.add('on');blurs[c].classList.add('slow');SFX.anticOn();felt.classList.add('hot');await wait(D(c===NRE-1?1300:1000)*(opt.turbo?1.6:1));SFX.anticOff();}
    else if(c>0)await wait(D(150));
    blurs[c].classList.remove('on');
    lands.push(dropIn(c,g[c],null,true));
    (function(cc,col){after(D(230+60),function(){SFX.thunk(cc);var n=0;for(var r=0;r<4;r++)if(col[r]===ENG.S)n++;if(n){tiles[cc].forEach(function(t){if(t&&t.__s===ENG.S){t.classList.add('hot');after(900,function(){t.classList.remove('hot');});var p=feltXY(t);spark(p.x,p.y,'#f2d27e',12,0.8);}});}});})(c,g[c]);
    for(var r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[c][r]===ENG.S){scat++;(function(n){after(D(290),function(){SFX.ding(n);});})(scat);}
    for(r=0;r<4;r++)if(g[c][r]===ENG.FP){pans++;(function(n,cc){after(D(290),function(){SFX.ding(n);tiles[cc].forEach(function(t){if(t&&t.__s===ENG.FP){t.classList.add('hot');after(900,function(){t.classList.remove('hot');});}});});})(pans,c);}
    glows[c].classList.remove('on');
  }
  felt.classList.remove('hot');
  await outP;await Promise.all(lands);await wait(D(80));
  SFX.whirOff();
}

/* =====================================================================
   Cascades: flash, glowing paths, burst, gravity, refill, multiplier
   ===================================================================== */
function center(c,r){var P=CS+Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07));return [c*P+CS/2,r*P+CS/2];}
function drawPaths(wins){
  var h='';wins.forEach(function(w){var by=[[],[],[],[],[]];w.cells.forEach(function(p){by[p[0]].push(p[1]);});var col=SYMCOL[SK[w.sym]];
    for(var c=0;c<w.n-1;c++)by[c].forEach(function(a){by[c+1].forEach(function(b){var p1=center(c,a),p2=center(c+1,b),mx=(p1[0]+p2[0])/2;
      h+='<path d="M'+p1[0].toFixed(1)+' '+p1[1].toFixed(1)+'C'+mx.toFixed(1)+' '+p1[1].toFixed(1)+' '+mx.toFixed(1)+' '+p2[1].toFixed(1)+' '+p2[0].toFixed(1)+' '+p2[1].toFixed(1)+'" stroke="'+col+'" style="color:'+col+'" pathLength="1" stroke-dasharray="1" stroke-dashoffset="1"/>';});});});
  pathsEl.innerHTML=h;
  [].forEach.call(pathsEl.querySelectorAll('path'),function(p,i){anim(p,[{strokeDashoffset:1},{strokeDashoffset:0}],{duration:D(360),delay:D(Math.min(i,20)*12),fill:'forwards',easing:'ease-out'});});
}
function showLabel(amt,m){wlab.innerHTML='<b>'+money(amt,true)+'</b>'+(m>1?'<span>\u00d7'+m+' MULTIPLIER</span>':'');wlab.style.opacity='1';
  anim(wlab,[{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.4)',opacity:0},{offset:0.6,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.12)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1)',opacity:1}],{duration:D(380),easing:'ease-out'});}
function hideLabel(){var a=anim(wlab,[{opacity:1,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1)'},{opacity:0,transform:'translate(-50%,-80%) scale(.9)'}],{duration:D(220),fill:'forwards'});done(a).then(function(){wlab.style.opacity='0';if(a)a.cancel();});}
var runShown=0;
function countWin(to,label){var from=runShown;runShown=to;if(to<=0)return;var bar=$('winbar');bar.classList.remove('pop');void bar.offsetWidth;bar.classList.add('pop');
  tween(D(450),function(p){winAmt(from+(to-from)*p,label);});}
async function wildLine(row){
  for(var c=1;c<4;c++){var t=tiles[c][row];if(t){t.classList.add('line');var p=feltXY(t);spark(p.x,p.y,'#34d399',16,1);ring(p.x,p.y,'#f2d27e',CS*0.8);}}
  SFX.bell();winMsg('STAMPEDE LINE! The jackpot lights up\u2026');await wait(D(1300));
  for(c=1;c<4;c++){var t2=tiles[c][row];if(t2)t2.classList.remove('line');}
}
async function playCascades(res,b,inFS){
  var total=0;
  for(var k=0;k<res.steps.length;k++){
    var s=res.steps[k];
    if(res.jp&&res.jp.at===k)await wildLine(res.jp.row);
    if(s.lasso)await lassoAnim(s.lasso);
    var on={};s.cells.forEach(function(p){on[p[0]+','+p[1]]=1;});
    var symOf={};s.wins.forEach(function(w){w.cells.forEach(function(p){if(!symOf[p[0]+','+p[1]])symOf[p[0]+','+p[1]]=w.sym;});});
    for(var c=0;c<NRE;c++)for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var t=tiles[c][r];if(!t)continue;var key=c+','+r;t.classList.toggle('win',!!on[key]);t.classList.toggle('dim',!on[key]);if(on[key])t.style.setProperty('--gc',SYMCOL[SK[symOf[key]]]);}
    drawPaths(s.wins);
    var amt=r2(s.win*b);total=r2(total+amt);
    setLadder(s.idx,k>0||inFS);
    showLabel(amt,s.mult);SFX.chime(k+(inFS?Math.min(s.idx,4):0));
    countWin(r2((inFS?st.fs.total:0)+total),inFS?'Stampede total':(k?'Cascade total':'Win'));
    var desc=s.wins.map(function(w){return w.ways+' way'+(w.ways>1?'s':'')+' of '+SYMNAME[SK[w.sym]];}).join(', ');
    $('winbar').title=desc;
    await wait(D(k===0?900:760));
    if(!inFS&&meterStep())st.meterFull=true;
    var tgt=BT.order[btState().t];if(s.wins.some(function(w){return w.sym===tgt;}))await bountyHit(b,inFS);
    /* burst */
    pathsEl.innerHTML='';hideLabel();SFX.burst();
    var pops=[];s.cells.forEach(function(p){var t=tiles[p[0]][p[1]];if(!t)return;var q=feltXY(t),col=SYMCOL[SK[t.__s]];spark(q.x,q.y,col,opt.turbo?7:12,1);ring(q.x,q.y,col,CS*0.7);
      t.classList.remove('win');pops.push(done(anim(t,[{transform:'scale(1)',filter:'brightness(1)',opacity:1},{offset:0.35,transform:'scale(1.22)',filter:'brightness(2.2)',opacity:1},{transform:'scale(.1) rotate(8deg)',filter:'brightness(3)',opacity:0}],{duration:D(280),fill:'forwards',easing:'ease-in'})));});
    if(k>=2)shake(k);
    if(k>=3&&opt.fx)felt.classList.add('hot');
    await Promise.all(pops);
    s.cells.forEach(function(p){var t=tiles[p[0]][p[1]];if(t&&t.parentNode)t.parentNode.removeChild(t);tiles[p[0]][p[1]]=null;});
    /* gravity */
    var old=tiles.map(function(col){return col.slice();}),falls=[];
    for(c=0;c<NRE;c++){var nc=[null,null,null,null];for(r=0;r<4;r++){var fr=s.falls[c][r];if(fr>=0){nc[r]=old[c][fr];nc[r].style.setProperty('--r',r);}}tiles[c]=nc;}
    for(c=0;c<NRE;c++)tiles[c].forEach(function(t){if(t)t.classList.remove('dim');});
    for(c=0;c<NRE;c++){(function(cc){var moved=s.falls[cc].some(function(f,r){return f!==r;});if(!moved)return;falls.push(dropIn(cc,s.grid[cc],s.falls[cc],false).then(function(){SFX.thunk(cc);}));})(c);}
    await Promise.all(falls);
    setLadder(s.idx+1,true);SFX.whoosh(Math.min(s.idx+1,4));
    await wait(D(160));
  }
  felt.classList.remove('hot');
  if(res.jp&&res.jp.at===res.steps.length)await wildLine(res.jp.row);
  for(var c2=0;c2<NRE;c2++)tiles[c2].forEach(function(t){if(t)t.classList.remove('dim','win');});
  st.stats.bestChain=Math.max(st.stats.bestChain,res.steps.length);
  return total;
}

/* =====================================================================
   Celebrations: big / mega / epic wins, the Stampede, jackpots
   ===================================================================== */
var celebEl=$('celeb');
function tierOf(x){return x>=100?2:x>=40?1:x>=15?0:-1;}
var TIERS=['BIG WIN','MEGA WIN','EPIC WIN'],TCLS=['','mega','epic'];
/* write a title on the plaque so it always sits inside the wooden face (x 24-76%, y 30-70% of the art), shrinking long titles */
function fitPlaque(pl){if(!pl)return;var b=pl.querySelector('b');function fit(){var W=pl.clientWidth,H=pl.clientHeight;if(!W)return;var f=Math.min(H*0.30,W*0.075);b.style.fontSize=f+'px';
  for(var i=0;i<80&&b.scrollWidth>b.clientWidth+1&&f>8;i++){f-=1;b.style.fontSize=f+'px';}}fit();requestAnimationFrame(fit);after(60,fit);if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.ready)document.fonts.ready.then(fit);}
function celebrate(o){
  return new Promise(function(res){
    var amt=o.amt||0,dur=o.dur||0,shown=false,ended=false,closeT=null,coinT=null,startVT=vt;
    celebEl.className='celeb on'+(o.cls?' '+o.cls:'');$('cPre').textContent=o.pre||'';var tt=$('cTier');tt.className='ctier '+(o.tcls||'');if(o.banner==='__fztitle'&&FZFRAME.frenzyTitle){tt.innerHTML='<span class="fzt"><img src="'+FZFRAME.frenzyTitle+'" alt=""><b>'+esc(o.tier||'')+'</b></span>';tt.classList.add('img');fitPlaque(tt.querySelector('.fzt'));tt.setAttribute('aria-label',o.tier||'');}else if(o.banner&&A(o.banner)){tt.innerHTML=imgTag(o.banner);tt.classList.add('img');tt.setAttribute('aria-label',o.tier||'');}else tt.textContent=o.tier||'';
    void tt.offsetWidth;tt.style.animation='none';void tt.offsetWidth;tt.style.animation='';
    $('cSub').textContent=o.sub||'';$('cAmt').textContent=o.amt!==undefined?money(o.amt?0:0,true):'';$('cAmt').style.display=o.amt!==undefined?'':'none';
    var btn=$('cBtn');btn.classList.toggle('hide',!o.btn);btn.textContent=o.btn||'';$('cTap').textContent=o.btn?'':'Tap to skip';
    st.celeb={skip:skip};
    if(o.coins){var rate=o.coins;(function emit(){if(ended)return;coins(rate,'top');if(Math.random()<0.5)coins(Math.ceil(rate/2),'bottom');if(vt-startVT<(dur+1200))coinT=after(260,emit);})();}
    var tw={stop:false};
    if(o.amt!==undefined&&dur>0){var clink=0;tween(dur,function(p){if(tw.stop)return;var e=1-Math.pow(1-p,2.2);$('cAmt').textContent=money(amt*e,true);if(++clink%5===0)SFX.coin();}).then(function(){if(!tw.stop)finish();});}
    else after(Math.max(dur,300),function(){finish();});
    function finish(){if(ended||shown)return;shown=true;tw.stop=true;if(o.amt!==undefined)$('cAmt').textContent=money(amt,true);
      var hold=o.hold!==undefined?o.hold:(st.auto>0?1300:2400);if(o.btn&&st.auto===0&&!o.autoStart)return;closeT=after(o.btn?(o.autoStart||hold):hold,close);}
    function skip(){if(ended)return;if(!shown){finish();return;}close();}
    function close(){if(ended)return;ended=true;if(closeT)cancelT(closeT);if(coinT)cancelT(coinT);celebEl.className='celeb'+(o.cls?' '+o.cls:'');st.celeb=null;after(330,res);}
    celebEl.onclick=function(e){e.stopPropagation();skip();};
    btn.onclick=function(e){e.stopPropagation();shown=true;close();};
  });
}
function bigWin(x,amt){var t=tierOf(x);if(t<0)return Promise.resolve();SFX.fanfare(t);shake(3+t*2);
  return celebrate({banner:['win_big','win_mega','win_epic'][t],tier:TIERS[t],tcls:TCLS[t],amt:amt,dur:[2600,3800,5200][t],coins:[3,5,8][t],sub:Math.round(x)+'\u00d7 your bet'});}
async function gallop(n){
  var fl=$('flyers');if(!opt.fx){SFX.gallop(1);return;}
  SFX.gallop(2.6);var H0=felt.clientHeight,W0=felt.clientWidth,hs=[],count=n||7;
  for(var i=0;i<count;i++){var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='horse'+(i%3===1?' gold':'');var sz=Math.round(clamp(H0*(0.16+Math.random()*0.16),60,220));h.style.setProperty('--hw',sz+'px');
    var y=H0*(0.12+0.7*(i/(count-1||1)))+(Math.random()-0.5)*H0*0.08-sz/2;h.style.top=y.toFixed(0)+'px';fl.appendChild(h);
    var kf=[],steps=14,dur=1500+Math.random()*700,dl=i*120+Math.random()*180;
    for(var s=0;s<=steps;s++){var p=s/steps,x=-sz*1.2+(W0+sz*2.4)*p,yy=-Math.abs(Math.sin(p*Math.PI*6))*sz*0.12;kf.push({transform:'translate('+x.toFixed(0)+'px,'+yy.toFixed(0)+'px) rotate('+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI*6)*-5).toFixed(1)+'deg)'});}
    var a=anim(h,kf,{duration:dur,delay:dl,fill:'both'});hs.push({el:h,a:a,sz:sz,y:y,t0:vt+dl,dur:dur,W0:W0});}
  var t0=vt,dt=after(60,function dusty(){hs.forEach(function(o){var p=(vt-o.t0)/o.dur;if(p>0&&p<1){dust(-o.sz*1.2+(o.W0+o.sz*2.4)*p,o.y+o.sz*0.88,2);}});if(vt-t0<2600)after(70,dusty);});
  for(var k=0;k<5;k++)after(k*420,function(){shake(4);});
  await Promise.all(hs.map(function(o){return done(o.a);}));
  hs.forEach(function(o){if(o.el.parentNode)o.el.parentNode.removeChild(o.el);});
}

/* =====================================================================
   One spin
   ===================================================================== */
function takeForce(fs){var f=st.force;if(!f||(!!f.fs)!==!!fs&&f.fs!==undefined&&f.fs!=='any')return {};st.force=null;var o={};
  if(f.grid)o.grid=f.grid.map(function(col){return String(col).split('').map(function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);});});
  if(f.queue)ENG.queue(f.queue);if(f.tier!==undefined)o.tier=f.tier;if(f.gold!==undefined)o.gold=f.gold;if(f.lasso!==undefined)o.lasso=f.lasso;if(f.tokens)o.tokens=f.tokens;if(f.bscript)st.bscript=f.bscript;return o;}
function affordable(){for(var i=BETS.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(BETS[i]<=st.bank+1e-9&&i<=st.betI)return i;return -1;}
async function spin(){
  if(st.busy||AWAY)return false;
  closePop();closePtab();
  if(st.bank<1-1e-9){showBroke();return false;}
  if(bet()>st.bank+1e-9){var j=affordable();if(j<0){showBroke();return false;}st.betI=j;renderBet();toastMsg('Bet lowered to '+money(bet())+' to fit your bankroll.');}
  var b=bet();st.busy=true;st.hurry=false;st.bank=r2(st.bank-b);st.spins++;st.stats.wager=r2(st.stats.wager+b);
  for(var i=0;i<4;i++)pools[i]+=JP.inc[i];saveJP();renderJP();hud();setButtons();
  runShown=0;setLadder(0,false);winMsg(pick(['Good luck!','Here we go\u2026','Ride \u2019em, cowboy!','Saddle up\u2026']));
  var spinWin=0,fsWin=0,jpWin=0,jpTier=-1;st.extra=0;st.meterFull=false;st.nearMsg='';
  try{
    var res=ENG.spin(takeForce(false));
    await spinReels(res.pre);
    if(res.gold>0)await goldRush(res.gold,res.start);
    var w=await playCascades(res,b,false);spinWin=w;
    if(res.jp){jpTier=res.jp.tier;jpWin=await jackpot(res.jp.tier,b);}
    if(spinWin>0){st.bank=r2(st.bank+spinWin);hud();}
    /* nugget family: near miss, nugget jackpots, then the Gold Frenzy first and the Nugget Respins second */
    var ng=res.nug;
    if(ng){
      if(ng.near&&!ng.frenzy)await nearMiss(ng);
      for(var jl=0;jl<ng.jps.length;jl++){jpTier=Math.max(jpTier,ng.jps[jl]);await labelJackpot(ng.jps[jl],b);}
      if(ng.frenzy)await bonusPause(function(){return nugBonus('frenzy',res.final,b);});
      if(ng.respin)await bonusPause(function(){return nugBonus('respin',res.final,b);});
    }
    var fullItems=await collectTokens(res.tokens||[],b);
    for(var fi=0;fi<fullItems.length;fi++)await bonusPause(function(){return minigame(fullItems[fi],b);});
    if(!spinWin&&!jpWin&&!res.fsAward&&!st.extra&&!st.meterFull){winMsg(st.nearMsg||pick(['No win this time.','Not this spin, partner.','The herd rides on.','So close. Spin again?']));SFX.nowin();}
    if(res.steps.length>=4&&spinWin>0)feedMine('a '+res.steps.length+'-cascade chain for '+money(spinWin),spinWin/b);
    var xb=spinWin/b;
    if(!res.fsAward&&tierOf(xb)>=0){await bigWin(xb,spinWin);shareWin(spinWin,'big',res.steps.length);}
    if(st.meterFull){st.stats.feats++;fsWin=r2(fsWin+await bonusPause(async function(){await finishLine();PROG.m[String(st.betI)]=0;saveProg();renderMeter();return freeSpins(MT.spins,b,spinWin+fsWin,MT.idx,'meter');}));}
  }catch(e){console.error(e);}
  var tot=r2(spinWin+fsWin+jpWin+(st.extra||0));
  if(tot>0){st.stats.hits++;st.stats.won=r2(st.stats.won+tot);if(tot>st.big)st.big=tot;if(tot>=b*5)logMine(tot,b,spinWin,fsWin,jpTier);}
  st.lastWin=tot;st.busy=false;st.hurry=false;hud();setButtons();renderStats();
  if(tot>0&&!st.fs)winAmt(tot,fsWin?'Total win':'Win');
  reactToMe(tot/b,fsWin>0||jpTier>=0);
  checkBroke();
  autoNext(tot,b);
  return true;
}
/* the jackpot lights run across the four coins and settle on the one you won, so it's clear which jackpot it was */
async function jpLights(tier){
  var order=[0,1,2,3],els=order.map(function(i){return $('jpw'+i);}),steps=14+tier+Math.floor(Math.random()*4)*4,i=0,cur=0;
  winMsg('<b>JACKPOT!</b> Three Wilds in a row. Which one will it be?');
  for(;i<steps;i++){cur=i%4;els.forEach(function(e,k){e.classList.toggle('lit',k===cur);});SFX.click();await wait(D(70+Math.pow(i/steps,3)*380));}
  while(cur!==tier){cur=(cur+1)%4;els.forEach(function(e,k){e.classList.toggle('lit',k===cur);});SFX.click();await wait(D(480));}
  await wait(D(500));els.forEach(function(e){e.classList.remove('lit');});
}
async function jackpot(tier,b){
  await jpLights(tier);
  var amt=r2(pools[tier]*b);pools[tier]=JP.seed[tier];saveJP();
  var el=$('jpw'+tier);el.classList.remove('hit');void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add('hit');
  SFX.bell();SFX.fanfare(2);shake(8);
  await celebrate({cls:'jack',pre:'Stampede line',tier:JP.label[tier].toUpperCase()+' JACKPOT',tcls:tier>=2?'epic':tier===1?'mega':'',amt:amt,dur:[2600,3400,4600,6000][tier],coins:[4,6,8,10][tier],sub:'Three Wilds in one row on reels 2 to 4 lit up the '+JP.label[tier]+' Jackpot.'});
  st.bank=r2(st.bank+amt);st.stats.jps++;hud();renderJP();
  feedMine('the '+JP.label[tier]+' Jackpot for '+money(amt),99);shareWin(amt,'jp',tier);
  after(3200,function(){el.classList.remove('hit');});
  return amt;
}
async function freeSpins(n,b,trigWin,startIdx,kind){
  st.fs={left:n,total:0,given:n,idx:startIdx||0,played:0,bet:b,x10:true};
  var gp=gallop(8);
  await wait(D(700));
  await celebrate({cls:'fs',pre:'The rider crossed the finish line',tier:'STAMPEDE!',tcls:'fs',sub:n+' free spins on reels packed with Wilds. Every win pays \u00d710.',btn:'Ride!',autoStart:st.autoHeld>0?1800:4500,dur:600});
  await gp;
  felt.classList.add('fsm');$('fsbar').classList.remove('hide');setLadder(LAD.length-1,false);renderFS();
  feedMine('the Stampede: '+n+' free spins',50);
  var capped=false;
  while(st.fs.left>0&&!capped){
    st.fs.left--;st.fs.played++;st.stats.fsSpins++;renderFS();
    await wait(D(380));
    var r=ENG.spin(Object.assign({fs:true,x10:true},takeForce(true)));
    runShown=st.fs.total;
    if(r.gold>0){await spinReels(r.pre);await goldRush(r.gold,r.start);}else await spinReels(r.start);
    var w=await playCascades(r,b,true);
    st.fs.idx=r.idx;
    if(r.jp){var jw=await jackpot(r.jp.tier,b);w=r2(w+jw);}
    var room=r2(ENG.MAXWIN*b-trigWin-st.fs.total);if(w>=room){w=Math.max(0,room);capped=true;}
    st.fs.total=r2(st.fs.total+w);st.bank=r2(st.bank+w);hud();
    if(w>0)countWin(st.fs.total,'Stampede total');else winMsg('Free spin '+st.fs.played+': no win. Total '+money(st.fs.total,true)+'.');
    renderFS();
    await wait(D(420));
  }
  var tot=st.fs.total,x=tot/b,t=tierOf((tot+trigWin)/b);
  if(capped)toastMsg('Max win reached: '+ENG.MAXWIN.toLocaleString()+'\u00d7 your bet.');
  SFX.fanfare(Math.max(0,t));
  await celebrate({cls:'fs',pre:'Stampede complete',banner:t>=0?['win_big','win_mega','win_epic'][t]:'',tier:t>=0?TIERS[t]:'STAMPEDE',tcls:t>=0?TCLS[t]:'fs',amt:tot,dur:tot>0?(t>=0?[3000,4200,5600][t]:1800):300,coins:tot>0?(t>=0?[4,6,9][t]:2):0,
    sub:st.fs.played+' free spins at \u00d710'+(tot>0?' \u00b7 '+Math.round(x)+'\u00d7 your bet':'')+'. The rider heads back to the start line.'});
  st.stats.lastFS={spins:st.fs.played,win:tot};
  feedMine('a '+st.fs.played+'-spin Stampede for '+money(tot),tot/b+40);shareWin(tot,'fs',st.fs.played);
  felt.classList.remove('fsm');$('fsbar').classList.add('hide');setLadder(0,false);st.fs=null;renderFS();
  return tot;
}
function renderFS(){
  var f=st.fs;if(!f){$('fswK').textContent='Max win';$('fswV').textContent=ENG.MAXWIN.toLocaleString()+'\u00d7';return;}
  $('fsbar').textContent='Stampede \u00b7 Free spin '+f.played+' of '+(f.played+f.left)+' \u00b7 '+money(f.total,true);
  $('fswK').textContent='Free spins';$('fswV').textContent=f.left+' left';
}

async function bountyHit(b,inFS){
  var o=btState(),el=$('bty');o.h++;saveProg();SFX.gun();
  el.classList.remove('hit');void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add('hit');
  var hp=$('holes').children[o.h-1];if(hp){var q=feltXY(hp);spark(q.x,q.y,'#fde68a',10,0.7);}
  renderBounty();
  if(o.h<BT.need){await wait(D(260));return 0;}
  /* collected */
  var k=SK[BT.order[o.t]],amt=r2(BT.x[o.lv]*b);
  await wait(D(300));
  var bpEl=$('bp');
  if(A('wanted_collected')){bpEl.classList.add('col');SFX.gun();after(140,SFX.gun);anim(bpEl,[{transform:'scale(1.18) rotate(-3deg)'},{transform:'none'}],{duration:380,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.4)'});await wait(900);}
  if(A('wanted_burning_collected')){bpEl.classList.add('burnc');var rr=feltXY(bpEl);for(var bi=0;bi<8;bi++)after(bi*110,function(){spark(rr.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*rr.w*0.8,rr.y+rr.h*0.35-Math.random()*rr.h*0.3,'#fb923c',9,0.6);});SFX.rumble();await wait(1000);}
  el.classList.add('burn');var r=feltXY(el);for(var i=0;i<6;i++)after(i*120,function(){spark(r.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*r.w,r.y+r.h*0.3-Math.random()*r.h*0.6,'#fb923c',8,0.6);});
  SFX.rumble();await wait(1100);
  SFX.fanfare(1);
  var ci=$('cImg');ci.classList.remove('hide','tip');ci.querySelector('i').style.backgroundImage='url(\''+artUrl(k)+'\')';ci.querySelector('i').style.backgroundPosition=k==='A'?'50% 30%':'50% 18%';ci.querySelector('b').textContent=SYMNAME[k].toUpperCase();
  void ci.offsetWidth;ci.classList.add('tip');
  await celebrate({pre:'Bounty Board',tier:'BOUNTY COLLECTED',tcls:'mega',amt:amt,dur:1800,coins:4,sub:SYMNAME[k]+' tips their hat. Much obliged, partner. Next poster pays more.'});
  ci.classList.add('hide');
  st.bank=r2(st.bank+amt);st.extra=r2((st.extra||0)+amt);hud();
  o.h=0;o.t=(o.t+1)%BT.order.length;o.lv=(o.lv+1)%BT.x.length;saveProg();
  el.classList.remove('burn');bpEl.classList.remove('col','burnc');renderBounty();void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add('fresh');after(700,function(){el.classList.remove('fresh');});
  var h='<i style="color:#34d399">You</i> collected the bounty on <b>'+esc(SYMNAME[k])+'</b> for <b>'+money(amt)+'</b>!';feedPush(h);logLine(h,true);shareWin(amt,'bounty',BT.order.indexOf(CODE.indexOf(k)));
  if(bots.length)botLine(pick(bots),'cheer',0.8,600,true);
  return amt;
}
/* ---------- Gold Rush ---------- */
async function goldRush(c,grid){
  var px=$('pickaxe'),t=tiles[c][0];if(!t)return;var top=feltXY(t),sz=Math.round(CS*1.25);
  if(A('fx_prospector')){px.className='pickaxe pros';px.style.backgroundImage=AU('fx_prospector');sz=Math.round(CS*2.3);px.style.width=px.style.height=sz+'px';px.style.left=(top.x-sz*0.42)+'px';px.style.top=(top.y-top.h/2-sz*0.62)+'px';px.style.opacity='1';
    banner('GOLD RUSH!','',1600);
    await done(anim(px,[{transform:'translate(70%,-30%) rotate(18deg) scale(.6)',opacity:0},{offset:.35,transform:'translate(0,0) rotate(-14deg) scale(1)',opacity:1},{offset:.6,transform:'rotate(-20deg) scale(1.02)',opacity:1},{offset:.85,transform:'rotate(10deg) scale(1)',opacity:1},{transform:'rotate(6deg)',opacity:1}],{duration:D(900),easing:'ease-in-out'}));
  }else{px.className='pickaxe';px.style.backgroundImage='';
  px.style.width=px.style.height=sz+'px';px.style.left=(top.x-sz*0.2)+'px';px.style.top=(top.y-top.h/2-sz*0.95)+'px';px.style.opacity='1';
  banner('GOLD RUSH!','',1500);
  await done(anim(px,[{transform:'rotate(-70deg)',opacity:0},{offset:.35,transform:'rotate(-85deg)',opacity:1},{offset:.75,transform:'rotate(22deg)',opacity:1},{transform:'rotate(14deg)',opacity:1}],{duration:D(700),easing:'cubic-bezier(.6,0,.9,.5)'}));}
  SFX.clang();shake(6);
  for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var q=feltXY(tiles[c][r]);spark(q.x,q.y,'#f2d27e',16,1.2);dust(q.x,q.y,5);rocks(q.x,q.y,6);}
  for(r=0;r<4;r++){(function(r){after(D(r*90),function(){var old=tiles[c][r],nw=makeSym(grid[c][r],c,r);nw.classList.add('gold');reelsEl.appendChild(nw);tiles[c][r]=nw;if(old&&old.parentNode)old.parentNode.removeChild(old);
    anim(nw,[{transform:'rotateY(90deg) scale(1.2)',filter:'brightness(3)'},{transform:'none',filter:'brightness(1)'}],{duration:D(320),easing:'ease-out'});SFX.ding(2+r);});})(r);}
  anim(px,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],{duration:D(400),delay:D(300),fill:'forwards'});
  await wait(D(800));px.style.opacity='0';
}
function rocks(x,y,n){if(!opt.fx)return;for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var a=-Math.PI*Math.random(),v=0.15+Math.random()*0.3;parts.push({k:'k',x:x,y:y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v,g:0.0012,life:0,max:900,sz:3+Math.random()*5,rot:Math.random()*6});}kick();}
/* ---------- Lasso ---------- */
async function lassoAnim(L){
  var src=tiles[L.from[0]][L.from[1]],dst=tiles[L.to[0]][L.to[1]],anchor=null;
  for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var t=tiles[0][r];if(t&&t.__s===L.sym){anchor=[0,r];break;}}
  if(!src||!dst)return;
  banner('LASSO!','lasso',1300);SFX.rope();
  var a=center(anchor?anchor[0]:0,anchor?anchor[1]:L.to[1]),b=center(L.from[0],L.from[1]),mx=(a[0]+b[0])/2,my=Math.min(a[1],b[1])-CS*1.1;
  pathsEl.innerHTML='<path class="rope" d="M'+a[0].toFixed(1)+' '+a[1].toFixed(1)+'Q'+mx.toFixed(1)+' '+my.toFixed(1)+' '+b[0].toFixed(1)+' '+b[1].toFixed(1)+'" pathLength="1" stroke-dasharray="1" stroke-dashoffset="1"/><circle class="loop" cx="'+b[0].toFixed(1)+'" cy="'+b[1].toFixed(1)+'" r="'+(CS*0.5).toFixed(1)+'" opacity="0"/>';
  var rope=pathsEl.querySelector('path'),loop=pathsEl.querySelector('circle'),limg=null;
  if(A('fx_lasso')){var lq=feltXY(src),ls=CS*1.5;limg=document.createElement('img');limg.className='lassoimg';limg.src=A('fx_lasso');limg.alt='';limg.style.cssText='left:'+(lq.x-ls/2)+'px;top:'+(lq.y-ls/2)+'px;width:'+ls+'px;height:'+ls+'px';limg.onerror=function(){if(limg.parentNode)limg.parentNode.removeChild(limg);};felt.appendChild(limg);
    anim(limg,[{transform:'translate(-120%,-80%) rotate(-140deg) scale(.4)',opacity:0},{transform:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:D(520),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1.2)'});}
  await done(anim(rope,[{strokeDashoffset:1},{strokeDashoffset:0}],{duration:D(520),fill:'forwards',easing:'ease-out'}));
  if(!limg)loop.setAttribute('opacity','1');anim(loop,[{transform:'scale(1.6)',transformOrigin:b[0]+'px '+b[1]+'px'},{transform:'scale(1)',transformOrigin:b[0]+'px '+b[1]+'px'}],{duration:D(220)});
  src.classList.add('win');await wait(D(320));SFX.whoosh(2);
  /* drag: swap the two tiles */
  var P=CS+Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07)),dx=(L.to[0]-L.from[0])*P,dy=(L.to[1]-L.from[1])*P;
  pathsEl.innerHTML='';
  var a1=anim(src,[{transform:'none'},{offset:.5,transform:'translate('+(dx/2)+'px,'+(dy/2-CS*0.5)+'px) rotate(-12deg) scale(1.1)'},{transform:'translate('+dx+'px,'+dy+'px)'}],{duration:D(560),easing:'ease-in-out'}),
      a2=anim(dst,[{transform:'none',opacity:1},{offset:.5,opacity:.4},{transform:'translate('+(-dx)+'px,'+(-dy)+'px)',opacity:1}],{duration:D(560),easing:'ease-in-out'});
  await Promise.all([done(a1),done(a2)]);
  if(limg){var la=anim(limg,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0,transform:'scale(.6)'}],{duration:D(300),fill:'forwards'});done(la).then(function(){if(limg.parentNode)limg.parentNode.removeChild(limg);});}
  src.classList.remove('win');src.style.setProperty('--c',L.to[0]);src.style.setProperty('--r',L.to[1]);dst.style.setProperty('--c',L.from[0]);dst.style.setProperty('--r',L.from[1]);
  tiles[L.to[0]][L.to[1]]=src;tiles[L.from[0]][L.from[1]]=dst;SFX.thunk(1);var q=feltXY(src);spark(q.x,q.y,'#fdba74',12,0.8);
  await wait(D(200));
}
/* ---------- Saddle Up meter ---------- */
function meterStep(){var k=String(st.betI),v=meterVal()+1;PROG.m[k]=Math.min(v,MT.len);saveProg();renderMeter();var h=$('mh');h.classList.remove('run');void h.offsetWidth;h.classList.add('run');
  try{var q=feltXY(h);dust(q.x,q.y+6,3);if(v/MT.len>=0.85){spark(q.x,q.y,'#fde68a',6,0.6);SFX.ding(2);if(v<MT.len&&MT.len-v<=8)winMsg((MT.len-v)+' more winning cascade'+(MT.len-v===1?'':'s')+' to the Stampede!');}}catch(e){}
  return v>=MT.len;}
/* the rider crosses the finish line: the big moment before the Stampede */
async function finishLine(){var m=$('mtr');m.classList.add('fin');var h=$('mh'),q=feltXY(h);SFX.bell();SFX.neigh();spark(q.x,q.y,'#fff4c8',40,1.6);ring(q.x,q.y,'#f2d27e',CS*1.4);coins(6,'top');
  banner('FINISH LINE!','',1700);shake(6);await wait(D(1500));m.classList.remove('fin');}


var mgEl=$('mg');
function mgOpen(cls,title,sub,pk){mgEl.className='mg '+cls;$('mgT').textContent=title;$('mgS').textContent=sub;$('mgPK').textContent=pk||'Prize';$('mgP').textContent='$0.00';$('mgs').innerHTML='';$('mgf').innerHTML='';}
function mgClose(){mgEl.className='mg hide';}
function mgPrize(v){$('mgP').textContent=money(v,true);var b=$('mgP');anim(b,[{transform:'scale(1.35)',color:'#fde68a'},{transform:'none',color:'#fff'}],{duration:300});}
function autoMode(){return st.auto>0;}

/* ---------- Collector: tokens fly into their items ---------- */
async function collectTokens(list,b){
  for(var i=0;i<3;i++){PROG.c[i].sb+=b;PROG.c[i].ns++;}
  var full=[];
  for(var j=0;j<list.length;j++){var it=list[j],c=crnd(NRE),r=crnd(4),t=tiles[c][r];if(!t)continue;
    var from=feltXY(t),to=feltXY($('ci'+it).querySelector('.ring')),f=document.createElement('div');f.className='tokfly';f.style.setProperty('--cc',CCOL[it]);f.innerHTML=tokInner(it);felt.appendChild(f);
    f.style.left=from.x+'px';f.style.top=from.y+'px';SFX.ding(2);spark(from.x,from.y,CCOL[it],10,0.7);
    await done(anim(f,[{transform:'scale(0)'},{offset:.25,transform:'scale(1.5)'},{offset:.45,transform:'scale(1.2)'},{transform:'translate('+(to.x-from.x)+'px,'+(to.y-from.y)+'px) scale(.7)'}],{duration:D(900),easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.4,1)',fill:'forwards'}));
    f.parentNode.removeChild(f);PROG.c[it].n=Math.min(CL.need,PROG.c[it].n+1);var ce=$('ci'+it);ce.classList.remove('pulse');void ce.offsetWidth;ce.classList.add('pulse');spark(to.x,to.y,CCOL[it],12,0.6);SFX.coin();
    if(PROG.c[it].n>=CL.need&&full.indexOf(it)<0)full.push(it);renderColl();}
  saveProg();
  return full;
}
/* minigame outcomes: a fixed-value pot drawn by the RNG, then play moves it modestly (about -20%..+30%) */
var MGPOT=[[2,22],[3,26],[4,20],[5,14],[7,9],[10,6],[15,3]];  /* mean 4.5 x average bet */
function potDraw(){var t=0,i;for(i=0;i<MGPOT.length;i++)t+=MGPOT[i][1];var x=crnd(t);for(i=0;i<MGPOT.length;i++){x-=MGPOT[i][1];if(x<0)return MGPOT[i][0];}return 4;}
async function minigame(it,b){
  var c=PROG.c[it],avg=c.ns?c.sb/c.ns:b;avg=r2(Math.max(1,avg));
  var ce=$('ci'+it);ce.classList.add('full');SFX.fanfare(1);
  await celebrate({pre:CL.names[it]+' full',tier:CL.games[it].toUpperCase()+'!',tcls:'fs',sub:['Bottles pop up on the fence. Tap them before time runs out: every hit raises the prize.','Ride the cart into the mine and pick left or right at each fork. Gold until a cave-in.','Stay on the bronco: press when the needle hits the green. Every buck you ride raises the prize.'][it],btn:'Let\u2019s go!',autoStart:st.auto>0?1600:5000,dur:500});
  var amt=0;
  if(it===0)amt=await quickDraw(avg);else if(it===1)amt=await mineCart(avg);else amt=await bronco(avg);
  amt=r2(amt);mgClose();
  c.n=0;c.sb=0;c.ns=0;saveProg();renderColl();ce.classList.remove('full');
  SFX.fanfare(amt>=avg*8?2:1);await celebrate({pre:CL.games[it],tier:amt>=avg*8?'MEGA WIN':'WELL RIDDEN',tcls:amt>=avg*8?'mega':'fs',amt:amt,dur:1800,coins:3,sub:Math.round(amt/avg*10)/10+'\u00d7 your average bet of '+money(avg)+'.'});
  st.bank=r2(st.bank+amt);st.extra=r2((st.extra||0)+amt);hud();
  var h='<i style="color:#34d399">You</i> won <b>'+money(amt)+'</b> in '+esc(CL.games[it])+'!';feedPush(h);logLine(h,true);
  return amt;
}
var BOTTLE='<svg viewBox="0 0 40 60"><path d="M15 2h10v14c0 3 8 6 8 14v24c0 3-2 5-5 5H12c-3 0-5-2-5-5V30c0-8 8-11 8-14z" fill="rgba(34,197,94,.75)" stroke="#14532d" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="14" y="0" width="12" height="5" rx="1.5" fill="#7c2d12"/><path d="M11 32v18" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.6)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round"/><rect x="10" y="36" width="20" height="12" rx="2" fill="#fde68a" stroke="#7a5a1d"/></svg>';
async function quickDraw(avg){
  var pot=potDraw(),floor=0.8*pot*avg,per=0.4*pot*avg/8,prize=floor,hits=0,N=8,dur=6000;
  mgOpen('qd','QUICK DRAW','Tap the bottles before they drop. Every hit adds '+money(per,true)+'.','Prize');mgPrize(prize);
  $('mgs').innerHTML='<div class="fence"></div>';$('mgf').innerHTML='<span id="qdl">Draw!</span><div class="bar"><i id="qdb"></i></div>';
  var stg=$('mgs'),W=stg.clientWidth,H=stg.clientHeight,tw=Math.round(clamp(Math.min(W,H)*0.16,36,70));
  await wait(500);
  var t0=vt,endP=new Promise(function(res){tween(dur,function(p){$('qdb').style.transform='scaleX('+(1-p)+')';}).then(res);});
  for(var i=0;i<N;i++){(function(i){after(i*(dur-900)/N+Math.random()*200,function(){
    var e=document.createElement('div');e.className='bottle';e.style.setProperty('--tw',tw+'px');e.innerHTML=A('mg_bottle')?imgTag('mg_bottle'):BOTTLE;e.style.left=(tw+Math.random()*(W-2*tw))+'px';e.style.top=(tw*0.9+Math.random()*(H*0.62-tw))+'px';stg.appendChild(e);
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){e.classList.add('on');});var hit=false;
    function shoot(ev){if(ev)ev.stopPropagation();if(hit)return;hit=true;hits++;prize=r2(prize+per);mgPrize(prize);SFX.gun();var q=feltXY(e);spark(q.x,q.y,'#bbf7d0',16,1.1);ring(q.x,q.y,'#fde68a',tw);
      var f=document.createElement('div');f.className='qdhit';f.textContent='+'+money(per,true);f.style.left=e.style.left;f.style.top=e.style.top;stg.appendChild(f);setTimeout(function(){if(f.parentNode)f.parentNode.removeChild(f);},900);var bim=e.querySelector('img');if(bim&&A('mg_bottle_break')){bim.src=A('mg_bottle_break');after(260,function(){e.classList.add('gone');});}else e.classList.add('gone');}
    e.addEventListener('pointerdown',shoot);
    if(autoMode()&&Math.random()<0.5)after(450+Math.random()*300,shoot);
    after(1100+Math.random()*300,function(){if(!hit){e.classList.add('gone');}after(200,function(){if(e.parentNode)e.parentNode.removeChild(e);});});
  });})(i);}
  await endP;$('qdl').textContent=hits+' of '+N+' bottles';await wait(900);
  return prize;
}
var CART='<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80"><path d="M10 14h100l-12 44H22z" fill="#78350f" stroke="#3b1d0b" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M14 26h92M18 40h84" stroke="#a16207" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M20 10c8-8 18-6 24 2 6-9 18-10 26-2 8-7 20-6 26 4" fill="#f2d27e" stroke="#7a5a1d" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="36" cy="64" r="11" fill="#334155" stroke="#0f172a" stroke-width="4"/><circle cx="86" cy="64" r="11" fill="#334155" stroke="#0f172a" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M0 76h120" stroke="#475569" stroke-width="4"/></svg>';
var GOLDV=[[0.5,35],[1,35],[1.5,15],[2,10],[4,5]];
function goldDraw(){var t=100,x=crnd(t);for(var i=0;i<GOLDV.length;i++){x-=GOLDV[i][1];if(x<0)return GOLDV[i][0];}return 1;}
async function mineCart(avg){
  var total=0,stretch=0,trail=[];
  mgOpen('mine','MINE CART RUN','Left or right? Every stretch of track holds gold until the mine caves in.','Gold');
  $('mgs').innerHTML='<div class="tun"><button type="button" data-s="0">LEFT</button><button type="button" data-s="1">RIGHT</button></div><div class="cart">'+(A('mg_cart')?imgTag('mg_cart'):CART)+'</div><div class="trail" id="trail"></div>';
  var bs=[].slice.call($('mgs').querySelectorAll('.tun button')),cart=$('mgs').querySelector('.cart');var ms=$('mgs');ms.classList.toggle('fit',ms.clientWidth/Math.max(1,ms.clientHeight)>=1.3);
  while(true){
    stretch++;$('mgf').textContent=autoMode()?'Autoplay is steering\u2026':'Stretch '+stretch+': pick a tunnel (or use \u2190 \u2192).';
    bs.forEach(function(x){x.disabled=false;var rv=x.querySelector('.rv');if(rv)rv.parentNode.removeChild(rv);x.firstChild.textContent=x.getAttribute('data-s')==='0'?'LEFT':'RIGHT';});
    var choice=await new Promise(function(res){var idle=after(autoMode()?D(900):12000,function(){fin(crnd(2));});
      function fin(s){cancelT(idle);removeEventListener('keydown',kd,true);res(s);}
      function kd(e){if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();fin(0);}else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();fin(1);}}
      addEventListener('keydown',kd,true);bs.forEach(function(x){x.onclick=function(ev){ev.stopPropagation();fin(+x.getAttribute('data-s'));};});});
    bs.forEach(function(x){x.disabled=true;});
    /* each tunnel's contents are drawn independently: picking a side never changes the odds */
    var cave=stretch>1&&crnd(100)<25,val=cave?0:goldDraw(),other=crnd(100)<25?0:goldDraw();
    SFX.rumble();await done(anim(cart,[{transform:'none'},{transform:'translateX('+(choice?60:-60)+'%) translateY(-30%) scale(.55)',opacity:.2}],{duration:D(700),easing:'ease-in'}));
    var mine=bs[choice],oth=bs[1-choice],rv=document.createElement('span');rv.className='rv'+(cave?' cave':'');rv.textContent=cave?'CAVE-IN!':'+'+money(val*avg,true);mine.appendChild(rv);mine.firstChild.textContent='';oth.firstChild.textContent='';
    var ro=document.createElement('span');ro.className='rv dim';ro.textContent=other?'had '+money(other*avg,true):'had a cave-in';oth.appendChild(ro);
    var q=feltXY(mine);
    if(cave){shake(9);rocks(q.x,q.y-20,18);dust(q.x,q.y,10);SFX.rumble();trail.push('<i class="cave">cave-in</i>');}
    else{total=r2(total+val*avg);mgPrize(total);spark(q.x,q.y,'#f2d27e',20,1.2);SFX.chime(Math.min(stretch,8));trail.push('<i>+'+money(val*avg,true)+'</i>');}
    $('trail').innerHTML=trail.join('');
    await wait(D(1100));anim(cart,[{transform:'translateY(40%)',opacity:0},{transform:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:D(350)});
    if(cave)break;
    if(stretch>=30)break;
  }
  $('mgf').textContent='The tunnel caved in after '+stretch+' stretches.';await wait(900);
  return total;
}
async function bronco(avg){
  var pot=potDraw(),N=6,ridden=0,prize=0.7*pot*avg,step=0.6*pot*avg/N;
  mgOpen('bronco','BRONCO RIDE','Press when the needle is in the green. Ride all '+N+' bucks to tame it.','Prize');mgPrize(prize);
  $('mgs').innerHTML='<div class="bron'+(A('mg_bronco')?' ab':'')+'" id="bron"></div><div class="mgbig" id="bbig"></div><div class="tbar" id="tbar"><div class="zone" id="zone"></div><div class="ndl" id="ndl"></div></div>';
  $('mgf').innerHTML='<button type="button" class="btn gold" id="holdBtn">HOLD ON! <kbd>Space</kbd></button>';
  await wait(600);
  for(var k=0;k<N;k++){
    var zw=Math.max(16,28-k*2.2),zx=10+Math.random()*(80-zw),speed=1300-k*120;$('zone').style.left=zx+'%';$('zone').style.width=zw+'%';$('bbig').textContent='Buck '+(k+1)+' of '+N;
    var ok=await new Promise(function(res){var pos=0,dir=1,fin=false,last=vt,tk=null;
      function step(){if(fin)return;var dt=vt-last;last=vt;pos+=dir*dt/speed*100;if(pos>=100){pos=100;dir=-1;}if(pos<=0){pos=0;dir=1;}$('ndl').style.left=pos+'%';tk=after(16,step);}
      function press(){if(fin)return;fin=true;if(tk)cancelT(tk);cleanup();res(pos>=zx-1.5&&pos<=zx+zw+1.5);}
      function kd(e){if(e.key===' '||e.key==='Spacebar'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();press();}}
      function cleanup(){removeEventListener('keydown',kd,true);$('holdBtn').onclick=null;}
      addEventListener('keydown',kd,true);$('holdBtn').onclick=function(e){e.stopPropagation();press();};
      step();
      var timeout=after(autoMode()?D(700+Math.random()*500):9000,function(){if(fin)return;if(autoMode()&&Math.random()<0.8)pos=zx+zw/2;press();});
    });
    var bar=$('tbar');bar.classList.remove('ok','miss');void bar.offsetWidth;bar.classList.add(ok?'ok':'miss');
    var br=$('bron');br.classList.remove('buck');void br.offsetWidth;
    if(ok){ridden++;prize=r2(prize+step);mgPrize(prize);br.classList.add('buck');SFX.neigh();SFX.thunk(0);$('bbig').textContent=pick(['Yee-haw!','Hang on!','Still riding!','Easy now!']);var q=feltXY(br);dust(q.x,q.y+40,8);await wait(D(700));}
    else{br.classList.add('thrown');SFX.rumble();$('bbig').textContent='THROWN!';shake(6);await wait(D(1200));break;}
  }
  if(ridden>=N){$('bbig').textContent='BRONCO TAMED!';SFX.fanfare(1);await wait(1000);}
  return prize;
}


/* =====================================================================
   Custom art. Paste the hosted URLs into ART; anything left empty (or that

try{if(typeof window.__SLOT_ART_LOCAL==='string'&&/^(https?:|file:)/i.test(window.__SLOT_ART_LOCAL))ART_LOCAL=window.__SLOT_ART_LOCAL;}catch(e){}
function art(k){return ART[k]||(ART_LOCAL?ART_LOCAL+k+'.webp':'');}
function cssUrl(u){return (typeof u==='string'&&/^(https?:|file:|\/\/)/i.test(u))?u.replace(/["'()\\<>\s]/g,encodeURIComponent):'';}
var ARTOK={};            /* key -> url, only for images that actually loaded */
function A(k){return (ARTOK&&ARTOK[k])||'';}
function AU(k){var u=A(k);return u?'url("'+u+'")':'';}

function imgTag(k,cls){var u=A(k);return u?'<img'+(cls?' class="'+cls+'"':'')+' src="'+u+'" alt="" draggable="false" onerror="__slotArtFail(this,\''+k+'\')">':'';}
var artPending=0;
function artPreload(){
  var keys=Object.keys(ART);artPending=keys.length;
  keys.forEach(function(k){var u=cssUrl(art(k));if(!u){artDone();return;}var im=new Image();im.decoding='async';
    im.onload=function(){ARTOK[k]=u;artDone();};im.onerror=function(){artDone();};im.src=u;});
}
function artDone(){if(--artPending===0)artApply();}
function artApply(){
  felt.classList.toggle('art',Object.keys(ARTOK).length>0);
  ['bg_casino','cabinet','wanted_blank','meter_horse','meter_flag','jp_grand','bg_quickdraw','bg_mine','bg_rodeo'].forEach(function(k){felt.classList.toggle('a-'+k,!!A(k));});
  if(A('bg_casino'))felt.style.setProperty('--bgc',AU('bg_casino'));
  ['sym_wild','bg_quickdraw','bg_mine','bg_rodeo','mg_bronco','meter_horse','meter_flag','wanted_blank','wanted_collected','wanted_burning_collected','bullethole'].forEach(function(k){if(A(k))felt.style.setProperty('--'+k.replace(/_/g,'-'),AU(k));});
  for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var jk=['jp_mini','jp_minor','jp_major','jp_grand'][i],el=$('jpw'+i);if(A(jk)){el.classList.add('coin');el.style.setProperty('--jpi',AU(jk));}}
  renderCollIcons();renderFeatures();
  if(!st.busy){var g=gridLetters().map(function(col){return col.split('').map(function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);});});if(g.length===NRE&&g.every(function(c){return c.length===4&&c.every(function(v){return v>=0;});}))placeGrid(g);}
  try{renderPaytable();closePtab();}catch(e){}
  relayout(true);
}
function renderCollIcons(){for(var i=0;i<3;i++){var k=['col_badge','col_lantern','col_boot'][i],ring=$('ci'+i).querySelector('.ring');var old=ring.querySelector('svg,img');if(old)old.parentNode.removeChild(old);
  ring.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',A(k)?imgTag(k,'cimgi'):CICON[i]);}}

/* the cabinet: full 9-slice cabinet in the page, just the gold reel frame in full screen */

/* per-skin cabinet art: its size, the reel window, the gold frame around it, the 9-slice borders and where the title and meter go */
var SKIN_CAB={neon:{W:615,H:1100,win:[132,306,462,790],frame:[92,262,503,842],slice:[306,153,310,132],stretch:1.6,word:[150,40,465,232],mtr:[150,880,465]},

var cabMode='';
function sizeCab(){
  var rw=$('rw'),G=Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07)),R=5*CS+4*G,RH=4*CS+3*G,fs=root.classList.contains('fs'),mtr=$('mtr'),cw=$('cabword');
  var mode=!A('cabinet')?'':(!fs&&LAY==='wide'&&CABFULL?'full':'frame');
  if(mode!==cabMode){felt.classList.toggle('cabfull',mode==='full');felt.classList.toggle('cabframe',mode==='frame');cabMode=mode;}
  rw.removeAttribute('style');mtr.removeAttribute('style');cw.removeAttribute('style');
  if(!mode)return;
  var u=AU('cabinet');
  if(mode==='frame'){
    var s=R/(CAB.win[2]-CAB.win[0]),sv=RH/(CAB.win[3]-CAB.win[1]),f=CAB.frame,w=CAB.win;
    rw.style.cssText='background-image:'+u+';background-repeat:no-repeat;background-size:'+(CAB.W*s).toFixed(1)+'px '+(CAB.H*sv).toFixed(1)+'px;background-position:'+(-f[0]*s).toFixed(1)+'px '+(-f[1]*sv).toFixed(1)+'px;'+
      'padding:'+((w[1]-f[1])*sv).toFixed(1)+'px '+((f[2]-w[2])*s).toFixed(1)+'px '+((f[3]-w[3])*sv).toFixed(1)+'px '+((w[0]-f[0])*s).toFixed(1)+'px;';
    mtr.style.cssText='left:'+((w[0]-f[0])*s).toFixed(1)+'px;width:'+R+'px;';
    return;
  }
  /* full cabinet: borders keep the art's proportions, the middle stretches around the reels */
  var sl=CAB.slice,sx=R/(CAB.win[2]-CAB.win[0]),k=sx/CAB.stretch,cW=CAB.W-sl[1]-sl[3],cH=CAB.H-sl[0]-sl[2];
  var sy=RH/(CAB.win[3]-CAB.win[1]);
  var pl=(CAB.win[0]-sl[3])*sx,pr=(CAB.W-sl[1]-CAB.win[2])*sx,pt=(CAB.win[1]-sl[0])*sy,pb=(CAB.H-sl[2]-CAB.win[3])*sy;
  var bt=sl[0]*k,br=sl[1]*k,bb=sl[2]*k,bl=sl[3]*k;
  rw.style.cssText='border-style:solid;border-color:transparent;border-width:'+bt.toFixed(1)+'px '+br.toFixed(1)+'px '+bb.toFixed(1)+'px '+bl.toFixed(1)+'px;'+
    'border-image-source:'+u+';border-image-slice:'+sl.join(' ')+' fill;border-image-repeat:stretch;padding:'+pt.toFixed(1)+'px '+pr.toFixed(1)+'px '+pb.toFixed(1)+'px '+pl.toFixed(1)+'px;';
  /* map a point of the original art to this box (relative to the padding box, where absolute children live) */
  var cx=(pl+R+pr)/cW,cy=(pt+RH+pb)/cH;
  function X(x){return x<=sl[3]?x*k-bl:x>=CAB.W-sl[1]?(pl+R+pr)+(x-(CAB.W-sl[1]))*k:(x-sl[3])*cx;}
  function Y(y){return y<=sl[0]?y*k-bt:y>=CAB.H-sl[2]?(pt+RH+pb)+(y-(CAB.H-sl[2]))*k:(y-sl[0])*cy;}
  var WB=CAB.word||[178,92,440,196],MB=CAB.mtr||[200,746,418];
  cw.style.cssText='left:'+X(WB[0]).toFixed(1)+'px;top:'+Y(WB[1]).toFixed(1)+'px;width:'+(X(WB[2])-X(WB[0])).toFixed(1)+'px;height:'+(Y(WB[3])-Y(WB[1])).toFixed(1)+'px';
  mtr.style.cssText='left:'+X(MB[0]).toFixed(1)+'px;top:'+Y(MB[1]).toFixed(1)+'px;width:'+(X(MB[2])-X(MB[0])).toFixed(1)+'px;';
}

function vfmt(x){var v=x*bet();if(st.nb)v=x*st.nb.b;return v>=1000?'$'+(Math.round(v/100)/10)+'K':v>=100||Math.abs(v-Math.round(v))<0.005?'$'+Math.round(v):'$'+(Math.round(v*100)/100).toFixed(2).replace(/0$/,'');}

function flyTo(fromEl,toEl,html,cls,ms,jc){if(!fromEl||!toEl)return Promise.resolve();var a=feltXY(fromEl),b=feltXY(toEl),f=document.createElement('div');f.className='nfly'+(cls?' '+cls:'');if(jc)f.style.setProperty('--jc',jc);f.innerHTML=html;
  f.style.left=a.x+'px';f.style.top=a.y+'px';felt.appendChild(f);
  if(!opt.fx){f.remove();return Promise.resolve();}
  return done(anim(f,[{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.5)',opacity:0},{offset:.2,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.25)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate(calc(-50% + '+(b.x-a.x).toFixed(0)+'px),calc(-50% + '+(b.y-a.y).toFixed(0)+'px)) scale(.75)',opacity:.85}],
    {duration:D(ms||620),easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.4,1)',fill:'forwards'})).then(function(){if(f.parentNode)f.parentNode.removeChild(f);});}

  st.stats.jps++;renderJP();feedMine('the '+JP.label[t]+' Jackpot for '+money(amt),99);shareWin(amt,'jp',t);return amt;}
async function labelJackpot(t,b){

  st.bank=r2(st.bank+amt);st.extra=r2((st.extra||0)+amt);hud();return amt;}

var NB=null; /* {kind,el,claims:[{el,cells:[16],slots:[16]}],nc} */
function sizeBoard(){
  if(!NB)return;layoutZoom();var G=Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07)),BW=5*CS+4*G,BH=4*CS+3*G,fz=NB.kind==='frenzy';
  if(NB.BW){var g0=Math.max(3,Math.round(NB.BW*0.014));G=g0;BW=NB.BW;BH=NB.BH;NB.el.style.width=BW+'px';NB.el.style.height=BH+'px';}
  var framed=fz&&!!FZFRAME.claimFrame,gap=fz?(framed?Math.max(4,Math.round(G*0.8)):Math.max(8,Math.round(G*2.2))):0,Qw=fz?(BW-gap)/2:BW,Qh=fz?(BH-gap)/2:BH,g2=fz?Math.max(2,Math.round(G*0.55)):G;
  /* framed claims: the frame keeps its natural width scale (thinner on small screens), the 5x4 grid sits centred in its window */
  var bs=framed?(Qw/FZFRAME.frameW)*(Qw<200?0.62:Qw<300?0.8:1):0,W=FZFRAME.win,sl=FZFRAME.slice;
  var wx=W[0]*bs,wy=W[1]*bs,ww=Qw-(W[0]+W[2])*bs,wh=Qh-(W[1]+W[3])*bs;if(!framed){wx=0;wy=0;ww=Qw;wh=Qh;}
  var cs2=Math.min((ww-4*g2)/5,(wh-3*g2)/4),gw=5*cs2+4*g2,gh=4*cs2+3*g2,ox=wx+(ww-gw)/2,oy=wy+(wh-gh)/2;
  NB.claims.forEach(function(o,i){var x=fz?(i%2)*(Qw+gap):0,y=fz?Math.floor(i/2)*(Qh+gap):0;
    o.el.style.cssText='left:'+x.toFixed(1)+'px;top:'+y.toFixed(1)+'px;width:'+Qw.toFixed(1)+'px;height:'+Qh.toFixed(1)+'px;--cs:'+cs2.toFixed(2)+'px;--g:'+g2+'px;--pitch:'+(cs2+g2).toFixed(2)+'px;'+
      '--ox:'+ox.toFixed(1)+'px;--oy:'+oy.toFixed(1)+'px;--gw:'+gw.toFixed(1)+'px;--gh:'+gh.toFixed(1)+'px;--wx:'+wx.toFixed(1)+'px;--wy:'+wy.toFixed(1)+'px;--ww:'+ww.toFixed(1)+'px;--wh:'+wh.toFixed(1)+'px;--fb:'+bs.toFixed(4)+';--sy:'+(FZFRAME.signY*bs).toFixed(1)+'px';
    var fr=o.el.querySelector('.cfr');if(fr)fr.style.cssText='border-width:'+sl.map(function(v){return (v*bs).toFixed(1)+'px';}).join(' ');});
}
function cellEl(cell,c,r){var k=SK[cell.k],el=makeSym(cell.k,c,r);if(cell.v!==undefined){var em=document.createElement('em');em.className='nv';em.textContent=vfmt(cell.v);el.appendChild(em);}

  el.__cell=cell;return el;}
