  /* zoom in: the board grows out of the reel window */
  var a=reelsEl.getBoundingClientRect(),b=box.getBoundingClientRect(),sc=a.width/Math.max(1,b.width);
  anim(z,[{backgroundColor:'rgba(6,4,2,0)',backdropFilter:'blur(0px)'},{}],{duration:D(520),easing:'ease-out'});
  anim(z.querySelector('.zhud'),[{opacity:0,transform:'translateY(-10px)'},{opacity:1,transform:'none'}],{duration:D(520),delay:D(200),fill:'backwards'});
  await done(anim(box,[{transform:'translate('+(a.left+a.width/2-b.left-b.width/2).toFixed(0)+'px,'+(a.top+a.height/2-b.top-b.height/2).toFixed(0)+'px) scale('+sc.toFixed(3)+')'},{transform:'none'}],{duration:D(opt.fx?650:200),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)'}));
}
/* immersive takeover: HUD along the top on portrait screens, down the sides on wide ones; the board as big as fits */
function layoutZoom(){if(!NB||!NB.z)return;var z=NB.z,W=z.clientWidth,H=z.clientHeight,wide=W>H*1.15;z.classList.toggle('zw',wide);z.classList.toggle('zn',!wide);
  var stg=$('zstage');if(!stg)return;var G=Math.max(3,Math.round(CS*0.07)),A=(5*CS+4*G)/(4*CS+3*G),bw=Math.floor(Math.min(stg.clientWidth-6,(stg.clientHeight-6)*A));NB.BW=Math.max(200,bw);NB.BH=Math.round(NB.BW/A);}
async function zoomOut(){if(!NB||!NB.z)return;var z=NB.z,box=NB.el,a=reelsEl.getBoundingClientRect(),b=box.getBoundingClientRect(),sc=a.width/Math.max(1,b.width);
  anim(z.querySelector('.zhud'),[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],{duration:D(300),fill:'forwards'});
  var p1=anim(box,[{transform:'none'},{transform:'translate('+(a.left+a.width/2-b.left-b.width/2).toFixed(0)+'px,'+(a.top+a.height/2-b.top-b.height/2).toFixed(0)+'px) scale('+sc.toFixed(3)+')',opacity:.2}],{duration:D(opt.fx?560:200),easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.6,1)',fill:'forwards'});
  anim(z,[{},{backgroundColor:'rgba(6,4,2,0)',backdropFilter:'blur(0px)'}],{duration:D(560),fill:'forwards'});await done(p1);}
function closeBoard(){if(NB&&NB.z&&NB.z.parentNode)NB.z.parentNode.removeChild(NB.z);if(nbEl&&nbEl.parentNode)nbEl.parentNode.removeChild(nbEl);nbEl=null;NB=null;felt.classList.remove('nbon','nbzoom');for(var t=0;t<4;t++){$('jpw'+t).classList.remove('jfull');}renderPips([0,0,0,0]);}
function renderPips(jpm){for(var t=0;t<4;t++){var e=$('jpm'+t);if(!e)continue;[].forEach.call(e.children,function(p,i){p.classList.toggle('on',i<jpm[t]);});$('jpw'+t).classList.toggle('jfull',jpm[t]>=3);var zj=$('zj'+t);if(zj){[].forEach.call(zj.querySelectorAll('.zp i'),function(p,i){p.classList.toggle('on',i<jpm[t]);});zj.classList.toggle('jfull',jpm[t]>=3);}}}
function renderNB(){var n=st.nb;if(!n)return;var fb=$('fsbar');fb.classList.remove('hide');fb.classList.add('nbar');
  var dots='';for(var i=0;i<NG.spins;i++)dots+=i<n.left?'\u25cf':'\u25cb';
  fb.innerHTML=(n.kind==='frenzy'?'Gold Frenzy':'Nugget Respins')+' <span class="dots">'+dots+'</span>'+(n.mult>1?' \u00b7 Meter \u00d7'+n.mult:'');
  renderPips(n.jpm);if($('zCnt'))$('zCnt').innerHTML=dots+(n.mult>1?' <em>Meter \u00d7'+n.mult+'</em>':'');}
function meterShow(){var n=st.nb;winAmt(r2(n.meter*n.b),n.mult>1?'Prize meter \u00d7'+n.mult:'Prize meter');var bar=$('winbar');bar.classList.remove('pop');void bar.offsetWidth;bar.classList.add('pop');
  var zm=$('zMeter');if(zm){zm.textContent=money(r2(n.meter*n.b),true);$('zMk').textContent=n.mult>1?'Prize meter \u00d7'+n.mult:'Prize meter';zm.classList.remove('pop');void zm.offsetWidth;zm.classList.add('pop');}}
function meterTgt(){return $('zMeter')||$('winV');}
function jpTgt(t){return $('zj'+t)||$('jpw'+t);}
async function blastIntro(){ /* dynamite on the trigger screen blows open into a nugget */
  var bl=[];NB.claims[0].cells.forEach(function(e){if(e&&e.__cell&&e.__cell.blast)bl.push(e);});
  for(var i=0;i<bl.length;i++){var e=bl[i],q=feltXY(e),c=e.style.getPropertyValue('--c'),r=e.style.getPropertyValue('--r');
    var tnt=makeSym(e.__cell.from===ENG.S?ENG.S:ENG.DY,+c,+r);e.parentNode.appendChild(tnt);e.style.opacity='0';SFX.boom();shake(7);spark(q.x,q.y,'#f97316',26,1.4);ring(q.x,q.y,'#fde047',CS*1.2);rocks(q.x,q.y,10);dust(q.x,q.y,8);
    await done(anim(tnt,[{transform:'scale(1)',filter:'brightness(1)'},{transform:'scale(1.35)',filter:'brightness(3)',opacity:1},{transform:'scale(.2)',opacity:0}],{duration:D(420),fill:'forwards'}));tnt.remove();
    e.style.opacity='';anim(e,[{transform:'scale(.3) rotate(-20deg)',filter:'brightness(3)'},{transform:'none',filter:'none'}],{duration:D(380),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.4)'});await wait(D(200));}
}
async function trainCross(ci){
  var fl=$('flyers'),cl=NB.claims[ci].el,y=feltXY(cl).y,W0=felt.clientWidth,tw=Math.round(clamp(CS*2.6,130,300)),t=document.createElement('div');t.className='train'+(FZART.loco?' art':'');t.style.setProperty('--tw',tw+'px');t.innerHTML=fzImg('loco')||LOCOSVG;
  t.style.top=(y-tw*0.3)+'px';fl.appendChild(t);SFX.whistle();SFX.chug(1.8);
  var x0=FZART.loco?W0+tw*0.2:-tw*1.2,x1=FZART.loco?-tw*1.2:W0+tw*0.2;
  var dur=D(opt.fx?1700:600),t0=vt,a=anim(t,[{transform:'translateX('+x0+'px)'},{transform:'translateX('+x1+'px)'}],{duration:dur,easing:'cubic-bezier(.4,.1,.6,.9)',fill:'both'});
  if(opt.fx)(function puff(){var p=(vt-t0)/dur;if(p>=1)return;var x=x0+(x1-x0)*p+tw*(FZART.loco?0.45:0.3),pf=document.createElement('div');pf.className='puff';pf.style.left=x+'px';pf.style.top=(y-tw*0.32)+'px';fl.appendChild(pf);
    done(anim(pf,[{transform:'scale(.4)',opacity:.9},{transform:'translate(-30px,-40px) scale(2.4)',opacity:0}],{duration:900,fill:'forwards'})).then(function(){pf.remove();});after(90,puff);})();
  for(var k=0;k<3;k++)after(dur*(0.2+k*0.25),function(){shake(3);});
  await done(a);t.remove();
}
/* the crate bursts open: chain and padlock drop, the planks fly off with splinters */
async function burstCrate(lk,ce){
  SFX.boom();SFX.clang();shake(6);var q=feltXY(lk);spark(q.x,q.y,'#f2d27e',30,1.4);ring(q.x,q.y,'#fff4c8',q.w*0.6);rocks(q.x,q.y,16);dust(q.x,q.y,10);
  var ps=[],parts=[].slice.call(lk.querySelectorAll('.pl,.brace,.ps'));lk.classList.add('bursting');
  parts.forEach(function(e,i){var dx=(Math.random()<0.5?-1:1)*(60+Math.random()*90),dy=-40-Math.random()*90,rot=(Math.random()-0.5)*120;
    ps.push(done(anim(e,[{transform:getComputedStyle(e).transform==='none'?'none':getComputedStyle(e).transform,opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+dx+'%,'+dy+'%) rotate('+rot+'deg) scale(.8)',opacity:0}],{duration:D(560+i*40),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.6,.4,1)',fill:'forwards'})));});
  [].forEach.call(lk.querySelectorAll('.chain,.padlock'),function(e){ps.push(done(anim(e,[{transform:'none',opacity:1},{transform:'translateY(140%) rotate(25deg)',opacity:0}],{duration:D(520),easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.9,.5)',fill:'forwards'})));});
  ps.push(done(anim(lk,[{backgroundColor:'rgba(20,12,4,.9)'},{backgroundColor:'rgba(20,12,4,0)'}],{duration:D(500),fill:'forwards'})));
  await Promise.all(ps);lk.remove();
}
async function unlockClaim(u){
  var o=NB.claims[u.cl],ce=o.el,lk=ce.querySelector('.lk'),tc=trainCross(u.cl);
  await wait(D(opt.fx?760:260));
  if(lk)await burstCrate(lk,ce);
  await tc;
  ce.classList.add('open');banner('CLAIM '+(u.cl+1)+' OPEN!','nug',1500);SFX.fanfare(1);
  anim(ce,[{filter:'brightness(2.2)'},{filter:'none'}],{duration:D(900)});
  for(var i=0;i<u.drops.length;i++){var d=u.drops[i],e=cellEl(d.cell,d.idx>>2,d.idx&3);ce.appendChild(e);o.cells[d.idx]=e;
    anim(e,[{transform:'translateY(-120%) scale(.6)',opacity:0},{transform:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:D(380),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.4)'});SFX.ding(2+i);
    st.nb.meter=r2(st.nb.meter+d.cell.v);flyTo(e,meterTgt(),vfmt(d.cell.v),null,620).then(meterShow);await wait(D(240));}
  await wait(D(500));
}
async function playStep(sp){
  var n=st.nb,fz=n.kind==='frenzy';
  /* spin the empty cells of the open, unfilled claims; the last respin gets a beat of anticipation */
  var last=n.left===1,rolling=[];NB.claims.forEach(function(o,i){if(!o.el.classList.contains('open')&&fz)return;if(o.el.classList.contains('full'))return;o.slots.forEach(function(sl,x){if(!o.cells[x]){sl.classList.add('roll');rolling.push(sl);}});});
  if(last){banner('LAST RESPIN!','small nug',1400);var zc=$('zCnt');if(zc)zc.parentNode.classList.add('last');SFX.anticOn();}
  n.left=Math.max(0,n.left-1);renderNB();if(!last)SFX.whirOn();
  await wait(D(last?1500:950));
  /* the reels stop one by one, left to right; what lands on a reel locks in as it stops */
  var flies=[];
  for(var col=0;col<NRE;col++){
    rolling.forEach(function(sl){if(+sl.style.getPropertyValue('--c')===col)sl.classList.remove('roll');});
    var colLands=sp.lands.filter(function(l){return (l.idx>>2)===col;});
    for(var i=0;i<colLands.length;i++){var l=colLands[i],o=NB.claims[l.cl],e=cellEl(l.cell,l.idx>>2,l.idx&3);o.el.appendChild(e);o.cells[l.idx]=e;
      anim(e,[{transform:'scale(1.5)',filter:'brightness(2.5)',opacity:0},{transform:'none',filter:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:D(340),easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.3)'});
      var q=feltXY(e);spark(q.x,q.y,SYMCOL[SK[l.cell.k]]||'#f2d27e',12,0.9);SFX.ding(2+Math.min(i,3));
      (function(l,e){if(l.cell.v!==undefined){flies.push(wait(D(650)).then(function(){return flyTo(e,meterTgt(),vfmt(l.cell.v),null,700);}).then(function(){n.meter=r2(n.meter+l.cell.v);meterShow();SFX.coin();}));}
        else if(l.cell.t!==undefined){var t=l.cell.t;flies.push(wait(D(650)).then(function(){return flyTo(e,jpTgt(t),JP.label[t].toUpperCase(),'jp',800,JTC[t]);}).then(function(){n.jpm[t]=Math.min(3,n.jpm[t]+1);renderPips(n.jpm);SFX.ding(n.jpm[t]+1);}));}})(l,e);}
    SFX.thunk(col);await wait(D(last?260:170));}
  SFX.whirOff();SFX.anticOff();rolling.forEach(function(sl){sl.classList.remove('roll');});
  var zc2=$('zCnt');if(zc2)zc2.parentNode.classList.remove('last');
  await Promise.all(flies);if(sp.lands.length)await wait(D(750));
  /* 3-4: a strongbox wakes every bandit in its claim, each bandit collects its neighbours' cash */
  if(sp.collects.length){var boxes=sp.lands.filter(function(l){return l.cell.k===ENG.BX;});
    boxes.forEach(function(l){var e=NB.claims[l.cl].cells[l.idx];if(e){e.classList.add('nhot');after(1400,function(){e.classList.remove('nhot');});}});
    banner('BANDITS!','small nug',1200);SFX.gun();await wait(D(500));
    for(var k=0;k<sp.collects.length;k++){var cc=sp.collects[k],ob=NB.claims[cc.cl],be=ob.cells[cc.idx];if(!be)continue;
      be.classList.add('act');SFX.gun();var bq=feltXY(be);spark(bq.x,bq.y,'#ef4444',14,1);
      await Promise.all(cc.from.map(function(x){return flyTo(ob.cells[x],be,'',null,420);}));
      var tag=be.querySelectorAll('.nv');tag=tag[tag.length-1];be.__cell.got=r2((be.__cell.got||0)+cc.amt);if(tag){tag.style.display='';tag.textContent=vfmt(be.__cell.got);}
      if(cc.amt>0){await flyTo(be,meterTgt(),'+'+vfmt(cc.amt),'red',600);n.meter=r2(n.meter+cc.amt);meterShow();SFX.coin();SFX.chime(3);}
      await wait(D(160));}}
  /* jackpot meters that filled this spin */
  for(var j=0;j<sp.jp.length;j++){var tj=sp.jp[j];banner(JP.label[tj].toUpperCase()+' FILLED!','nug',1400);SFX.bell();var je=jpTgt(tj);je.classList.remove('hit');void je.offsetWidth;je.classList.add('hit');await wait(D(900));}
  /* locomotives: open the next claim, or raise the meter multiplier */
  for(var m=0;m<sp.locos.length;m++){var lo=sp.locos[m],ol=NB.claims[lo.cl],le=makeSym(ENG.LO,lo.idx>>2,lo.idx&3);ol.el.appendChild(le);SFX.whistle();
    await done(anim(le,[{transform:'scale(.3)',opacity:0},{offset:.3,transform:'scale(1.2)',opacity:1},{offset:.8,transform:'scale(1)',opacity:1},{transform:'translateX(60%)',opacity:0}],{duration:D(900)}));le.remove();
    var u=sp.unlocks[m];if(u)await unlockClaim(u);
    else{n.mult++;renderNB();meterShow();banner('METER \u00d7'+n.mult+'!','nug',1500);SFX.fanfare(1);shake(5);await wait(D(1000));}}
  if(sp.multUp&&!sp.locos.length){n.mult=sp.mult;renderNB();}
  /* full claim / full grid */
  for(var f=0;f<sp.full.length;f++){var fc=NB.claims[sp.full[f]].el;fc.classList.add('full');SFX.fanfare(2);shake(8);var fq=feltXY(fc);spark(fq.x,fq.y,'#fff4c8',40,1.6);
    banner(fz?'CLAIM FULL! +'+NG.claimFull+'\u00d7':'MOTHER LODE!','nug',1800);await wait(D(1400));}
  /* sync with the engine (meter multiplier, counter) */
  n.meter=sp.meter;n.mult=sp.mult;n.jpm=sp.jpm.slice();
  var reset=sp.left===NG.spins&&(sp.lands.length||sp.locos.length);n.left=sp.left;renderNB();meterShow();
  if(reset){var zcp=$('zCnt');if(zcp){zcp.parentNode.classList.remove('pop');void zcp.offsetWidth;zcp.parentNode.classList.add('pop');SFX.chime(5);}var fb=$('fsbar');anim(fb,[{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.35)',filter:'brightness(1.6)'},{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%)',filter:'none'}],{duration:D(380)});}
  await wait(D(sp.lands.length?500:450));
}
/* end of bonus: count up the prize meter, then each jackpot, then the total, with a pause between */
function tally(o){
  return new Promise(function(res){
    var fast=false,ended=false;celebEl.className='celeb on fs';$('cImg').classList.add('hide');$('cPre').textContent=o.pre;$('cBtn').classList.add('hide');$('cTap').textContent='Tap to skip';$('cAmt').style.display='';$('cSub').innerHTML='';
    var tt=$('cTier');st.celeb={skip:function(){if(!fast){fast=true;return;}close();}};celebEl.onclick=function(e){e.stopPropagation();st.celeb&&st.celeb.skip();};
    function close(){if(ended)return;ended=true;celebEl.className='celeb fs';st.celeb=null;after(330,res);}
    function setTier(t,cls){tt.className='ctier '+(cls||'gold');tt.textContent=t;void tt.offsetWidth;tt.style.animation='none';void tt.offsetWidth;tt.style.animation='';}
    function count(to,ms){if(fast||to<=0){$('cAmt').textContent=money(to,true);return Promise.resolve();}var c2=0;return tween(ms,function(p){if(fast)p=1;$('cAmt').textContent=money(to*(1-Math.pow(1-p,2)),true);if(++c2%5===0)SFX.coin();});}
    function pause(ms){return fast?Promise.resolve():wait(ms);}
    (async function(){var lines=[];
      for(var i=0;i<o.rows.length;i++){var r=o.rows[i];setTier(r.label,r.cls);$('cAmt').textContent=money(0,true);SFX.chime(i+2);await count(r.amt,r.ms||1800);$('cAmt').textContent=money(r.amt,true);
        lines.push(r.label.charAt(0)+r.label.slice(1).toLowerCase()+' '+money(r.amt,true));$('cSub').innerHTML='<div class="trow">'+esc(lines.join(' \u00b7 '))+'</div>';await pause(1000);}
      setTier(o.tier||'TOTAL WIN',o.tcls||'gold');SFX.fanfare(o.big||1);coins(o.big>=2?8:4,'top');$('cAmt').textContent=money(0,true);await count(o.total,2200);$('cAmt').textContent=money(o.total,true);
      if(o.sub)$('cSub').innerHTML='<div class="trow">'+esc(lines.join(' \u00b7 '))+'</div><div>'+esc(o.sub)+'</div>';
      if(!ended)after(fast?600:(st.autoHeld>0?2000:3400),close);})();
  });
}
async function nugBonus(kind,g,b){
  var fz=kind==='frenzy',R=ENG.bonusRun(kind,g,{script:st.bscript||undefined});st.bscript=null;
  st.nb={kind:kind,meter:R.start.meter,mult:1,jpm:R.start.jpm.slice(),left:NG.spins,b:b};
  /* trigger highlight + intro */
  var trig=[];for(var c=0;c<NRE;c++)for(var r=0;r<4;r++){var v=g[c][r];if(fz?v===ENG.FP:(ENG.NUG.cnt[v]||v===ENG.S))trig.push([c,r]);}
  trig.forEach(function(p){var t=tiles[p[0]][p[1]];if(t){t.classList.add('nhot');var q=feltXY(t);spark(q.x,q.y,'#fde68a',16,1);}});SFX.scatterWin();
  var nsc=0;for(var c3=0;c3<NRE;c3++)for(var r3=0;r3<4;r3++)if(g[c3][r3]===ENG.S)nsc++;
  winMsg(fz?trig.length+' Frenzy Horseshoes! The Gold Frenzy is coming\u2026':nsc>=ENG.SCATN?nsc+' Respin Charges! The Nugget Respins are lit\u2026':'Dynamite! The Nugget Respins are lit\u2026');await wait(D(1300));
  trig.forEach(function(p){var t=tiles[p[0]][p[1]];if(t)t.classList.remove('nhot');});
  if(fz){SFX.whistle();await trainCross0();}
  await celebrate({cls:'fs',pre:fz?trig.length+' Frenzy Horseshoes':'Nugget Respins',tier:fz?'GOLD FRENZY':'NUGGET RESPINS',tcls:'gold',banner:FZFRAME.frenzyTitle?'__fztitle':'',
    sub:fz?'Four mine claims. Claim 1 opens with 3 nuggets; a steam locomotive opens the next claim. Every new nugget resets the 3 spins.':'Your nuggets lock in place and fill the prize meter. 3 respins, back to 3 every time something new lands.',
    btn:fz?'Start digging!':'Light the fuse!',autoStart:st.autoHeld>0?1800:4500,dur:600});
  await openBoard(kind,R.start);renderNB();meterShow();winMsg(fz?'Claim 1 is open. Here come the respins\u2026':'Your nuggets are locked in. Here come the respins\u2026');
  feedMine(fz?'the Gold Frenzy':'the Nugget Respins',50);
  await wait(D(1700));
  if(!fz)await blastIntro();
  for(var i=0;i<R.steps.length;i++)await playStep(R.steps[i]);
  /* pay */
  await wait(D(600));
  var meterAmt=r2(R.meter*b),rows=[{label:'PRIZE METER',amt:meterAmt}];
  if(R.mult>1)rows.push({label:'METER \u00d7'+R.mult,amt:r2(R.meter*R.mult*b)});
  if(R.fillX)rows.push({label:fz?R.full+' FULL CLAIM'+(R.full>1?'S':''):'MOTHER LODE',amt:r2(R.fillX*b),cls:'epic'});
  var jpTot=0;R.jp.forEach(function(t){var a=r2(pools[t]*b);pools[t]=JP.seed[t];jpTot=r2(jpTot+a);st.stats.jps++;rows.push({label:JP.label[t].toUpperCase()+' JACKPOT',amt:a,cls:t>=2?'epic':t===1?'mega':''});shareWin(a,'jp',t);});
  saveJP();renderJP();
  var total=r2(R.cashX*b+jpTot),capped=false;if(total>NG.cap*b){total=NG.cap*b;capped=true;}
  var x=total/b;
  await tally({pre:fz?'Gold Frenzy complete':'Nugget Respins complete',rows:rows,total:total,big:x>=100?2:x>=30?1:0,tier:tierOf(x)>=0?TIERS[tierOf(x)]:'TOTAL WIN',tcls:tierOf(x)>=1?TCLS[tierOf(x)]:'gold',
    sub:(capped?'Max bonus win reached: '+NG.cap.toLocaleString()+'\u00d7 your bet. ':'')+Math.round(x)+'\u00d7 your bet'+(fz?' \u00b7 '+R.open+' of 4 claims opened':'')+'.'});
  await zoomOut();closeBoard();$('fsbar').classList.add('hide');$('fsbar').classList.remove('nbar');st.nb=null;
  st.bank=r2(st.bank+total);st.extra=r2((st.extra||0)+total);st.stats.feats++;hud();
  feedMine((fz?'a Gold Frenzy':'Nugget Respins')+' for '+money(total),x+40);shareWin(total,'fs',fz?4:3);
  if(bots.length)botLine(pick(bots),'cheer',0.8,600,true);
  return total;
}
async function trainCross0(){ /* the Gold Frenzy intro: a train runs along the reels */
  var fl=$('flyers'),y=feltXY(reelsEl).y,W0=felt.clientWidth,tw=Math.round(clamp(CS*2.6,130,300)),t=document.createElement('div');t.className='train'+(FZART.loco?' art':'');t.style.setProperty('--tw',tw+'px');t.innerHTML=fzImg('loco')||LOCOSVG;t.style.top=(y-tw*0.5)+'px';fl.appendChild(t);SFX.chug(1.4);
  var x0=FZART.loco?W0+tw*0.2:-tw*1.2,x1=FZART.loco?-tw*1.2:W0+tw*0.2;
  await done(anim(t,[{transform:'translateX('+x0+'px)'},{transform:'translateX('+x1+'px)'}],{duration:D(opt.fx?1400:400),easing:'ease-in',fill:'both'}));t.remove();
}
/* ---------- autoplay ---------- */
function startAuto(n){if(st.busy||AWAY)return;closePop();st.auto=n;st.autoBank=st.bank;setButtons();spin();}
function stopAuto(why){if(st.auto>0||st.autoHeld>0){st.auto=0;st.autoHeld=0;setButtons();if(why)toastMsg(why);}}
/* a bonus (Nugget Respins, Gold Frenzy, collector minigame, Stampede free spins) during autoplay: autoplay PAUSES so the player plays the
   bonus by hand with its normal intro, then picks up again with the spins it had left (unless "On a bonus: Stop" is set,
   or the player pressed Stop while the bonus ran) */
async function bonusPause(fn){
  var held=st.auto;
  if(held>0){st.auto=0;st.autoHeld=held;setButtons();toastMsg(opt.stopFS?'Bonus! Autoplay stopped so you can play it.':'Bonus! Autoplay paused so you can play it.');}
  try{return await fn();}
  finally{if(held>0){var left=st.autoHeld;st.autoHeld=0;
      if(left>0&&!opt.stopFS){st.auto=left;toastMsg('Autoplay resumed: '+left+' spin'+(left===1?'':'s')+' left.');}
      setButtons();}}
}
function autoNext(tot,b){
  if(st.auto<=0)return;
  st.auto--;
  if(opt.stopBig&&tot/b>=15){st.auto=0;toastMsg('Autoplay stopped on a big win.');}
  else if(opt.loss&&st.autoBank-st.bank>=opt.loss*b-1e-9){st.auto=0;toastMsg('Autoplay stopped: loss limit of '+money(opt.loss*b)+' reached.');}
  else if(st.bank<1){st.auto=0;}
  setButtons();
  if(st.auto>0)after(D(380),function(){if(st.auto>0&&!st.busy&&!AWAY)spin();});
}
var pop=$('autopop');
function placeAutoPop(){if(!pop.classList.contains('on'))return;var a=feltXY($('autoBtn')),w=pop.offsetWidth,h=pop.offsetHeight;
  var x=clamp(a.x-w/2,8,felt.clientWidth-w-8),y=a.y-a.h/2-h-10;if(y<8)y=clamp(a.y+a.h/2+10,8,felt.clientHeight-h-8);pop.style.left=x+'px';pop.style.top=y+'px';}
function openPop(){pop.classList.add('on');placeAutoPop();}
function closePop(){pop.classList.remove('on');}
$('autoBtn').addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();SFX.click();if(st.auto>0||st.autoHeld>0){stopAuto('Autoplay stopped.');return;}if(pop.classList.contains('on'))closePop();else openPop();});
$('autoGo').addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();startAuto(opt.autoN);});
pop.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();});
document.addEventListener('click',function(){closePop();});

/* ---------- controls ---------- */
function pressSpin(){
  if(st.celeb){st.celeb.skip();return;}
  if(st.auto>0){stopAuto('Autoplay stopped.');return;}
  if(st.busy){st.hurry=true;return;}
  spin();
}
$('spinBtn').addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();pressSpin();});
$('betDn').addEventListener('click',function(){if(st.busy||st.auto)return;st.betI=Math.max(0,st.betI-1);SFX.click();savePrefs();renderBet();renderPaytable();});
$('betUp').addEventListener('click',function(){if(st.busy||st.auto)return;st.betI=Math.min(BETS.length-1,st.betI+1);SFX.click();savePrefs();renderBet();renderPaytable();});
$('maxBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){if(st.busy||st.auto)return;var j=BETS.length-1;while(j>0&&BETS[j]>st.bank)j--;st.betI=j;SFX.click();savePrefs();renderBet();renderPaytable();toastMsg('Max bet: '+money(bet())+'.');});
$('turboBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){var b=document.querySelector('#optTurbo button[data-v="'+(opt.turbo?'0':'1')+'"]');if(b)b.click();});
$('infoBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){openPtab();});$('infoBtn2').addEventListener('click',function(){openPtab();if(!root.classList.contains('fs'))felt.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});});
addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
  var tg=e.target;if(tg&&(tg.tagName==='INPUT'||tg.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||tg.isContentEditable))return;if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey)return;
  var k=e.key;
  if(k===' '||k==='Spacebar'){e.preventDefault();pressSpin();}
  else if(k==='Escape'){closePop();closePtab();}
  else if(k==='ArrowUp'){e.preventDefault();$('betUp').click();}
  else if(k==='ArrowDown'){e.preventDefault();$('betDn').click();}
  else if(k==='m'||k==='M')$('maxBtn').click();
  else if(k==='a'||k==='A'){if(st.auto>0||st.autoHeld>0)stopAuto('Autoplay stopped.');else if(!st.busy)startAuto(opt.autoN);}
  else if(k==='t'||k==='T')$('turboBtn').click();
  else if(k==='i'||k==='I'){if($('ptab').classList.contains('hide'))openPtab();else closePtab();}
});

/* ---------- broke ---------- */
function checkBroke(){if(!st.busy&&st.bank<1-1e-9){stopAuto();showBroke();}}
function showBroke(){$('ovText').textContent='The reels took the last of it. Biggest win this session: '+(st.big?money(st.big):'none yet')+'. Rebuy and ride again.';$('ov').classList.remove('hide');}
function rebuy(){st.bank=r2(st.bank+1000);st.sessBase=r2(st.sessBase+1000);$('ov').classList.add('hide');hud();renderBet();winMsg('Rebought $1,000. Saddle up.');}
function newSession(){if(st.busy)return;stopAuto();st.bank=START;st.sessBase=START;st.spins=0;st.big=0;st.stats={hits:0,wager:0,won:0,feats:0,fsSpins:0,jps:0,best:0,bestChain:0,lastFS:null};try{if(WAL)WAL.adopt();}catch(e){}
  $('ov').classList.add('hide');hud();renderBet();renderStats();winMsg('Fresh session. Good luck, partner.');}
$('rebuyBtn').addEventListener('click',rebuy);$('newBtn').addEventListener('click',newSession);$('newBtn2').addEventListener('click',newSession);

/* =====================================================================
   Paytable / rules overlay and the "This machine" panel
   ===================================================================== */
var MATH={rtp:94.7,hit:27.7,sd:9.2,stampede:443,stAvg:118,vol:'Medium-high',gold:25,lasso:50,bounty:245,coll:222,spins:'1',respin:77,frenzy:436};
function payCell(k){var s=CODE.indexOf(k),p=ENG.PAY[s],b=bet();
  return '<div class="pcell"><div class="sy s-'+k+'">'+symInner(k)+'</div><div class="pv"><i>'+SYMNAME[k]+'</i>5 of a kind <b>'+money(r2(p[2]*b),true)+'</b><br>4 of a kind <b>'+money(r2(p[1]*b),true)+'</b><br>3 of a kind <b>'+money(r2(p[0]*b),true)+'</b></div></div>';}
function nugCell(k,txt){return '<div class="pcell"><div class="sy s-'+k+'">'+symInner(k)+'</div><div class="pv"><i>'+SYMNAME[k]+'</i>'+txt+'</div></div>';}
function nugRules(b){var P=NG.pot;
  return '<h5>Gold nuggets</h5><div class="pgrid">'+
    nugCell('1','Counts as <b>1</b> nugget.')+nugCell('2','Counts as <b>2</b> nuggets.')+nugCell('3','Counts as <b>3</b> nuggets.')+
    nugCell('D','Counts as 1 nugget and blows open into a <b>bigger cash nugget</b> (1.5&times; to 7.5&times;) when the respins start.')+nugCell('X','Counts as 1 nugget and holds cash (1.5&times; to 18.75&times;). In a bonus it wakes the bandits.')+
    nugCell('F','The <b>Frenzy Horseshoe</b> (amber, marked FRENZY). <b>3 or more anywhere</b> start the <b>Gold Frenzy</b>. ')+'</div>'+
    '<div class="pgrid" style="margin-top:8px">'+['m','n','j','g'].map(function(k,t){return nugCell(k,'Counts as 1 nugget. <b>3 on screen</b> win the '+JP.label[t]+' Jackpot.');}).join('')+'</div>'+
    '<div class="pgrid" style="margin-top:8px">'+nugCell('T','Bonus only. Locks in place; when a <b>Strongbox</b> lands it collects the cash of the nuggets right next to it.')+nugCell('L','Gold Frenzy only. <b>Opens the next mine claim</b>, or once all 4 are open adds +1 to the meter multiplier (up to &times;'+NG.multMax+').')+'</div>'+
    '<p style="margin-top:8px">Nuggets don&rsquo;t pay on the ways; they are counted on the screen <b>after the cascades finish</b>. Count every nugget symbol: a Double counts 2, a Triple counts 3, Dynamite, a Strongbox and a jackpot nugget count 1. With 4 or 5 the machine tells you how many more you need for the Respins.</p>'+
    '<h5>Nugget Respins</h5><p><b>6 or more nuggets anywhere</b> (Dynamite, Strongboxes and jackpot nuggets count too), or <b>3 or more Respin Charges</b>, start the Nugget Respins (about 1 in '+MATH.respin+' spins in all). Every nugget locks in place and shows its cash value (0.38&times; to 18.75&times; the bet); Dynamite and Respin Charges blow open into cash nuggets. The <b>prize meter</b> starts with their total.</p>'+
    '<p>You get <b>3 respins</b>. Only the empty spaces spin. <b>Anything new that lands resets the count to 3</b>. New nuggets lock in and fly into the prize meter; jackpot nuggets fly into their jackpot&rsquo;s meter (the three lights on each coin). Each spin is settled in this order: land, lock, <b>Strongbox wakes every Bandit</b>, each Bandit collects the cash of the nuggets directly above, below, left and right of it, then the meters update and full jackpot meters are checked. <b>3 jackpot nuggets of one kind fill that meter</b> and win that jackpot. <b>Fill all 20 spaces</b> for the <b>Mother Lode: '+NG.lode+'&times; the bet</b>.</p>'+
    '<h5>Gold Frenzy</h5><p><b>3 or more Frenzy Horseshoes</b> start the Gold Frenzy (about 1 in '+MATH.frenzy+' spins): <b>four mine claims</b>, each its own 5&times;4 grid. Claim 1 opens with 3 nuggets; claims 2 to 4 start chained shut. All open claims share <b>one 3-spin counter</b>, reset by anything new landing on any claim. When a <b>steam locomotive</b> pulls in, the next claim opens and the train drops 3 nuggets on it. A locomotive after all four are open adds <b>+1 to the prize meter multiplier</b>. A completely filled claim pays <b>'+NG.claimFull+'&times; the bet</b>. Bandits, Strongboxes and jackpot meters work just like in the respins.</p>'+
    '<p><b>Which comes first:</b> if one spin starts both, the Gold Frenzy is played first and the Nugget Respins right after it. Neither bonus can start the other from inside. At the end the machine counts up the prize meter, then any jackpots, then the total. A nugget bonus pays at most <b>'+NG.cap.toLocaleString()+'&times; the bet</b>. The jackpots are the same progressive jackpots shown on the coins.</p>';}
function renderPaytable(){
  var b=bet(),el=$('ptab');
  el.innerHTML='<div class="ph"><b>Paytable &middot; <span>'+money(b)+' bet</span></b><button type="button" class="btn2" id="ptClose">Close</button></div>'+
    '<p>Wins pay <b>left to right on adjacent reels from reel 1</b>, 3, 4 or 5 of a kind, in any row: that is <b>1,024 ways</b>. Each value below is paid <b>per way</b>; two matching symbols on one reel double the ways.</p>'+
    '<h5>The Saddle Up crew</h5><div class="pgrid">'+['A','K','Q','J'].map(payCell).join('')+'</div>'+
    '<h5>Trade jewels</h5><div class="pgrid">'+['E','H','R','P'].map(payCell).join('')+'</div>'+
    '<h5>Specials</h5><div class="pgrid">'+
      '<div class="pcell"><div class="sy s-W">'+symInner('W')+'</div><div class="pv"><i>Wild</i>Lands on reels 2 to 5 and stands in for every paying symbol (not the horseshoes or the nugget family).</div></div>'+
      '<div class="pcell"><div class="sy s-S">'+symInner('S')+'</div><div class="pv"><i>Respin Charge</i>Purple, marked RESPINS, on reels 1 to 4. <b>3 or more anywhere start the Nugget Respins</b>: each one blows open into a cash nugget.</div></div></div>'+
    nugRules(b)+
    '<h5>Cascades &amp; multiplier</h5><p>Winning symbols burst, everything above falls and new symbols tumble in. Each cascade in a row moves the multiplier up the ladder: <b>1&times; &rarr; 2&times; &rarr; 3&times; &rarr; 5&times; &rarr; 10&times;</b>. It resets on the next paid spin.</p>'+
    '<h5>The Stampede</h5><p>The Stampede is started <b>only by the rider on the Stampede track</b> under the reels. When he crosses the finish line you get <b>'+MT.spins+' free spins at the same bet, on reels packed with Wilds, and every win pays &times;10</b> (the multiplier is locked at 10&times;). There are no retriggers. A Stampede pays at most '+ENG.MAXWIN.toLocaleString()+'&times; the bet. On average it comes about every '+MATH.stampede+' spins and pays about '+MATH.stAvg+'&times; the bet.</p>'+
    '<h5>Bounty Board</h5><p>The wanted poster names one of the face cards. <b>Every cascade that includes that character puts a bullet hole in the poster</b>; '+BT.need+' holes collect the bounty, paid on the spot: <b>'+BT.x.map(function(x){return x+'&times;';}).join(', ')+'</b> the bet for each poster in turn (then it starts over). Posters run Jack, Queen, King, Ace. Progress is saved on this device for each bet size. On average a bounty is collected about every '+MATH.bounty+' spins.</p>'+
    '<h5>Gold Rush</h5><p>On about <b>1 in '+MATH.gold+'</b> paid spins the prospector swings his pickaxe before the first win is counted and turns <b>one whole reel (2, 3, 4 or 5) into Wilds</b>.</p>'+
    '<h5>Lasso</h5><p>When a crew member sits on reels 1 and 4 and matches on reel 3 but reel 2 is the only gap, the <b>Lasso</b> may rope the reel-4 symbol and drag it into reel 2 to complete the win (60% of those chances, about 1 in '+MATH.lasso+' spins, once per spin).</p>'+
    '<h5>Stampede track</h5><p>Every <b>winning cascade</b> in the base game moves the rider one step along the track under the reels (a spin with 3 cascades moves him 3 steps). At <b>'+MT.len+'</b> steps he crosses the finish line and the <b>Stampede</b> starts; the track then resets to the start. The last 15% of the track glows, and the machine counts down the final steps. Wins during the Stampede itself don&rsquo;t move the rider. Progress is saved on this device for each bet size.</p>'+
    '<h5>Collector</h5><p>Three items sit up top: the <b>Sheriff&rsquo;s Badge</b>, the <b>Lantern</b> and the <b>Lucky Boot</b>. Each paid spin has a 1 in '+Math.round(1000/CL.p)+' chance per item of a token landing; it flies up into its item. <b>'+CL.need+' tokens fill it</b> (about every '+MATH.coll+' spins per item) and start its minigame, paid on your <b>average bet while collecting</b>:</p>'+
    '<p><b>Quick Draw</b> (Badge): 8 bottles pop up in 6 seconds; the prize starts at 80% of a hidden pot and each bottle hit adds 5%. <b>Mine Cart Run</b> (Lantern): pick left or right at each fork; every stretch holds gold (0.5&times; to 4&times;) until a cave-in (25% per stretch after the first; your choice never changes the odds). <b>Bronco Ride</b> (Boot): press in the green zone to ride each of 6 bucks; the prize starts at 70% of a hidden pot and each buck ridden adds 10%.</p>'+
    '<p>The hidden pot averages 4.5&times; the average bet, so a typical game is worth about 4.5&times;. Skill moves Quick Draw and Bronco by about &minus;20% to +30% at most; Autoplay plays them at an average level. Collector progress is saved on this device.</p>'+
    '<h5>Progressive jackpots</h5><p>Every paid spin adds 1% of the bet to the four jackpots (shown for your current bet, saved on this device). A <b>Stampede Line</b>, three Wilds across one row on reels 2&ndash;4, awards one: Mini (70%), Minor (22%), Major (7%) or Grand (1%). Seeds: Mini '+JP.seed[0]+'&times;, Minor '+JP.seed[1]+'&times;, Major '+JP.seed[2]+'&times;, Grand '+JP.seed[3]+'&times; the bet.</p>'+
    '<h5>Machine math</h5><div class="mstats"><div><span>RTP</span><b>'+MATH.rtp.toFixed(2)+'%</b></div><div><span>Hit frequency</span><b>'+MATH.hit+'%</b></div><div><span>Volatility</span><b>'+MATH.vol+'</b></div><div><span>Stampede</span><b>1 in ~'+MATH.stampede+'</b></div><div><span>Max win</span><b>'+ENG.MAXWIN.toLocaleString()+'&times;</b></div><div><span>Gold Rush</span><b>1 in '+MATH.gold+'</b></div><div><span>Bounty</span><b>~'+MATH.bounty+' spins</b></div><div><span>Each collector item</span><b>~'+MATH.coll+' spins</b></div><div><span>Nugget Respins</span><b>1 in ~'+MATH.respin+'</b></div><div><span>Gold Frenzy</span><b>1 in ~'+MATH.frenzy+'</b></div></div>'+
    '<p style="margin-top:10px">RTP includes the jackpot contribution and comes from a '+MATH.spins+'-million-spin simulation of this exact math: cascades, free spins, Gold Rush, Lasso, bounties, the Saddle Up meter, collector minigames (at their average value), the Prize Pot, Nugget Respins, the Gold Frenzy and jackpots. Every symbol comes from your browser\u2019s <b>crypto.getRandomValues</b> secure random generator. Play money only.</p>';
  $('ptClose').addEventListener('click',closePtab);
}
function openPtab(){closePop();renderPaytable();$('ptab').classList.remove('hide');$('ptab').scrollTop=0;}
function closePtab(){$('ptab').classList.add('hide');}
function renderStats(){
  var s=st.stats,rt=s.wager?(s.won/s.wager*100):0;
  $('slist').innerHTML='<div>Spins <b>'+st.spins+'</b></div><div>Winning spins <b>'+(st.spins?Math.round(s.hits/st.spins*100):0)+'%</b></div>'+
    '<div>Wagered <b>'+money(s.wager)+'</b></div><div>Paid out <b>'+money(s.won)+'</b></div><div>Session RTP <b>'+(s.wager?rt.toFixed(1)+'%':'\u2013')+'</b></div><div>Longest chain <b>'+s.bestChain+'</b></div>'+
    '<div>Stampedes <b>'+s.feats+'</b></div><div>Jackpots <b>'+s.jps+'</b></div>';
  $('mathNote').innerHTML='Theoretical RTP <b>'+MATH.rtp.toFixed(2)+'%</b>, hit frequency <b>'+MATH.hit+'%</b>, volatility <b>'+MATH.vol.toLowerCase()+'</b> (standard deviation '+MATH.sd+'&times; bet per spin). Stampede about 1 in '+MATH.stampede+' spins. Short sessions swing a lot; that is what volatility means.';
}

var bots=[],benched={},SEATN=3;
function buildSeats(){var sd=$('side');for(var i=0;i<SEATN;i++){var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='nb gone';d.innerHTML='<div class="talk"></div><div class="av"></div><div class="pi"><div class="nm"><span></span><em></em></div><div class="st"></div></div><div class="mini"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>';sd.appendChild(d);}}
function seatEl(i){return $('side').querySelectorAll('.nb')[i];}
function renderSeat(b){var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(!d)return;d.classList.remove('gone');d.querySelector('.av').style.background=b.col;d.querySelector('.av').textContent=b.name.charAt(0);
  d.querySelector('.nm span').textContent=b.name;d.querySelector('.nm em').textContent=money(b.bank);}
function seatStatus(b,t,cls){var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(!d)return;var s=d.querySelector('.st');s.textContent=t;s.className='st'+(cls?' '+cls:'');}
function botSay(b,t){if(!opt.talk||bots.indexOf(b)<0)return;var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(!d)return;var k=d.querySelector('.talk');k.textContent=t;k.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(b.tt);b.tt=setTimeout(function(){k.classList.remove('on');},3000);try{if(CH)CH.post(b.id,b.name,t,b.col);}catch(e){}}
function botBubble(b,t){if(!opt.talk)return;var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(!d)return;var k=d.querySelector('.talk');k.textContent=t;k.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(b.tt);b.tt=setTimeout(function(){k.classList.remove('on');},2600);}
function botLine(b,ev,prob,delay,chat){if(!opt.talk||Math.random()>(prob===undefined?1:prob))return;var a=(BL[ev]||{})[b.key];if(!a)return;var t=pick(a);after(delay||0,function(){if(bots.indexOf(b)<0)return;if(chat)botSay(b,t);else botBubble(b,t);});}
function sysChat(t){try{if(CH)CH.sys(t);}catch(e){}}
function newBot(slot){var seated={};bots.forEach(function(b){seated[b.name]=1;});var pool=POOL.filter(function(p){return !seated[p[0]];}),fresh=pool.filter(function(p){return !benched[p[0]];});
  var p=fresh.length?pick(fresh):pool.sort(function(a,b){return (benched[a[0]]||0)-(benched[b[0]]||0);})[0];
  var bank=p[2]==='whale'?6000+Math.round(Math.random()*9000):800+Math.round(Math.random()*3200);
  return {id:'bot:'+p[0].toLowerCase(),name:p[0],col:p[1],key:p[2],bank:bank,start:bank,slot:slot,bet:pick(BETSTY[p[2]]),tm:null,fs:null,tt:0,bt:{t:crnd(4),h:crnd(5),lv:crnd(4)}};}
function seatBot(slot,quiet){var b=newBot(slot);bots.push(b);renderSeat(b);seatStatus(b,'Sitting down\u2026');if(!quiet){sysChat(b.name+' sat down at the next machine');}botLine(b,'hello',quiet?0.4:0.85,700,!quiet);schedBot(b,1200+Math.random()*2500);renderWho();try{if(CH)CH.refresh();}catch(e){}return b;}
function unseat(b,why){if(b.tm)cancelT(b.tm);var i=bots.indexOf(b);if(i>=0)bots.splice(i,1);benched[b.name]=Date.now();var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(d)d.classList.add('gone');if(why)sysChat(b.name+' '+why);renderWho();try{if(CH)CH.refresh();}catch(e){}}
function setBots(n,quiet){n=clamp(n,0,SEATN);while(bots.length>n){var b=bots[bots.length-1];botLine(b,'broke',0.5,0,false);unseat(b,quiet?'':'cashed out');}
  for(var s=0;s<SEATN&&bots.length<n;s++)if(!bots.some(function(x){return x.slot===s;}))seatBot(s,quiet);}
function schedBot(b,ms){if(b.tm)cancelT(b.tm);b.tm=after(ms,function(){botSpin(b);});}
function botSpin(b){
  if(bots.indexOf(b)<0)return;
  if(Math.random()<0.12)b.bet=pick(BETSTY[b.key]);
  if(b.bank<b.bet){var lo=BETSTY[b.key].filter(function(x){return x<=b.bank;});if(lo.length)b.bet=lo[lo.length-1];else{botLine(b,'broke',1,0,true);seatStatus(b,'Tapped out');after(1800,function(){unseat(b,'is tapped out and heads for the cashier');after(4000+Math.random()*5000,function(){if(bots.length<opt.bots&&!bots.some(function(x){return x.slot===b.slot;}))seatBot(b.slot,false);});});return;}}
  b.bank=r2(b.bank-b.bet);var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(d)d.classList.add('spin');seatStatus(b,'Spinning \u00b7 '+money(b.bet));renderSeat(b);
  var r=ENG.spin({}),win=r2((r.lineWin+r.scatPay)*b.bet),jw=0,fsw=0,fsn=0,chain=r.steps.length,bw=0,bsym=-1;
  r.steps.forEach(function(s){if(s.wins.some(function(w){return w.sym===BT.order[b.bt.t];})){b.bt.h++;if(b.bt.h>=BT.need){bw+=BT.x[b.bt.lv]*b.bet;bsym=BT.order[b.bt.t];b.bt.h=0;b.bt.t=(b.bt.t+1)%4;b.bt.lv=(b.bt.lv+1)%BT.x.length;}}});
  win=r2(win+bw);
  if(r.jp)jw=r2(JP.seed[r.jp.tier]*b.bet*(1+Math.random()*0.6));
  var nb=r.nug,nbw=0,nbk='';if(nb){nb.jps.forEach(function(q){jw=r2(jw+JP.seed[q]*b.bet);});
    ['frenzy','respin'].forEach(function(kd){if(!nb[kd])return;var R=ENG.bonusRun(kd,r.final),x=R.cashX;R.jp.forEach(function(q){x+=JP.seed[q];});nbw=r2(nbw+Math.min(x,ENG.NUG.cap)*b.bet);nbk=kd;});win=r2(win+nbw);}
  if(r.fsAward){var left=r.fsAward,idx=0,given=left;while(left>0){left--;fsn++;var f=ENG.spin({fs:true,idx:idx});idx=f.idx;fsw+=(f.lineWin+f.scatPay)*b.bet;if(f.fsAward&&given<ENG.FSMAX){var add=Math.min(f.fsAward,ENG.FSMAX-given);left+=add;given+=add;}}fsw=r2(Math.min(fsw,ENG.MAXWIN*b.bet));}
  after(1100+Math.random()*700,function(){
    if(bots.indexOf(b)<0)return;if(d)d.classList.remove('spin');
    b.bank=r2(b.bank+win+jw);renderSeat(b);
    var x=win/b.bet;
    if(win>0){seatStatus(b,'+'+money(win,true)+(chain>1?' \u00b7 '+chain+' cascades':''),'g');flashSeat(b);}else seatStatus(b,'No win');
    if(bw){feedBot(b,'collected the bounty on '+SYMNAME[SK[bsym]]+' for '+money(bw)+'!');botLine(b,'jp',0.7,400,true);}
    if(nbk==='frenzy'){feedBot(b,'hit the Gold Frenzy for '+money(nbw)+'!');botLine(b,'fs',1,300,true);}else if(nbk==='respin'){feedBot(b,'blew open the Nugget Respins for '+money(nbw)+'!');botLine(b,'win',0.8,300,true);}
    if(jw){feedBot(b,'hit the '+JP.label[r.jp.tier]+' Jackpot for '+money(jw)+'!');botLine(b,'jp',1,200,true);}
    if(chain>=4&&win>0){feedBot(b,'hit a '+chain+'-cascade chain for '+money(win)+'!');botLine(b,'chain',0.6,300,false);}
    else if(x>=15){feedBot(b,'landed a '+TIERS[tierOf(x)].toLowerCase()+' of '+money(win)+'!');botLine(b,'win',0.8,300,x>=40);}
    else if(win>0&&x>=4)botLine(b,'win',0.15,300,false);
    if(fsn){b.fs={n:fsn,win:fsw};seatStatus(b,'STAMPEDE! '+r.fsAward+' free spins','f');feedBot(b,'triggered the Stampede: '+r.fsAward+' free spins!');botLine(b,'fs',1,500,true);
      var meBot=bots.filter(function(o){return o!==b;});if(meBot.length)botLine(pick(meBot),'feat',0.5,2600,false);
      after(4500+fsn*500,function(){if(bots.indexOf(b)<0)return;b.bank=r2(b.bank+fsw);renderSeat(b);seatStatus(b,'Stampede paid '+money(fsw,true),'g');flashSeat(b);
        feedBot(b,'rode a '+fsn+'-spin Stampede for '+money(fsw)+'!');if(fsw/b.bet>=15)botLine(b,'fsend',1,200,true);b.fs=null;schedBot(b,2500+Math.random()*3000);});
      return;}
    if(Math.random()<0.025)botLine(b,'idle',1,800,true);
    schedBot(b,2400+Math.random()*3600);
  });
}
function flashSeat(b){var d=seatEl(b.slot);if(!d)return;d.classList.add('won');setTimeout(function(){d.classList.remove('won');},1400);}
function reactToMe(x,feature){if(!bots.length||!opt.talk)return;if(x>=15||feature){var b=pick(bots);botLine(b,'cheer',x>=40?1:0.75,900,x>=40||feature);}}
function renderWho(){var w=$('who');w.innerHTML=bots.length?bots.map(function(b){return '<div><i style="background:'+b.col+'"></i><b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> '+money(b.bank)+'</div>';}).join(''):'<div>No neighbours. Just you and the machine.</div>';}

/* ---------- win feed ticker + panel log ---------- */
var feedQ=[],feedBusy=false,lastFeedAt=0;
function feedPush(html){feedQ.push(html);if(feedQ.length>6)feedQ.shift();if(!feedBusy)feedNext();}
function feedNext(){var h=feedQ.shift();if(!h){feedBusy=false;return;}feedBusy=true;var tr=$('ftrack'),old=tr.querySelector('.fi:not(.out)');if(old){old.classList.add('out');setTimeout(function(){if(old.parentNode)old.parentNode.removeChild(old);},450);}
  var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='fi';d.innerHTML=h;tr.appendChild(d);setTimeout(feedNext,feedQ.length?2600:600);}
function logLine(html,me){var ul=$('log'),li=document.createElement('li');if(me)li.className='me';var dt=new Date();li.innerHTML=html+'<small>'+(dt.getHours()%12||12)+':'+('0'+dt.getMinutes()).slice(-2)+'</small>';ul.insertBefore(li,ul.firstChild);while(ul.children.length>40)ul.removeChild(ul.lastChild);}
function feedBot(b,t){var h='<i style="color:'+b.col+'">'+esc(b.name)+'</i> '+esc(t).replace(/(\$[\d,\.]+)/g,'<b>$1</b>');feedPush(h);logLine(h);}
function feedMine(t,x){if(x<15)return;var h='<i style="color:#34d399">You</i> hit '+esc(t).replace(/(\$[\d,\.]+)/g,'<b>$1</b>')+'!';feedPush(h);logLine(h,true);}
function logMine(tot,b,sw,fw,jt){if(tot<b*15&&!fw&&jt<0){var h='<i style="color:#34d399">You</i> won <b>'+money(tot)+'</b> ('+Math.round(tot/b)+'\u00d7)';logLine(h,true);}}
function feedSeed(){feedPush('Welcome to <b>Saddle Up Stampede</b>. Win cascades to move the rider down the Stampede track.');}

var L=null,CH=null,liveOn=true;
try{L=Live.create({game:'slots',maxSeats:5});
  CH=Live.chat(L,felt,{local:function(){return bots.length>0;},localLabel:'Slot floor',myName:function(){return 'You';},
    onMessage:function(m){if(!opt.talk||!bots.length)return;
      Live.banter(m.text,bots.map(function(b){return {id:b.id,name:b.name,key:b.key,col:b.col};}),{game:'slots',
        advice:function(){return pick(['Pick a bet you can ride for a few hundred spins. The rider reaches the Stampede about every 443 spins, the Nugget Respins come about every 77.','Every spin is independent. The machine has no memory, hot or cold.',
          'Turbo just speeds up the show. Same math, same 96%.','Set a loss limit on autoplay and stick to it, partner.']);},
        mood:function(b){var o=bots.filter(function(x){return x.id===b.id;})[0];if(!o)return 'even';return o.bank>o.start*1.1?'up':o.bank<o.start*0.9?'down':'even';},
        say:function(b,txt,delay){var o=bots.filter(function(x){return x.id===b.id;})[0];if(o)after(delay||0,function(){botSay(o,txt);});}});}});
  L.on('msg:win',function(p,from){if(!p||typeof p!=='object')return;var amt=+p.amt,kind=String(p.kind||'');if(!isFinite(amt)||amt<=0||amt>1e8)return;
    var nm=String(p.name||'Guest').slice(0,18),x=+p.x||0,txt=kind==='bounty'?'collected the bounty on '+SYMNAME[SK[BT.order[clamp(x|0,0,3)]]]+' for '+money(amt)+'!':kind==='jp'?'hit the '+(JP.label[clamp(x|0,0,3)])+' Jackpot for '+money(amt)+'!':kind==='fs'?'rode a '+clamp(x|0,1,60)+'-spin Stampede for '+money(amt)+'!':kind==='chain'?'hit a '+clamp(x|0,1,40)+'-cascade chain for '+money(amt)+'!':'landed a big win of '+money(amt)+'!';
    var h='<i style="color:hsl('+liveHue(from||'')+',75%,70%)">'+esc(nm)+'</i> <small style="color:#64748b">online</small> '+esc(txt).replace(/(\$[\d,\.]+)/g,'<b>$1</b>');feedPush(h);logLine(h);});
  L.on('status',liveUI);L.on('roster',liveUI);
}catch(e){CH=null;}
function liveHue(id){var h=0;for(var i=0;i<id.length;i++)h=(h*31+id.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;return h%360;}
function shareWin(amt,kind,x){try{if(L&&liveOn&&L.status==='live'&&L.roster().length>=2)L.send('win',{name:L.me.name,amt:r2(amt),kind:kind,x:x||0});}catch(e){}}
function liveUI(){if(!L)return;var s=L.status,avail=L.available(),dot=$('liveDot'),txt=$('liveTxt');dot.className='ldot '+(!avail||!liveOn?'off':s);
  var r=L.roster();txt.textContent=!avail?'Offline (online play is not set up)':!liveOn?'Off: online players hidden':s==='live'?(r.length>1?r.length+' players on the slot floor':'Live. Nobody else is spinning right now.'):s==='connecting'?'Connecting\u2026':s==='error'?'Could not connect.':'Offline';
  $('livePeople').innerHTML=r.length>1?r.map(function(p){return '<span class="lp'+(p.isMe?' me':'')+'">'+esc(p.name)+(p.isMe?' <i>you</i>':'')+'</span>';}).join(''):'';
  $('feedL').textContent=s==='live'&&r.length>1&&liveOn?'Live \u00b7 '+r.length+' online':'Win feed';
  if(document.activeElement!==$('liveName'))$('liveName').value=L.me.name;try{if(CH)CH.refresh();}catch(e){}}
$('liveName').addEventListener('change',function(){if(L){L.setName($('liveName').value);liveUI();}});

  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#'+id+' button'),function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});fn(b.getAttribute('data-v'));});});}

  else{lastRT=0;try{if(ac&&unlocked&&ac.state==='suspended')ac.resume();}catch(_){}anims(function(a){try{if(a.playState==='paused')a.play();}catch(_){}});kick();}});

/* jackpot coins explain themselves: hover (desktop) or tap (phone) */
(function(){
  var tip=null;function hide(){if(tip){tip.remove();tip=null;}}
  function show(el,i){hide();var tot=JP.w.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0),pc=JP.w.map(function(x){return Math.round(x/tot*100);});
    var txt=i<0?'':'<b>'+JP.label[i]+' Jackpot: '+$('jp'+i).textContent+'</b><br>';
    tip=document.createElement('div');tip.className='jptip';
    tip.innerHTML=txt+'Land <b>3 Wilds side by side in one row on reels 2, 3 and 4</b> (Wilds never land on reel 1). The jackpot lights then run across the coins and stop on one: '+
      JP.label.map(function(l,k){return (k===i?'<b>':'')+l+' '+pc[k]+'%'+(k===i?'</b>':'');}).join(' &middot; ')+'.<br><b>Jackpot nuggets</b> win them too: 3 of one kind on the screen, or 3 filling its meter during the Nugget Respins or the Gold Frenzy.<br>Every bet grows all four, and a bigger bet pays a bigger jackpot.';
    felt.appendChild(tip);var r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),f=felt.getBoundingClientRect(),w=tip.offsetWidth;
    tip.style.left=Math.max(6,Math.min(f.width-w-6,r.left-f.left+r.width/2-w/2))+'px';tip.style.top=(r.bottom-f.top+8)+'px';}
  [0,1,2,3].forEach(function(i){var el=$('jpw'+i);if(!el)return;el.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){show(el,i);});el.addEventListener('mouseleave',hide);
    el.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();if(tip)hide();else show(el,i);});});
  var h=$('jph');if(h){h.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){show(h,-1);});h.addEventListener('mouseleave',hide);h.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();if(tip)hide();else show(h,-1);});}
  document.addEventListener('click',hide);
})();
window.__slots_dbg={
  /* next spin starts from this grid: 4 strings, one per reel, top to bottom, e.g. ['AAKQ','AJQE','WAPP','KAHR'].
     opts: {queue:[4 strings fed in from above on cascades], tier:0..3 for a Stampede Line, fs:true to apply to the next free spin} */
  force:function(grid,o){o=o||{};st.force={grid:grid,queue:o.queue,tier:o.tier,gold:o.gold,lasso:o.lasso,tokens:o.tokens,bscript:o.bscript,fs:o.fs===undefined?'any':o.fs};},
  /* feature hooks: next spin options without a grid, e.g. feature({gold:3}), feature({tokens:[0]}), feature({lasso:true}) */
  feature:function(o){st.force=Object.assign({fs:false},o||{});},
  /* nugget family. force(grid,{bscript:[[{cl,c,r,k,v},...],...]}) also scripts the bonus that grid triggers (k = symbol index: 11-13 nuggets,
     15 strongbox, 17-20 Mini..Grand nuggets, 21 bandit, 22 locomotive; an empty list = nothing lands). sym('X') gives an index. */
  sym:function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);},
  nug:function(){return {nb:st.nb?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st.nb)):null,board:!!NB,claims:NB?NB.claims.map(function(o){return {open:o.el.classList.contains('open'),full:o.el.classList.contains('full'),filled:o.cells.filter(Boolean).length};}):null,
    rect:(function(){var r=reelsEl.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:r.x,y:r.y,w:r.width,h:r.height};})()};},
  bonus:function(kind,grid,script){if(st.busy)return Promise.resolve(0);st.busy=true;setButtons();st.bscript=script||null;
    var g=(grid||['1122','2D11','1111','XFFF']).map(function(col){return String(col).split('').map(function(ch){return CODE.indexOf(ch);});});
    return nugBonus(kind,g,bet()).then(function(v){st.busy=false;setButtons();hud();return v;});},
  bounty:function(h){var o=btState();o.h=clamp(h|0,0,BT.need-1);saveProg();renderBounty();return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));},
  meter:function(v){PROG.m[String(st.betI)]=clamp(v|0,0,MT.len-1);saveProg();renderMeter();},
  collector:function(i,n){PROG.c[i].n=clamp(n|0,0,CL.need-1);saveProg();renderColl();},
  minigame:function(i){if(st.busy)return Promise.resolve(0);st.busy=true;setButtons();PROG.c[i].n=CL.need;renderColl();return minigame(i,bet()).then(function(v){st.busy=false;setButtons();return v;});},
  progress:function(){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(PROG));},
  art:function(){return {loaded:Object.keys(ARTOK).length,keys:Object.keys(ARTOK),cab:cabMode};},
  state:function(){return {bank:st.bank,bet:bet(),busy:st.busy,spins:st.spins,big:st.big,auto:st.auto,autoHeld:st.autoHeld,away:AWAY,fs:st.fs?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st.fs)):null,lastWin:st.lastWin,
    pools:pools.slice(),jackpots:pools.map(function(m){return r2(m*bet());}),grid:gridLetters(),celeb:!!st.celeb,layout:LAY,cs:CS,timers:timers.length,vt:vt,
    bots:bots.map(function(b){return {name:b.name,bank:b.bank,bet:b.bet,key:b.key,slot:b.slot};}),stats:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st.stats)),ladder:ladEls.findIndex(function(e){return e.classList.contains('on');}),
    win:$('winV').textContent,nb:st.nb?{kind:st.nb.kind,left:st.nb.left,meter:st.nb.meter,mult:st.nb.mult,jpm:st.nb.jpm.slice()}:null,feed:($('ftrack').textContent||''),bountyState:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(btState())),meter:meterVal(),collector:PROG.c.map(function(c){return c.n;}),mg:!mgEl.classList.contains('hide')};},
  spin:function(){return spin();},
  /* n instant spins through the real engine (no animation), bankroll applied */
  fast:function(n){var w=0,ret=0,hits=0,fsn=0;for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var b=bet();if(st.bank<b)break;st.bank=r2(st.bank-b);w+=b;st.spins++;for(var k=0;k<4;k++)pools[k]+=JP.inc[k];
      var r=ENG.spin({}),t=(r.lineWin+r.scatPay)*b;if(r.jp){t+=pools[r.jp.tier]*b;pools[r.jp.tier]=JP.seed[r.jp.tier];}
      if(r.fsAward){fsn++;var left=r.fsAward,idx=0,given=left;while(left>0){left--;var f=ENG.spin({fs:true,idx:idx});idx=f.idx;t+=(f.lineWin+f.scatPay)*b;if(f.jp){t+=pools[f.jp.tier]*b;pools[f.jp.tier]=JP.seed[f.jp.tier];}if(f.fsAward&&given<ENG.FSMAX){var a=Math.min(f.fsAward,ENG.FSMAX-given);left+=a;given+=a;}}}
      if(r.nug){var ng=r.nug;ng.jps.forEach(function(q){t+=pools[q]*b;pools[q]=JP.seed[q];});
        ['frenzy','respin'].forEach(function(kd){if(!ng[kd])return;fsn++;var R=ENG.bonusRun(kd,r.final),bt=R.cashX*b;R.jp.forEach(function(q){bt+=pools[q]*b;pools[q]=JP.seed[q];});t+=Math.min(bt,ENG.NUG.cap*b);});}
      t=r2(t);st.bank=r2(st.bank+t);ret+=t;if(t>0)hits++;if(t>st.big)st.big=t;}
    saveJP();renderJP();hud();return {spins:n,wager:w,ret:r2(ret),rtp:w?ret/w:0,hit:n?hits/n:0,features:fsn,bank:st.bank};},
  setBank:function(v){st.bank=r2(v);hud();setButtons();if(v>=1)$('ov').classList.add('hide');},
  setBet:function(v){var i=BETS.indexOf(v);if(i>=0){st.betI=i;renderBet();}},
  speed:function(x){DBGF=1/x;},
  away:function(on){window.postMessage({arcade:'visible',on:!on},'*');},
  skip:function(){if(st.celeb)st.celeb.skip();},
  auto:function(n){startAuto(n);},
  celebrate:function(x){return bigWin(x,x*bet());},
  gallop:function(){return gallop(7);},
  engine:ENG,
  layout:function(){layout();return {lay:LAY,cs:CS,cabH:cab.clientHeight,cabSH:cab.scrollHeight,cabW:cab.clientWidth,cabSW:cab.scrollWidth};},
  bots:function(n){setBots(n);},
  botSpin:function(i){if(bots[i])botSpin(bots[i]);}
};
})();
<\u002fscript>
<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
<\u002fscript>
</body>
</html>
